/* MarginPad ops v2 client (2026-09-03). Real file (named .js.txt so wrangler ships it as TEXT, not code; node --check still parses it), served at /api/stats/asset/ops.js behind the admin cookie.
   Hash router (#section/view), sidebar built from NAV, every view renders from JSON endpoints only (26 views native since
   2026-09-03; the old server-rendered dashboard is reachable only as /api/stats?legacy=1 for the JSON feed and emergencies).
   No frameworks. No emojis. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  var esc = function (s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[<>&"]/g, function (m) { return { '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '&': '&amp;', '"': '&quot;' }[m]; }); };
  var N = function (x) { return (+x || 0).toLocaleString('en-US'); };
  var USD = function (v, d) { return '$' + (+v || 0).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: d == null ? 2 : d, maximumFractionDigits: d == null ? 2 : d }); };
  var ago = function (t) { if (!t) return '—'; var s = Math.round((Date.now() - t) / 1000); if (s < 0) s = 0; return s < 60 ? s + 's' : s < 3600 ? Math.floor(s / 60) + 'm' : s < 86400 ? Math.floor(s / 3600) + 'h' : Math.floor(s / 86400) + 'd'; };
  // UTC by default (crons, seasons and every server stamp are UTC); the top-bar toggle switches every rendered time to the owner's local zone
  var TZ = 'utc'; try { TZ = localStorage.getItem('ops_tz') === 'local' ? 'local' : 'utc'; } catch (e) {}
  var p2 = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; };
  var dt = function (t) { try { var d = new Date(t); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—'; if (TZ === 'local') return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p2(d.getDate()) + ' ' + p2(d.getHours()) + ':' + p2(d.getMinutes()); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' '); } catch (e) { return '—'; } };
  var dtFull = function (t) { try { var d = new Date(t); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; return TZ === 'local' ? dt(t) + ':' + p2(d.getSeconds()) + ' local' : d.toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace('T', ' ') + ' UTC'; } catch (e) { return ''; } };
  var agoT = function (t) { return t ? '<span title="' + esc(dtFull(t)) + '">' + ago(t) + '</span>' : '—'; }; // relative time that reveals the exact moment on hover
  // tiles show 10k / 1.2M, never seven digits (UI rule: short numbers, one row); the exact value stays in the tooltip
  var compact = function (v) { var s = String(v); var m = s.match(/^(\$?)(\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+)(\.\d+)?$/); if (!m) return null; var n = parseFloat(m[2].replace(/,/g, '')); if (n < 100000) return null; var out = n >= 1e6 ? (n / 1e6).toFixed(n < 1e7 ? 1 : 0).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M' : Math.round(n / 1e3) + 'k'; return { txt: m[1] + out, full: s }; };
  var fdur = function (ms) { if (ms == null || !isFinite(ms) || ms < 0) return '—'; var m = Math.round(ms / 60000); if (m < 60) return m + 'm'; var h = Math.floor(m / 60); if (h < 48) return h + 'h ' + (m % 60) + 'm'; return Math.floor(h / 24) + 'd ' + (h % 24) + 'h'; };
  var median = function (arr) { var a = arr.filter(function (x) { return x != null && isFinite(x); }).sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; }); if (!a.length) return null; var i = Math.floor(a.length / 2); return a.length % 2 ? a[i] : (a[i - 1] + a[i]) / 2; };
  // Country flag as an IMAGE: emoji flags (regional-indicator pairs) render as bare letters on Windows, so the legacy
  // dashboard never showed a single flag there. flagcdn serves 20x15 PNGs; the code stays next to it as text.
  var flag = function (c) { c = String(c || '').toUpperCase(); return /^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(c) ? '<span class="cc"><img class="fl" src="https://flagcdn.com/20x15/' + c.toLowerCase() + '.png" width="20" height="15" alt="" loading="lazy" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\'">' + c + '</span>' : ''; };
  function J(u, o) { return fetch(u, o).then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (j) { if (!r.ok && j && !j.error) j.error = 'http_' + r.status; return j; }); }); }
  function post(u, body) { return J(u, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body || {}) }); }
  var toastT = null; function toast(msg) { var t = $('toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.hidden = false; clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(function () { t.hidden = true; }, 3500); }
  // the stamp is a live "updated 12s ago" counter; amber once a view has not repainted for 3 minutes (a dead timer or a failing endpoint)
  var stampTs = 0; function stamp() { stampTs = Date.now(); tickStamp(); }
  function tickStamp() { var e = $('stamp'); if (!stampTs) { e.textContent = ''; return; } var s = Math.round((Date.now() - stampTs) / 1000); e.textContent = 'updated ' + (s < 5 ? 'just now' : ago(stampTs) + ' ago'); e.classList.toggle('old', s > 180); }
  setInterval(tickStamp, 1000);
  function failHtml(msg) { return '<div class="empty err">' + esc(msg || 'could not load') + ' <button class="btn sm" data-retry style="margin-left:8px">Retry</button></div>'; }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-retry]'); if (b) { b.disabled = true; show(); } });
  function store(k, v) { try { if (v === undefined) return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ops_' + k) || 'null'); localStorage.setItem('ops_' + k, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  // per-view filter memory: a segment, sort or tab you picked is still picked when you come back (this browser)
  function recall(key, st, fields) { var m = store('f:' + key); if (!m) return; fields.forEach(function (f) { if (m[f] !== undefined) st[f] = m[f]; }); }
  function remember(key, st, fields) { var m = {}; fields.forEach(function (f) { m[f] = st[f]; }); store('f:' + key, m); }
  // skeleton while a view loads: the shape of what is coming, not a word
  var SKEL = '<div class="grid g4">' + '<div class="tile sk"><div class="skl w40"></div><div class="skl w70 s"></div></div>'.repeat(4) + '</div><div class="card sk"><div class="skl w30 s"></div><div class="skl w90"></div><div class="skl w80"></div><div class="skl w60"></div></div><div class="card sk"><div class="skl w30 s"></div><div class="skl w70"></div><div class="skl w90"></div></div>';
  // every table sorts by a click on its header (numbers as numbers, text as text; second click flips), no per-view wiring
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var th = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.tbl th'); if (!th || e.target.closest('a,button,input')) return; var tr = th.parentNode, tbody = th.closest('table').tBodies[0]; if (!tbody) return; var idx = Array.prototype.indexOf.call(tr.children, th); var dir = th.getAttribute('data-dir') === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'; Array.prototype.forEach.call(tr.children, function (x) { x.removeAttribute('data-dir'); }); th.setAttribute('data-dir', dir);
    var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(tbody.rows).filter(function (r) { return r.children.length > idx; }); var num = function (s) { s = String(s).replace(/[$,%\s]/g, '').replace(/^\+/, ''); var m = s.match(/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?([kM])?$/); if (!m) return null; var v = parseFloat(s); if (m[2] === 'k') v *= 1e3; if (m[2] === 'M') v *= 1e6; return v; };
    rows.sort(function (a, b) { var ta = a.children[idx].innerText.trim(), tb = b.children[idx].innerText.trim(); var na = num(ta), nb = num(tb); var c = (na != null && nb != null) ? na - nb : ta.localeCompare(tb, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' }); return dir === 'asc' ? c : -c; }); rows.forEach(function (r) { tbody.appendChild(r); }); });
  // CSV of any table: the button sits in the card header, the file carries the view and the date
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-csv]'); if (!b) return; var t = b.closest('.card, .fold'); var tb = t && t.querySelector('table.tbl'); if (!tb) return; var q = function (s) { s = String(s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s; }; var lines = Array.prototype.map.call(tb.rows, function (r) { return Array.prototype.map.call(r.children, function (c) { return q(c.innerText); }).join(','); }); var blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv' }); var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = 'ops-' + (route().key.replace('/', '-')) + '-' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) + '.csv'; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 1000); toast('csv: ' + (tb.rows.length - 1) + ' rows'); });

  // ---------- information architecture ----------
  // 8 sections instead of 24 flat tabs. Every view is native since 2026-09-03 evening (the old server-rendered dashboard is no longer embedded).
  var NAV = [
    { id: 'today', title: 'Today', views: [{ id: 'overview', title: 'Overview' }] },
    { id: 'inbox', title: 'Inbox', views: [{ id: 'support', title: 'Support' }, { id: 'chat', title: 'Trader chat' }, { id: 'mail', title: 'Email' }, { id: 'tgbot', title: 'Telegram bot' }] },
    { id: 'money', title: 'Money', views: [{ id: 'withdrawals', title: 'Withdrawals' }, { id: 'faucet', title: 'Faucet & rewards' }, { id: 'premium', title: 'Premium desk' }, { id: 'revenue', title: 'Revenue' }, { id: 'affiliate', title: 'Affiliate' }, { id: 'shop', title: 'Shop' }] },
    { id: 'people', title: 'People', views: [{ id: 'users', title: 'Users' }, { id: 'security', title: 'Multi-account' }, { id: 'retention', title: 'Retention' }, { id: 'journeys', title: 'Journey map' }, { id: 'activity', title: 'Activity' }] },
    { id: 'trading', title: 'Trading', views: [{ id: 'live', title: 'Live trades' }, { id: 'orders', title: 'Limit orders' }, { id: 'botapi', title: 'Bot API' }, { id: 'spot', title: 'Demo Spot' }] },
    { id: 'growth', title: 'Growth', views: [{ id: 'seo', title: 'Traffic & SEO' }, { id: 'funnel', title: 'Funnel' }, { id: 'guests', title: 'Guests' }, { id: 'community', title: 'Community' }] },
    { id: 'health', title: 'Health', views: [{ id: 'overview', title: 'Alerts & health' }, { id: 'perf', title: 'Performance' }] },
    { id: 'settings', title: 'Settings', views: [{ id: 'switches', title: 'Switches' }, { id: 'rewards', title: 'Reward config' }, { id: 'codes', title: 'Codes' }] }
  ];
  var VIEWS = {}; // 'section/view' -> { render(el), refresh() }
  var counts = {}; // sidebar counters from the attention list
  var cur = null, timers = [];

  function route() { var h = (location.hash || '').replace(/^#\/?/, ''); var p = h.split('/'); var sec = NAV.filter(function (s) { return s.id === p[0]; })[0] || NAV[0]; var v = sec.views.filter(function (x) { return x.id === p[1]; })[0] || sec.views[0]; return { sec: sec, view: v, key: sec.id + '/' + v.id, arg: p.slice(2).join('/') }; }
  function renderNav() { var r = route(); $('nav').innerHTML = NAV.map(function (s) { return '<div class="sec">' + esc(s.title) + '</div>' + s.views.map(function (v) { var k = s.id + '/' + v.id; return '<a href="#' + k + '" class="' + (k === r.key ? 'on' : '') + '">' + esc(v.title) + (counts[k] ? '<span class="cnt">' + counts[k] + '</span>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join(''); }).join(''); }
  function clearTimers() { timers.forEach(clearInterval); timers = []; }
  function every(ms, fn) { timers.push(setInterval(function () { if (!document.hidden) fn(); }, ms)); }
  function show() {
    var r = route(); cur = r; clearTimers(); renderNav(); $('side').classList.remove('open'); stampTs = 0; tickStamp();
    $('crumb').innerHTML = esc(r.sec.title) + ' <small>' + esc(r.view.title) + '</small>';
    baseTitle = r.view.title + ' · ' + r.sec.title + ' · MarginPad ops'; setTitle();
    document.querySelectorAll('#tabbar a').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('on', a.getAttribute('data-sec') === r.sec.id); });
    var rec = (store('recent') || []).filter(function (k) { return k !== r.key; }); rec.unshift(r.key); store('recent', rec.slice(0, 6));
    // a FRESH #view node per route: a fetch the previous view started (Live trades fires ~25 price lookups) resolves after the
    // route change and paints into the element it holds; that element is now detached, so the new view is never overwritten
    var old = $('view'), el = old.cloneNode(false); old.parentNode.replaceChild(el, old); el.innerHTML = SKEL; window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    paintAttn(); closeModal();
    var v = VIEWS[r.key];
    if (v) v.render(el, r.arg); else el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No such view.</div>';
    try { if (navigator.sendBeacon) navigator.sendBeacon('/api/admin/usage', JSON.stringify({ tab: 'v2:' + r.key })); } catch (e) {}
  }
  var baseTitle = 'MarginPad ops', redN = 0; function setTitle() { document.title = (redN ? '(' + redN + ') ' : '') + baseTitle; } // the tab itself shows how many red items wait
  function viewKeys() { var o = []; NAV.forEach(function (s) { s.views.forEach(function (v) { o.push(s.id + '/' + v.id); }); }); return o; }
  function typing() { var a = document.activeElement; return !!(a && (a.tagName === 'INPUT' || a.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || a.tagName === 'SELECT' || a.isContentEditable)); }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', show);
  $('menuBtn').addEventListener('click', function () { $('side').classList.add('open'); });
  $('tabMore').addEventListener('click', function () { $('side').classList.add('open'); });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey || typing()) return; var help = $('help');
    if (e.key === '?') { e.preventDefault(); help.hidden = !help.hidden; return; }
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { if (!help.hidden) help.hidden = true; return; }
    if (!pal.hidden || !help.hidden || MODAL) return;
    if (e.key === '/') { e.preventDefault(); palOpen(); }
    else if (e.key === 'r') { e.preventDefault(); show(); loadTop(); toast('refreshed'); }
    else if (e.key === '[' || e.key === ']') { var ks = viewKeys(), i = ks.indexOf(route().key); var n = ks[(i + (e.key === ']' ? 1 : -1) + ks.length) % ks.length]; location.hash = n; }
    else if (/^[1-8]$/.test(e.key)) { var sec = NAV[+e.key - 1]; if (sec) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = sec.id + '/' + sec.views[0].id; } } // 1..8 = the eight sections in sidebar order
    else if (e.key === 'u') { e.preventDefault(); setTz(TZ === 'utc' ? 'local' : 'utc'); }
  });
  function setTz(z, quiet) { TZ = z; try { localStorage.setItem('ops_tz', z); } catch (e) {} var b = $('tzBtn'); if (b) { b.textContent = z === 'utc' ? 'UTC' : 'local'; b.title = 'times are shown in ' + (z === 'utc' ? 'UTC (click for your local zone)' : 'your local zone (click for UTC)') + ' · key u'; } if (!quiet) { show(); toast('times in ' + (z === 'utc' ? 'UTC' : 'your local zone')); } tickClock(); }
  $('tzBtn').addEventListener('click', function () { setTz(TZ === 'utc' ? 'local' : 'utc'); });
  // top-bar clock: the hour the crons, seasons and server stamps live in, plus the season day
  var seasonEnd = 0, seasonStart = 0;
  function tickClock() { var e = $('clock'); if (!e) return; var d = new Date(); var t = TZ === 'local' ? p2(d.getHours()) + ':' + p2(d.getMinutes()) + ' local' : p2(d.getUTCHours()) + ':' + p2(d.getUTCMinutes()) + ' UTC'; var s = ''; if (seasonEnd > Date.now()) { var left = seasonEnd - Date.now(), day = Math.min(14, Math.floor((Date.now() - seasonStart) / 86400000) + 1); s = ' · season day ' + day + '/14 · ' + (left > 86400000 ? Math.floor(left / 86400000) + 'd ' + Math.floor(left % 86400000 / 3600000) + 'h' : Math.floor(left / 3600000) + 'h ' + Math.floor(left % 3600000 / 60000) + 'm') + ' left'; } e.textContent = t + s; e.title = 'season boards pay out at the end (Monday 00:00 UTC) · click UTC/local to switch every time on the page'; }
  setInterval(tickClock, 15000); setTz(TZ === 'local' ? 'local' : 'utc', true);
  $('help').addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === $('help')) $('help').hidden = true; });
  // Quick user card: any @user link (they all point at /api/admin/user) opens the card in place; Ctrl/Cmd-click or middle-click keeps the full profile in a new tab
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.shiftKey || e.button !== 0) return; var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href^="/api/admin/user?"]'); if (!a || a.classList.contains('btn')) return; e.preventDefault(); var m = a.getAttribute('href').match(/[?&](u|email)=([^&]+)/); if (!m) return; userCard(decodeURIComponent(m[2]), m[1] === 'email'); });
  function userCard(who, isEmail) {
    var m = modal('<div class="mhead"><b>' + esc(who) + '</b><button class="btn sm x" id="ucX">close</button></div><div id="ucBody"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>'); $('ucX').onclick = closeModal;
    var p = isEmail ? Promise.resolve(who) : J('/api/admin/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(who)).then(function (d) { var u = ((d && d.users) || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.username || '').toLowerCase() === who.toLowerCase(); })[0] || ((d && d.users) || [])[0]; return u && u.email; });
    p.then(function (email) { if (!email) { $('ucBody').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">no account matches</div>'; return; } userCardInto($('ucBody'), email, true); }).catch(function () { $('ucBody').innerHTML = failHtml('could not load the user'); });
  }
  $('sideClose').addEventListener('click', function () { $('side').classList.remove('open'); });
  $('refreshBtn').addEventListener('click', function () { show(); loadTop(); });

  // ---------- top bar: online + attention strip ----------
  var attnItems = [];
  // The strip lives on Today/Overview only (owner 2026-09-03: on the phone it ate half of every screen); everywhere else the
  // sidebar counters carry the same signal.
  function paintAttn() { var box = $('attnbar'); if (route().key !== 'today/overview') { box.innerHTML = ''; return; } box.innerHTML = attnItems.map(function (a) { var go = GO[a.go] || 'today/overview'; return '<a class="al ' + esc(a.cls) + '" href="#' + go + '">' + a.html + '</a>'; }).join(''); }
  var topS = null, attnSeen = null, flashT = null;
  function pulse() { var e = $('pulse'); if (!e || !topS) return; var t = counts['inbox/support'] || 0, w = counts['money/withdrawals'] || 0; e.innerHTML = '<span title="visitors today"><b>' + N(topS.uvToday) + '</b> uv</span><span title="money clicks today"><b>' + N(topS.affToday) + '</b> clicks</span><span title="support tickets waiting for a reply" class="' + (t ? 'hot' : '') + '"><b>' + N(t) + '</b> tickets</span><span title="withdrawals waiting for payment" class="' + (w ? 'hot' : '') + '"><b>' + N(w) + '</b> payouts</span>'; }
  // a red or amber item that was not on the strip a minute ago = something new needs you: toast + tab-title flash (+ desktop notification if enabled)
  function newItems(items) { var keys = {}; items.forEach(function (a) { keys[a.k + '|' + a.cls] = a; }); if (attnSeen) { Object.keys(keys).forEach(function (k) { var a = keys[k]; if (attnSeen[k] || (a.cls !== 'red' && a.cls !== 'amber')) return; var txt = String(a.html || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 90); toast('New: ' + txt); flash(); if (store('notif') && window.Notification && Notification.permission === 'granted') { try { var n = new Notification('MarginPad ops', { body: txt, tag: k }); n.onclick = function () { window.focus(); location.hash = GO[a.go] || 'today/overview'; }; } catch (e) {} } }); } attnSeen = keys; }
  function flash() { clearTimeout(flashT); var n = 0; (function tick() { document.title = (n % 2 ? '● ' : '') + (redN ? '(' + redN + ') ' : '') + baseTitle; if (++n < 12) flashT = setTimeout(tick, 900); else setTitle(); })(); }
  function loadTop() {
    J('/api/stats?format=json').then(function (d) { if (d && typeof d.online === 'number') { $('online').innerHTML = '<i></i><b>' + N(d.online) + '</b> online'; topS = d; pulse(); } }).catch(function () {});
    J('/api/admin/attention').then(function (d) {
      attnItems = (d && d.items) || []; paintAttn(); newItems(attnItems);
      counts = {}; attnItems.forEach(function (a) { if (a.cls === 'red' || a.cls === 'amber') { var go = GO[a.go]; if (go) counts[go] = (counts[go] || 0) + 1; } }); renderNav(); pulse();
      redN = attnItems.filter(function (a) { return a.cls === 'red' || a.cls === 'amber'; }).length; setTitle();
    }).catch(function () {});
  }
  // market strip: BTC and ETH with the 24h move and the in-house Fear & Greed - volatility is what fills the site
  var fngNow = null;
  function loadMkt() { Promise.all([J('/api/prices').catch(function () { return null; }), J('/api/fng').catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (r) { var e = $('mkt'); if (!e) return; var P = {}; (((r[0] || {}).pairs) || []).forEach(function (p) { P[String(p.symbol || '').replace(/USDT$/, '')] = p; }); var f = ((r[1] || {}).data || [])[0]; fngNow = f || null; var px = function (s) { var p = P[s]; if (!p) return ''; var ch = +p.changePct || 0, v = +p.price || 0; return '<span title="' + s + ' · 24h change"><b>' + s + '</b> ' + (v >= 1000 ? N(Math.round(v)) : v.toFixed(v >= 10 ? 2 : 4)) + ' <i class="' + (ch >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + (ch >= 0 ? '+' : '') + ch.toFixed(1) + '%</i></span>'; }; e.innerHTML = px('BTC') + px('ETH') + (f ? '<span title="Fear &amp; Greed (in-house), ' + esc(f.c || '') + '"><b>F&amp;G</b> ' + esc(f.v) + ' <i class="' + (+f.v >= 55 ? 'up' : +f.v <= 45 ? 'dn' : '') + '">' + esc(String(f.c || '').toLowerCase()) + '</i></span>' : ''); }); }
  var GO = { wd: 'money/withdrawals', rewards: 'money/faucet', support: 'inbox/support', stats: 'health/overview', spot: 'trading/spot', premium: 'money/premium', users: 'people/users' };

  // ---------- components ----------
  function tile(v, l, cls, sub, mini, attr) { var c = compact(v); var vv = c ? '<span title="' + esc(c.full) + '">' + c.txt + '</span>' : v; return '<div class="tile ' + (cls || '') + (attr ? ' click' : '') + '"' + (attr ? ' ' + attr + ' title="click for the list behind this number"' : '') + '><div class="v">' + vv + (sub ? '<small>' + sub + '</small>' : '') + '</div><div class="l">' + l + '</div>' + (mini || '') + '</div>'; }
  // 24 cells, one per UTC hour: today in lime over the usual hour (7-day average) in grey; the current hour is marked
  function hours(today, avg, hour) { var mx = 1; for (var i = 0; i < 24; i++) { if (today[i] > mx) mx = today[i]; if (avg[i] > mx) mx = avg[i]; } var out = '<div class="hr">'; for (var h = 0; h < 24; h++) { out += '<div class="hc' + (h === hour ? ' now' : h > hour ? ' fut' : '') + '" title="' + p2(h) + ':00 UTC · today ' + N(today[h]) + ' · usual ' + N(avg[h]) + '"><i class="a" style="height:' + Math.max(2, avg[h] / mx * 100) + '%"></i><i class="t" style="height:' + (h > hour ? 0 : Math.max(today[h] ? 3 : 0, today[h] / mx * 100)) + '%"></i></div>'; } return out + '</div><div class="hrx"><span>00</span><span>06</span><span>12</span><span>18</span><span>24</span></div>'; }
  // collapsible section: long lists stay one line until you want them (owner 2026-09-04: nothing should be a wall of scroll)
  function fold(title, body, sub, open) { return '<details class="fold"' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '><summary><b>' + title + '</b>' + (sub ? '<span>' + sub + '</span>' : '') + '</summary><div class="fold-b">' + body + '</div></details>'; }
  // Overview drill-down: the list behind a KPI tile (/api/admin/drill?k=visitors|returning|affiliate|signups|dwell)
  var DRILL = { visitors: 'Visitors today: landing page per visitor', returning: 'Returning today', affiliate: 'Money clicks today', signups: 'Sign-ups today', dwell: 'Most time on site, all users' };
  function drill(kind) {
    modal('<div class="mhead"><b>' + esc(DRILL[kind] || kind) + '</b><button class="btn sm x" id="drX">close</button></div><div id="drBody"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>'); $('drX').onclick = closeModal;
    J('/api/admin/drill?k=' + encodeURIComponent(kind)).then(function (d) { var rows = (d && d.rows) || []; var b = $('drBody'); if (!b) return;
      var row = function (x, i) { var whoH = x.u ? userLink(x.u, x.email) : (x.email ? userLink('', x.email) : '<span class="meta">visitor</span>');
        if (kind === 'visitors') return '<div class="row">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoH + '</span><span class="grow mono">' + esc(x.path) + (x.src ? ' <span class="meta">from ' + esc(x.src) + '</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="meta">' + esc(x.dev || '') + ' · ' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span></div>';
        if (kind === 'affiliate') return '<div class="row">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoH + '</span><span class="grow"><b>' + esc(x.ex) + '</b> <span class="meta mono">' + esc(x.path) + '</span></span><span class="meta">' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span></div>';
        if (kind === 'dwell') { var s = +x.secs || 0; var dlt = x.delta == null ? '' : (x.delta > 0 ? '<span class="up">&#9650;' + x.delta + '</span>' : x.delta < 0 ? '<span class="dn">&#9660;' + Math.abs(x.delta) + '</span>' : '<span class="meta">=</span>'); return '<div class="row"><span class="mono meta" style="width:32px">' + (i + 1) + '.</span>' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoH + '</span><span class="grow"></span>' + dlt + '<span class="mono" style="min-width:60px;text-align:right">' + (s < 3600 ? Math.round(s / 60) + 'm' : (s / 3600).toFixed(1) + 'h') + '</span></div>'; }
        return '<div class="row">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoH + '</span><span class="grow meta">' + esc(x.email || '') + (x.dev ? ' · ' + esc(x.dev) : '') + (x.pv != null ? ' · ' + N(x.pv) + ' pv' : '') + '</span><span class="meta">' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span></div>'; };
      b.innerHTML = (d.note ? '<div class="line" style="margin-bottom:8px;font-size:12px">' + esc(d.note) + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="line" style="margin-bottom:6px"><b>' + N(rows.length) + '</b> rows</div><div class="feed" style="max-height:60vh">' + (rows.map(row).join('') || '<div class="empty">nothing yet today</div>') + '</div>'; }).catch(function () { var b = $('drBody'); if (b) b.innerHTML = failHtml(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.tile[data-drill]'); if (t && !e.target.closest('a')) drill(t.getAttribute('data-drill')); });
  function mini(arr, key) { if (!arr || arr.length < 3) return ''; var mx = 1; arr.forEach(function (x) { if (+x[key] > mx) mx = +x[key]; }); return '<div class="mini" title="last ' + arr.length + ' days">' + arr.map(function (x, i) { return '<i class="' + (i === arr.length - 1 ? 'last' : '') + '" style="height:' + Math.max(6, (+x[key] || 0) / mx * 100) + '%"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }
  function card(title, body, sub, right) { var csv = /<table class="tbl"/.test(body || '') ? '<button class="csv" data-csv title="download this table as CSV">csv</button>' : ''; return '<div class="card"><h2>' + title + (sub ? '<span>' + sub + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="r">' + (right || '') + csv + '</span></h2>' + body + '</div>'; }
  function dl(v, a) { a = +a || 0; v = +v || 0; if (a < 5) return ''; var p = Math.round((v - a) / a * 100); return ' <span class="' + (p >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + (p >= 0 ? '&#9650;' : '&#9660;') + Math.abs(p) + '%</span>'; }
  function spark(arr, key) { if (!arr || !arr.length) return ''; var mx = 1; arr.forEach(function (x) { if (+x[key] > mx) mx = +x[key]; }); return '<div class="spark">' + arr.map(function (x, i) { return '<i class="' + (i === arr.length - 1 ? 'last' : '') + '" style="height:' + Math.max(2, (+x[key] || 0) / mx * 100) + '%" title="' + esc(x.day || x.d || '') + ' · ' + N(x[key]) + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }

  // modal: one at a time, Escape or backdrop closes, route change closes
  var MODAL = null;
  function closeModal() { if (MODAL && MODAL.parentNode) MODAL.parentNode.removeChild(MODAL); MODAL = null; }
  function modal(html) { closeModal(); var m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'modal'; m.innerHTML = '<div class="mbox">' + html + '</div>'; m.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === m) closeModal(); }); document.body.appendChild(m); MODAL = m; return m; }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && MODAL) closeModal(); });
  function tog(id, on, label, sub) { return '<div class="fr"><label for="' + id + '">' + label + (sub ? '<small>' + sub + '</small>' : '') + '</label><button type="button" class="tog ' + (on ? 'on' : '') + '" id="' + id + '" aria-pressed="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '"><i></i></button></div>'; }
  function togOn(id) { var b = $(id); return !!(b && b.classList.contains('on')); }
  function wireTogs(el) { el.querySelectorAll('.tog').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { b.classList.toggle('on'); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.classList.contains('on') ? 'true' : 'false'); }; }); }
  function num(id, v, label, sub, step, min) { return '<div class="fr"><label for="' + id + '">' + label + (sub ? '<small>' + sub + '</small>' : '') + '</label><input class="in" type="number" id="' + id + '" value="' + (v == null ? '' : esc(v)) + '" step="' + (step || 1) + '" min="' + (min == null ? 0 : min) + '"></div>'; }
  function val(id) { var e = $(id); var v = e ? parseFloat(e.value) : NaN; return isFinite(v) ? v : 0; }
  function setStat(id, txt, ok) { var e = $(id); if (!e) return; e.textContent = txt; e.className = 'stat ' + (ok === true ? 'ok' : ok === false ? 'bad' : ''); if (txt) setTimeout(function () { if (e.textContent === txt) e.textContent = ''; }, 4000); }

  // ---------- TODAY ----------
  // "Since your last visit" remembers when this browser last had the dashboard open (localStorage) and lists what arrived
  // in between: sign-ups, tickets, withdrawals, alarms, money clicks. The stamp moves only when the tab goes away, so a
  // long session keeps its baseline.
  var visitPrev = store('seen') || 0; var visitStart = Date.now(), hiddenAt = 0;
  function markSeen() { store('seen', Date.now()); }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (document.hidden) { hiddenAt = Date.now(); markSeen(); } else if (hiddenAt && Date.now() - hiddenAt > 10 * 60000) { visitPrev = hiddenAt; visitStart = Date.now(); hiddenAt = 0; if (route().key === 'today/overview') show(); } }); // back after 10+ minutes away: the baseline moves to when you left
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', markSeen);
  VIEWS['today/overview'] = { render: function (el) {
    function paint() {
      if (document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'opsNotes') return; // never repaint under the owner's cursor

        // season: leaders per board + who would be paid if it ended now
