        if (lb.weekEnd) { var bp = lb.boardPrizes || {}; var lead = [['Highest ROE', (lb.top || [])[0], function (x) { return '+' + N(Math.round(+x.roe)) + '% on ' + esc(x.symbol); }], ['Best win rate', (lb.topWr || [])[0], function (x) { return x.wr + '% (' + x.w + '-' + x.l + ')'; }], ['Season XP', (lb.topXp || [])[0], function (x) { return N(x.xp) + ' XP'; }], ['Green days', (lb.topGreen || [])[0], function (x) { return x.days + ' green day' + (x.days === 1 ? '' : 's'); }]];
          var pay = {}; [['roe', lb.top], ['wr', lb.topWr], ['xp', lb.topXp], ['green', lb.topGreen]].forEach(function (x) { (x[1] || []).slice(0, 5).forEach(function (e, i) { var pz = +((bp[x[0]] || [])[i]) || 0; if (!pz) return; pay[e.who] = (pay[e.who] || 0) + pz; }); }); var pk = Object.keys(pay).sort(function (a2, b2) { return pay[b2] - pay[a2]; }); var payTot = pk.reduce(function (t, k) { return t + pay[k]; }, 0);
          ssC = card('Season', '<div class="grid g4">' + lead.map(function (x) { return '<div class="tile dim"><div class="v" style="font-size:16px">' + (x[1] ? '@' + esc(x[1].who) : '<span class="meta">nobody yet</span>') + '</div><div class="l">' + x[0] + (x[1] ? ' · ' + x[2](x[1]) : '') + '</div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="line" style="margin-top:10px">If it ended now: <b>' + N(pk.length) + '</b> traders share <b>' + USD(payTot, 0) + '</b>' + (pk.length ? ' · ' + pk.slice(0, 6).map(function (k) { return userLink(k) + ' ' + USD(pay[k], 0); }).join(' · ') + (pk.length > 6 ? ' · +' + (pk.length - 6) + ' more' : '') : '') + '</div>', 'ends ' + dt(lb.weekEnd) + ' · top 5 per board paid automatically · WR board needs 20 counted trades', '<a href="#money/faucet">boards</a>'); }
        // sign-ups today with whether they already traded (the only quality signal on day one)
        var suC = suToday.length ? card('Sign-ups today', '<div class="line">' + suToday.slice(0, 20).map(function (u) { var tr = (+u.trades || 0) + (+u.sTrades || 0); return '<span style="display:inline-block;margin:0 12px 4px 0">' + userLink(u.username, u.email) + ' ' + flag(u.cc) + (tr ? ' <span class="badge ok">traded</span>' : (u.vpn ? ' <span class="badge">VPN</span>' : '')) + ' <span class="meta mono" style="font-size:11px">' + agoT(u.created) + '</span></span>'; }).join('') + (suToday.length > 20 ? ' <span class="meta">+' + (suToday.length - 20) + ' more</span>' : '') + '</div>', N(suTraded.length) + ' of ' + N(suToday.length) + ' already opened a trade', '<a href="#people/users">users</a>') : '';
        // notes to self (KV): the same text on every device, saved as you type
        var ntC = card('Notes to self', '<textarea class="in" id="opsNotes" rows="3" placeholder="things to do, people to pay, ideas - saved as you type, same on every device">' + esc(notes.text || '') + '</textarea><div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:4px"><span id="opsNotesSt" class="stat">' + (notes.ts ? 'saved ' + ago(notes.ts) + ' ago' : '') + '</span></div>', 'private, only this dashboard');
        var bestI = -1, bestV = 0; sn.forEach(function (x, i) { if ((+x.uv || 0) > bestV) { bestV = +x.uv; bestI = i; } });
        var sp2 = days.length ? card('Last 14 days', spark(days.slice(-14), 'uv') + '<div class="line" style="margin-top:6px">visitors per day' + (bestI >= 0 ? ' · best <b>' + N(bestV) + '</b> on ' + esc(String(sn[bestI].day).slice(5)) : '') + ' · ' + spark(days.slice(-14), 'clicks').replace('class="spark"', 'class="spark" style="height:26px"') + ' money clicks</div>') : '';
        el.innerHTML = html + '<div class="grid g4" style="margin-top:12px">' + paceT + netT + quT + ssT + '</div>' + since + rnC + card('Yesterday and money', yl + ml, 'full-day numbers; the tiles above are today so far') + hrC + onl + laC + ssC + suC + sp2 + hl + ntC + card('Live activity', '<div class="feed">' + (feed || '<div class="empty">quiet</div>') + '</div>', 'last events · refreshes every 30s', '<a href="#people/activity">all activity</a>');
        var nt = $('opsNotes'); if (nt) { var ntT = null, ntLast = nt.value; nt.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(ntT); $('opsNotesSt').textContent = 'typing…'; ntT = setTimeout(function () { var v = nt.value; if (v === ntLast) return; post('/api/admin/notes', { text: v }).then(function (j) { ntLast = v; notes.text = v; $('opsNotesSt').textContent = j && j.ok ? 'saved just now' : 'not saved'; }).catch(function () { $('opsNotesSt').textContent = 'not saved - network'; }); }, 800); }; }
        stamp();
      });
    }
    paint(); every(30000, paint);
  } };

  // ---------- shared user card (Support right pane + the quick card behind every @user link) ----------
  var ucCache = {};
  function userCardInto(u, email) {
    var k = email.toLowerCase(); if (ucCache[k] && Date.now() - ucCache[k].ts < 60000) { u.innerHTML = ucCache[k].html; return; }
    u.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">loading user…</div>';
    J('/api/auth/user?email=' + encodeURIComponent(email)).then(function (d) {
      if (!d || d.error || !d.user) { u.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No MarginPad account with this email - a visitor, or a different address.</div>'; return; }
      var usr = d.user; return J('/api/reward/detail?address=' + encodeURIComponent('u:' + usr.id)).catch(function () { return null; }).then(function (rw) {
        var lvl = usr.level || {}, acct = (rw && rw.account) || {}, fraud = (rw && rw.fraud) || {}, ts = d.tradeSummary || {}, prem = (+usr.premium || 0) > Date.now();
        var wr = (ts.wins + ts.losses) > 0 ? Math.round(ts.wins / (ts.wins + ts.losses) * 100) : null;
        var badges = (lvl.name ? '<span class="badge lime">' + esc(lvl.name) + ' · ' + N(usr.xp) + ' XP</span> ' : '') + (prem ? '<span class="badge amber">PREMIUM</span> ' : '') + (usr.status && usr.status !== 'active' ? '<span class="badge red">' + esc(usr.status.toUpperCase()) + '</span> ' : '') + (usr.muted ? '<span class="badge red">MUTED</span> ' : '') + (usr.vpn ? '<span class="badge">VPN?</span> ' : '') + (acct.banned ? '<span class="badge red">FAUCET BAN</span> ' : '') + ((usr.streak || 0) > 1 ? '<span class="badge">' + usr.streak + '-DAY STREAK</span> ' : '') + (fraud.riskLevel ? '<span class="badge ' + (fraud.riskLevel === 'high' ? 'red' : fraud.riskLevel === 'med' ? 'amber' : '') + '">RISK ' + esc(String(fraud.riskLevel).toUpperCase()) + '</span>' : '');
        var flags = (fraud.flags || []).slice(0, 4).map(function (f) { return '<div class="line dn" style="font-size:12px">' + esc(f) + '</div>'; }).join('');
        var evs = (d.events || []).slice(0, 6).map(function (e) { var lbl = e.type === 'pageview' ? ('viewed ' + (e.path || '/')) : (e.type + (e.label ? ' ' + e.label : '')); return '<div class="row" style="padding:4px 0"><span class="meta" style="min-width:36px">' + ago(e.ts) + '</span><span class="grow" style="font-size:12px">' + esc(String(lbl).slice(0, 48)) + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
        var kv = function (a, b) { return '<div class="sup-kv"><span>' + a + '</span><b>' + b + '</b></div>'; };
        var html = '<div class="sup-uh"><div class="sup-uav">' + (usr.avatar && usr.avatar.indexOf('data:image') === 0 ? '<img src="' + usr.avatar + '" alt="">' : esc(((usr.username || email).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 1) || '?').toUpperCase())) + '</div><div><div class="sup-un">' + (usr.username ? '@' + esc(usr.username) : '(no username)') + '</div><div class="meta">' + esc(usr.email || '') + '</div><div class="meta">' + flag(usr.cc) + ' ' + esc(usr.dev || '?') + ' · ' + esc(usr.br || '?') + '</div></div></div><div style="margin:8px 0 10px;line-height:2">' + badges + '</div>'
          + (usr.bio ? '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-bottom:8px">' + esc(usr.bio).slice(0, 160) + '</div>' : '')
          + kv('joined', usr.created ? ago(usr.created) + ' ago' : '—') + kv('last seen', usr.last_seen ? ago(usr.last_seen) + ' ago' : 'never') + kv('time on site', (function (s) { s = +d.dwellTotal || 0; return s < 3600 ? Math.round(s / 60) + 'm' : (s / 3600).toFixed(1) + 'h'; })()) + kv('page views', N(usr.pv))
          + '<div class="sup-sec">Trading</div>' + kv('trades, lifetime', (ts.n || 0) + ' (' + (ts.wins || 0) + 'W-' + (ts.losses || 0) + 'L' + (wr != null ? ' · ' + wr + '%' : '') + ')') + (ts.season ? kv('this season', N(ts.season.trades) + ' opened · ' + N(ts.season.wins) + 'W-' + N(ts.season.losses) + 'L · <span class="' + ((+ts.season.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + ((+ts.season.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + USD(Math.abs(+ts.season.pnl || 0)) + '</span>') : '') + kv('open now', ts.opens || 0) + kv('total P&amp;L', '<span class="' + ((+ts.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + ((+ts.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + USD(Math.abs(+ts.pnl || 0)) + '</span>')
          + '<div id="ucBoards" class="sup-kv"><span>season boards</span><b class="meta">…</b></div>'
          + (function () { var tr = (d.trades || []).filter(function (t) { return t && (t.status === 'win' || t.status === 'loss' || t.status === 'open'); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.closeTs || b.ts || 0) - (a.closeTs || a.ts || 0); }).slice(0, 5); return tr.length ? '<div class="sup-sec">Last trades</div>' + tr.map(function (t) { var p = +t.pnl || 0, m2 = +t.margin || 0, roe = m2 ? p / m2 * 100 : 0; return '<div class="row" style="padding:4px 0;font-size:12px"><span class="mono" style="width:92px">' + esc(String(t.sym || '').toUpperCase()) + ' <span class="' + (t.side === 'short' ? 'dn' : 'up') + '">' + (t.side === 'short' ? 'S' : 'L') + '</span> ' + (+t.lev || 1) + 'x</span><span class="mono grow ' + (t.status === 'open' ? 'meta' : p >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + (t.status === 'open' ? 'open · $' + N(Math.round(m2)) : (p >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + USD(Math.abs(p)) + ' <small>' + (roe >= 0 ? '+' : '') + roe.toFixed(0) + '%</small>') + (t.src === 'srv' && t.sc ? '' : t.status === 'open' ? '' : ' <span class="badge" title="not settled by the server: never counts on the boards">local</span>') + '</span><span class="meta">' + agoT(t.closeTs || t.ts) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : ''; })()
          + '<div class="sup-sec">Rewards <button type="button" class="btn sm" data-money="u:' + esc(usr.id) + '" data-money-t="@' + esc(usr.username || email) + '" style="margin-left:8px;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0">Money history</button></div>' + kv('balance', USD(acct.balanceUsd)) + kv('earned total', USD(acct.earnedUsd)) + kv('claims', N(acct.claims)) + kv('withdrawals', N((rw && rw.withdrawals && rw.withdrawals.length) || 0)) + flags + '<div id="ucMoney"></div>'
          + '<div class="sup-sec">Recent activity</div>' + (evs || '<div class="meta">no tracked activity</div>')
          + '<div class="bar" style="margin-top:10px"><a class="btn sm lime" href="/api/admin/user?email=' + encodeURIComponent(email) + '" target="_blank">Full profile</a><a class="btn sm" href="#people/journeys/' + encodeURIComponent(usr.username || email) + '">Journey</a><a class="btn sm" href="#inbox/mail/' + encodeURIComponent(email) + '">Email</a><a class="btn sm" href="#inbox/support/' + encodeURIComponent(email) + '">Tickets</a></div>';
        ucCache[k] = { ts: Date.now(), html: html }; u.innerHTML = html;
        boardsFor(usr.username).then(function (bh) { var b = u.querySelector('#ucBoards b'); if (b) { b.innerHTML = bh; b.className = ''; } ucCache[k].html = u.innerHTML; });
        J('/api/admin/acctlog?acct=' + encodeURIComponent('u:' + usr.id) + '&n=8').then(function (ml) { var box = u.querySelector('#ucMoney'); if (!box || !ml || ml.error) return; var rows = moneyRows(ml).slice(0, 5); box.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(function (r) { var cls = r.dir === 'in' ? 'up' : r.dir === 'out' ? 'dn' : r.dir === 'paid' ? 'gold' : 'meta'; return '<div class="row" style="padding:3px 0;font-size:12px"><span class="meta" style="min-width:36px">' + ago(r.ts) + '</span><span class="grow">' + esc(r.what) + (r.det ? ' <span class="meta">' + esc(String(r.det).slice(0, 36)) + '</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="mono ' + cls + '">' + (r.dir === 'in' ? '+' : r.dir === 'out' ? '-' : '') + USD(r.usd) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="meta" style="font-size:12px">no money movements on record</div>'; ucCache[k].html = u.innerHTML; }).catch(function () {});
      });
    }).catch(function () { u.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load the user'); });
  }
  // where a user stands on the four season boards right now (the question behind half the support tickets)
  var lbCache = null;
  function boardsFor(uname) { if (!uname) return Promise.resolve('<span class="meta">no username</span>'); var get = lbCache && Date.now() - lbCache.ts < 60000 ? Promise.resolve(lbCache.d) : J('/api/reward/lb').then(function (d) { lbCache = { ts: Date.now(), d: d }; return d; });
    return get.then(function (d) { var u = String(uname).toLowerCase(); var find = function (arr) { return (arr || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.who || '').toLowerCase() === u; })[0]; }; var r = find(d.top), w = find(d.topWr), x = find(d.topXp), g = find(d.topGreen); var out = [];
      out.push(r ? '<span class="badge ok" title="Highest ROE board">ROE #' + r.rank + ' · +' + N(Math.round(+r.roe)) + '%</span>' : '<span class="badge" title="Highest ROE board: not in the top 15">ROE —</span>');
      out.push(w ? '<span class="badge ok" title="Best win rate board">WR #' + w.rank + ' · ' + w.w + '-' + w.l + '</span>' : '<span class="badge" title="Best win rate board: not listed (needs 20 counted trades)">WR —</span>');
      out.push(x ? '<span class="badge ok" title="Season XP board">XP #' + x.rank + '</span>' : '<span class="badge" title="Season XP board: not in the top 15">XP —</span>');
      out.push(g ? '<span class="badge ok" title="Green days board">Green #' + g.rank + ' · ' + g.days + 'd</span>' : '<span class="badge" title="Green days board: not listed">Green —</span>');
      return out.join(' '); }).catch(function () { return '<span class="meta">boards unavailable</span>'; }); }

  // ---------- MONEY HISTORY of one account (2026-09-06, owner: "kad udjem u neciji profil... istoriju kako i odakle je dobijao novac i kad se oduzima") ----------
  // /api/admin/acctlog: the durable per-account ledger (every credit and debit since 2026-09-06), the withdrawals with their
  // status, Vault cash, and the reconstructed earnings for the time before the log existed. One timeline, newest first.
  var MT = { welcome: ['Welcome bonus', 'in'], claim: ['Faucet claim', 'in'], mission: ['Daily mission', 'in'], gift: ['Gift', 'in'], lbprize: ['Season board prize', 'in'], promo: ['Promo post', 'in'], promo_paid: ['Promo post paid', 'in'], exsign: ['Exchange sign-up', 'in'], exsign_paid: ['Sign-up bonus paid', 'in'], xengage_paid: ['X engagement paid', 'in'], moon: ['Moon sign-up', 'in'], adjust: ['Owner adjustment', 'adj'], shop: ['Vault purchase', 'out'], shoprefund: ['Vault refund', 'in'], withdraw: ['Withdrawal requested', 'out'], wdreject: ['Withdrawal rejected, refunded', 'in'], wdcancel: ['Withdrawal cancelled, refunded', 'in'], refpaid: ['Referral bonus', 'in'], code: ['Code', 'in'] };
  function moneyRows(d) {
    var out = [], oldest = +d.oldest || 0;
    (d.rows || []).forEach(function (r) { var m = MT[r.type] || [r.type, r.amount >= 0 ? 'in' : 'out']; var amt = +r.amount || 0; var dir = m[1] === 'adj' ? (amt >= 0 ? 'in' : 'out') : m[1]; var det = r.detail || ''; if (r.type === 'gift' && det === 'code') { m = MT.code; det = 'redeemed a code'; } else if (r.type === 'gift' && det) det = 'from @' + det; out.push({ ts: r.ts, what: m[0], det: det, usd: Math.abs(amt) / 100, dir: dir, src: 'ledger' }); });
    (d.wds || []).forEach(function (w) { if (w.status === 'paid' && w.paid_ts) out.push({ ts: w.paid_ts, what: 'Withdrawal PAID', det: (w.address || '') + (w.txid ? ' · tx ' + String(w.txid).slice(0, 12) + '…' : ''), usd: ((+w.amount || 0) + (+w.bonus || 0)) / 100, dir: 'paid', src: 'wd' }); if (w.status === 'rejected' && w.paid_ts) out.push({ ts: w.paid_ts, what: 'Withdrawal rejected', det: w.note || '', usd: (+w.amount || 0) / 100, dir: 'note', src: 'wd' }); if (w.ts < oldest || !oldest) out.push({ ts: w.ts, what: 'Withdrawal requested', det: w.address || '', usd: ((+w.amount || 0) + (+w.bonus || 0)) / 100, dir: 'out', src: 'wd-old' }); });
    (d.shop || []).forEach(function (s) { if (s.ts < oldest || !oldest) out.push({ ts: s.ts, what: s.kind === 'refund' ? 'Vault refund' : 'Vault purchase', det: s.item || '', usd: (+s.cents || 0) / 100, dir: s.kind === 'refund' ? 'in' : 'out', src: 'shop-old' }); });
    if (d.earnings) (d.earnings.items || []).forEach(function (it) { if (oldest && it.ts >= oldest && it.type !== 'welcome') return; var L = { exsign: 'Exchange sign-up', promo: 'Promo post', lbprize: 'Season board prize', welcome: 'Welcome bonus', claim: 'Faucet claim', moon: 'Moon sign-up' }; if (it.type === 'claim' && oldest && it.ts >= oldest) return; out.push({ ts: it.ts, what: L[it.type] || it.type, det: it.type === 'lbprize' ? ('#' + it.rank + ' ' + (it.board || '') + ' · week ' + it.week) : it.type === 'promo' ? (it.platform || '') + ' ' + (it.url || '') : it.type === 'exsign' ? (it.exchange || '') + ' UID ' + (it.uid || '') : (it.approx ? 'approximate' : ''), usd: +it.usd || 0, dir: 'in', src: 'earn-old' }); });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.ts || 0) - (a.ts || 0); });
    return out;
  }
  function moneyView(acct, title) {
    modal('<div class="mhead"><b>Money · ' + esc(title || acct) + '</b><span class="meta" id="mvSub"></span><button class="btn sm x" id="mvX">close</button></div><div id="mvBody"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>'); $('mvX').onclick = closeModal;
    var f = 'all';
    J('/api/admin/acctlog?acct=' + encodeURIComponent(acct) + '&n=1000&_=' + Date.now()).then(function (d) { var b = $('mvBody'); if (!b) return; if (!d || d.error) { b.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">no ledger account for this user (never claimed, never paid)</div>'; return; }
      var a = d.account || {}, rows = moneyRows(d); var inSum = 0, outSum = 0, paid = 0; rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.dir === 'in') inSum += r.usd; else if (r.dir === 'out') outSum += r.usd; else if (r.dir === 'paid') paid += r.usd; });
      function paint() { var list = rows.filter(function (r) { return f === 'all' || (f === 'in' && r.dir === 'in') || (f === 'out' && (r.dir === 'out')) || (f === 'wd' && (r.src === 'wd' || r.src === 'wd-old' || r.what.indexOf('Withdrawal') === 0)); });
        $('mvList').innerHTML = list.length ? list.slice(0, 500).map(function (r) { var cls = r.dir === 'in' ? 'up' : r.dir === 'out' ? 'dn' : r.dir === 'paid' ? 'gold' : 'meta'; return '<div class="row" style="padding:5px 0"><span class="meta mono" style="min-width:96px" title="' + esc(dtFull(r.ts)) + '">' + esc(dt(r.ts)) + '</span><span class="grow"><b>' + esc(r.what) + '</b>' + (r.det ? ' <span class="meta">' + esc(String(r.det).slice(0, 80)) + '</span>' : '') + (r.src === 'earn-old' || r.src === 'wd-old' || r.src === 'shop-old' ? ' <span class="badge" title="from the reconstructed history (before the durable log existed)">history</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="mono ' + cls + '" style="min-width:70px;text-align:right">' + (r.dir === 'in' ? '+' : r.dir === 'out' ? '-' : '') + USD(r.usd) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">nothing in this filter</div>'; var sub = $('mvSub'); if (sub) sub.textContent = N(list.length) + ' rows'; }
      b.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(USD(((+a.balance || 0)) / 100), 'balance now', 'dim') + tile(USD(inSum), 'credited (on record)', 'dim', N((+a.claims || 0)) + ' claims') + tile(USD(outSum), 'spent + requested', 'dim') + tile(USD(paid), 'paid out', paid ? '' : 'dim') + '</div>'
        + '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin:8px 0">' + (d.oldest ? 'exact ledger since ' + dt(d.oldest) + ' · older rows are the reconstructed history (approximate claims)' : 'no ledger rows yet — everything shown is reconstructed history') + (a.banned ? ' · <span class="dn">FAUCET BANNED</span>' : '') + (a.payout_addr ? ' · payout ' + esc(a.payout_addr) : '') + '</div>'
        + '<div class="chips">' + [['all', 'All'], ['in', 'Money in'], ['out', 'Money out'], ['wd', 'Withdrawals']].map(function (x) { return '<span class="chip ' + (f === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-mf="' + x[0] + '">' + x[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="feed" id="mvList" style="max-height:60vh"></div>';
      b.querySelectorAll('[data-mf]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { f = c.getAttribute('data-mf'); b.querySelectorAll('[data-mf]').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.toggle('on', x === c); }); paint(); }; });
      paint();
    }).catch(function () { var b = $('mvBody'); if (b) b.innerHTML = failHtml(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-money]'); if (!b) return; e.preventDefault(); moneyView(b.getAttribute('data-money'), b.getAttribute('data-money-t') || ''); });

  // ---------- INBOX / SUPPORT (native, 2026-09-03) ----------
  // Three panes: conversations (open/closed, search, affiliate inbox first), the thread with a composer that sends from a
  // whitelisted identity, and the live user card. Data: /api/reward/support/convs, /api/reward/reply, /api/reward/support/close,
  // /api/reward/support/new, /api/auth/user?email=, /api/reward/detail?address=u:<id>.
  VIEWS['inbox/support'] = { render: function (el, arg) {
    var st = { tab: 'open', q: '', sel: null, convs: [], emailReady: false, compose: false, pane: 'list' };
    // canned replies live in this browser (localStorage); a chip inserts, the small x forgets
    function tpls() { return store('tpl') || []; }
    function tplBar() { var t = tpls(); return '<div class="tpl" id="supTpl">' + t.map(function (x, i) { return '<span class="chip" data-tpl="' + i + '">' + esc(x.name) + ' <b data-tpldel="' + i + '" title="forget this template">x</b></span>'; }).join('') + '<button class="btn sm" id="supTplSave" title="save the current reply text as a template">+ save as template</button></div>'; }
    function wireTpl(ta) { el.querySelectorAll('[data-tpl]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function (e) { if (e.target.hasAttribute('data-tpldel')) return; var x = tpls()[+c.getAttribute('data-tpl')]; if (!x) return; ta.value = (ta.value ? ta.value.replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n\n' : '') + x.text; ta.focus(); }; }); el.querySelectorAll('[data-tpldel]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var t = tpls(); t.splice(+b.getAttribute('data-tpldel'), 1); store('tpl', t); paintThread(); }; }); var s = $('supTplSave'); if (s) s.onclick = function () { var txt = ta.value.trim(); if (!txt) { toast('write the reply first, then save it'); return; } var name = prompt('Template name:', txt.slice(0, 24)); if (!name) return; var t = tpls(); t.push({ name: name.slice(0, 32), text: txt }); store('tpl', t); paintThread(); }; }
    function staleAnswered() { var lim = Date.now() - 14 * 86400000; return st.convs.filter(function (c) { return !c.closed && last(c).dir === 'out' && (c.lastTs || 0) < lim; }); }
    function frame() {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="sup" data-pane="' + esc(st.pane) + '"><div class="sup-l"><div class="bar" style="margin:0 0 8px"><input class="in" id="supQ" placeholder="search tickets" value="' + esc(st.q) + '"></div><div class="sup-stats" id="supStats"></div><div class="chips" style="margin:0 0 8px"><span class="chip ' + (st.tab === 'open' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-st="open">Open <b id="supNo"></b></span><span class="chip ' + (st.tab === 'closed' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-st="closed">Closed <b id="supNc"></b></span><button class="btn sm lime" id="supNew" style="margin-left:auto">New ticket</button></div><div id="supList" class="sup-list"></div></div>'
        + '<div class="sup-t" id="supThread"><div class="empty">Select a ticket. The conversation opens here and the user card on the right.</div></div><div class="sup-u" id="supUser"><div class="empty">No ticket selected</div></div></div>';
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-st]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { st.tab = c.getAttribute('data-st'); frame(); paintList(); }; });
      var q = $('supQ'); q.oninput = function () { st.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paintList(); };
      $('supNew').onclick = function () { st.compose = true; st.sel = null; paintThread(); showPane('thread'); };
    }
    function showPane(p) { st.pane = p; el.querySelector('.sup').setAttribute('data-pane', p); }
    function load(silent) { return J('/api/reward/support/convs?_=' + Date.now()).then(function (d) { st.convs = (d && d.conversations) || []; st.emailReady = !!(d && d.emailReady); paintList(); if (st.sel && !silent) paintThread(); }).catch(function () { if (!silent) $('supList').innerHTML = '<div class="empty err">could not load</div>'; }); }
    function convOf(id) { return st.convs.filter(function (c) { return c.conv === id; })[0] || null; }
    function last(c) { return c.messages[c.messages.length - 1] || {}; }
    function paintList() {
      var open = st.convs.filter(function (c) { return !c.closed; }), closed = st.convs.filter(function (c) { return c.closed; });
      $('supNo').textContent = open.length; $('supNc').textContent = closed.length;
      // response-time facts from the threads themselves: first reply after each incoming message (30 days), the oldest still waiting, replies today
      var now = Date.now(), rts = [], oldest = null, repliedToday = 0, todayStart = Date.UTC(new Date().getUTCFullYear(), new Date().getUTCMonth(), new Date().getUTCDate()), byEmail = {};
      st.convs.forEach(function (c) { var em = String(c.email || '').toLowerCase(); if (em) byEmail[em] = (byEmail[em] || 0) + 1; var pin = null; c.messages.forEach(function (m) { if (m.dir === 'in') { if (pin == null) pin = m.ts; } else { if (pin != null && now - m.ts < 30 * 86400000) rts.push(m.ts - pin); pin = null; if (m.ts >= todayStart) repliedToday++; } }); if (!c.closed && pin != null && (!oldest || pin < oldest)) oldest = pin; });
      var med = median(rts); var ss = $('supStats'); if (ss) ss.innerHTML = '<span title="median time from a user message to our reply, last 30 days"><b>' + (med != null ? fdur(med) : '—') + '</b> median reply</span><span title="the longest a user has been waiting right now" class="' + (oldest && now - oldest > 86400000 ? 'hot' : '') + '"><b>' + (oldest ? fdur(now - oldest) : '0') + '</b> oldest waiting</span><span title="replies sent today (UTC)"><b>' + N(repliedToday) + '</b> replied today</span>';
      var list = (st.tab === 'open' ? open : closed).filter(function (c) { if (!st.q) return true; return ((c.email || '') + ' ' + (c.title || '') + ' ' + c.messages.map(function (m) { return m.body || ''; }).join(' ')).toLowerCase().indexOf(st.q) >= 0; });
      var aff = list.filter(function (c) { return c.aff; }), rest = list.filter(function (c) { return !c.aff; });
      var row = function (c) { var l = last(c), wait = !c.closed && l.dir === 'in'; var nm = (c.email || '?'); var waitMs = wait ? now - (c.lastInTs || c.lastTs || now) : 0; var wcls = waitMs > 72 * 3600000 ? 'dn' : waitMs > 24 * 3600000 ? 'amber' : ''; var isNew = wait && visitPrev && (c.lastInTs || 0) > visitPrev; var nth = byEmail[nm.toLowerCase()] || 0; var hasImg = c.messages.some(function (m) { return m.img; });
        return '<div class="sup-row ' + (st.sel === c.conv ? 'on' : '') + '" data-conv="' + esc(c.conv) + '"><span class="sup-av">' + esc(nm.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()) + '</span><span class="sup-b"><b>' + esc(nm) + (wait ? ' <i class="sup-dot" title="waiting for a reply"></i>' : '') + (isNew ? ' <span class="badge lime" title="arrived since your last visit">NEW</span>' : '') + (nth > 1 ? ' <span class="badge" title="this person has ' + nth + ' threads - click to see them all" data-em="' + esc(nm) + '">' + nth + ' threads</span>' : '') + (hasImg ? ' <span class="badge" title="has a screenshot">img</span>' : '') + '</b><span>' + esc((l.body || c.title || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 64)) + '</span></span><span class="sup-m">' + c.messages.length + ' msg<br>' + (wait ? '<span class="' + wcls + '" title="waiting since ' + esc(dtFull(c.lastInTs || c.lastTs)) + '">' + fdur(waitMs) + ' waiting</span>' : agoT(c.lastTs) + ' ago') + '</span></div>'; };
      var stale = st.tab === 'open' && !st.q ? staleAnswered() : [];
      $('supList').innerHTML = (stale.length ? '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-bottom:8px"><button class="btn sm" id="supBulk">Close ' + stale.length + ' answered, idle 14+ days</button></div>' : '') + (aff.length ? '<div class="sup-sec">Affiliate / creator inbox</div>' + aff.map(row).join('') : '') + (rest.length ? (aff.length ? '<div class="sup-sec">Support</div>' : '') + rest.map(row).join('') : '') + (!list.length ? '<div class="empty">nothing here</div>' : '');
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-conv]').forEach(function (r) { r.onclick = function (e) { var em = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-em]'); if (em) { st.q = em.getAttribute('data-em').toLowerCase(); st.tab = 'open'; frame(); $('supQ').value = st.q; paintList(); return; } st.sel = r.getAttribute('data-conv'); st.compose = false; paintList(); paintThread(); showPane('thread'); }; });
      var bulk = $('supBulk'); if (bulk) bulk.onclick = function () { var s = staleAnswered(); if (!confirm('Close ' + s.length + ' threads where we answered and the user has been silent for 14+ days? They can be reopened.')) return; bulk.disabled = true; var i = 0; (function next() { if (i >= s.length) { toast('closed ' + s.length); load(); loadTop(); return; } post('/api/reward/support/close', { conv: s[i++].conv }).then(next).catch(next); })(); };
    }
    function paintThread() {
      var t = $('supThread'), u = $('supUser');
      if (st.compose) { t.innerHTML = '<div class="sup-h"><b>New ticket</b><span class="meta">starts a thread and emails the user</span><button class="btn sm sup-back" id="supBack">Back</button></div><div class="sup-comp"><input class="in" id="supNEmail" placeholder="user@email"><textarea class="in" id="supNBody" rows="6" placeholder="Message"></textarea><div class="bar"><select class="in" id="supNFrom" style="flex:0 0 auto"><option value="support">support@marginpad.io</option><option value="hello">hello@marginpad.io</option></select><button class="btn lime" id="supNSend">Send</button><span class="meta" id="supNSt"></span></div></div>'; u.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No ticket selected</div>';
        $('supBack').onclick = function () { st.compose = false; paintThread(); showPane('list'); };
        $('supNSend').onclick = function () { var to = $('supNEmail').value.trim(), bd = $('supNBody').value, stt = $('supNSt'); if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(to)) { stt.textContent = 'enter a valid email'; return; } if (!bd.trim()) { stt.textContent = 'write a message first'; return; } stt.textContent = 'sending…'; $('supNSend').disabled = true;
          post('/api/reward/reply', { to: to, subject: 'A message from MarginPad', message: bd, from: $('supNFrom').value }).then(function (j) { if (!j || !j.ok) { stt.textContent = 'failed: ' + ((j && (j.error || j.detail)) || '?'); $('supNSend').disabled = false; return; } return post('/api/reward/support/new', { email: to, message: bd }).then(function () { stt.textContent = 'sent'; toast('ticket created and emailed'); st.compose = false; load().then(function () { showPane('list'); }); }); }).catch(function () { stt.textContent = 'network error'; $('supNSend').disabled = false; }); };
        return; }
      var c = st.sel && convOf(st.sel); if (!c) { t.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Select a ticket. The conversation opens here and the user card on the right.</div>'; u.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No ticket selected</div>'; return; }
      var msgs = c.messages.map(function (m) { return '<div class="sup-msg ' + (m.dir === 'out' ? 'out' : 'in') + '"><div class="sup-bub">' + esc(m.body || '').replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + (m.img ? '<br><img src="' + esc(m.img) + '" alt="" style="max-width:100%;border-radius:8px;margin-top:6px">' : '') + '</div><div class="sup-ts">' + (m.dir === 'out' ? 'MarginPad' : esc(c.email || 'user')) + ' · ' + dt(m.ts) + '</div></div>'; }).join('');
      t.innerHTML = '<div class="sup-h"><button class="btn sm sup-back" id="supBack">Back</button><b>' + esc(c.email || 'no email') + '</b>' + (c.aff ? '<span class="badge lime">AFFILIATE</span>' : '') + (c.closed ? '<span class="badge">CLOSED</span>' : '<span class="badge amber">OPEN</span>') + '<span class="meta">' + c.messages.length + ' messages · last ' + ago(c.lastTs) + ' ago</span><button class="btn sm" id="supClose" style="margin-left:auto">' + (c.closed ? 'Reopen' : 'Close') + '</button></div><div class="sup-msgs" id="supMsgs">' + msgs + '</div>'
        + '<div class="sup-comp">' + (st.emailReady ? '' : '<div class="line dn">Email sending is not configured (RESEND_API_KEY) — replies are only logged.</div>') + '' + tplBar() + '<textarea class="in" id="supBody" rows="4" placeholder="Reply to ' + esc(c.email || 'the user') + '…"></textarea><div class="bar"><select class="in" id="supFrom" style="flex:0 0 auto"><option value="support">support@marginpad.io</option><option value="hello">hello@marginpad.io</option></select><input class="in" id="supSubj" value="Re: your message to MarginPad" style="flex:1"><button class="btn lime" id="supSend"' + (c.email ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Send</button><span class="meta" id="supSt"></span></div></div>';
      var box = $('supMsgs'); box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
      $('supBack').onclick = function () { showPane('list'); };
      $('supClose').onclick = function () { $('supClose').disabled = true; post('/api/reward/support/close', { conv: c.conv, reopen: c.closed ? 1 : 0 }).then(function () { toast(c.closed ? 'reopened' : 'closed'); load(); loadTop(); }); };
      $('supSend').onclick = function () { var bd = $('supBody').value, stt = $('supSt'); if (!bd.trim()) { stt.textContent = 'write a message first'; return; } stt.textContent = 'sending…'; $('supSend').disabled = true;
        post('/api/reward/reply', { to: c.email, subject: $('supSubj').value.trim() || 'Re: your message to MarginPad', message: bd, from: $('supFrom').value, conv: c.conv }).then(function (j) { if (!j || !j.ok) { stt.textContent = 'failed: ' + ((j && (j.error || j.detail)) || '?'); $('supSend').disabled = false; return; } toast(j.logOnly ? 'logged (no email)' : 'sent'); load(); loadTop(); }).catch(function () { stt.textContent = 'network error'; $('supSend').disabled = false; }); };
      $('supBody').addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'Enter') $('supSend').click(); });
      // a half-written reply survives a refresh or a wrong click (per ticket, this browser); cleared once it is sent
      var dk = 'draft:' + c.conv, dr = store(dk); if (dr && !$('supBody').value) { $('supBody').value = dr; $('supSt').textContent = 'draft restored'; }
      $('supBody').addEventListener('input', function () { var v = $('supBody').value; if (v.trim()) store(dk, v); else { try { localStorage.removeItem('ops_' + dk); } catch (e) {} } });
      var sendBtn = $('supSend'); sendBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (sendBtn.disabled) { try { localStorage.removeItem('ops_' + dk); } catch (e) {} } }, 50); });
      wireTpl($('supBody'));
      paintUser(c.email);
    }
    function paintUser(email) { var u = $('supUser'); if (!email) { u.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No email on this ticket</div>'; return; } userCardInto(u, email); }
    recall('inbox/support', st, ['tab']); frame(); load(); every(30000, function () { load(true); });
    if (arg) { st.q = decodeURIComponent(arg); $('supQ').value = st.q; }
    el.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-st]')) remember('inbox/support', st, ['tab']); });
  } };

  // ---------- MONEY / WITHDRAWALS ----------
  VIEWS['money/withdrawals'] = { render: function (el) {
    var st = { f: 'pending', q: '' }; recall('money/withdrawals', st, ['f']);
    function paint() {
      J('/api/admin/withdrawals').then(function (d) {
        var ae = document.activeElement; if (ae && el.contains(ae) && /^(wdUids|wdUidAdd|wdq)$/.test(ae.id)) return; // the 30s repaint must never wipe what the owner is typing
        var all = (d && d.wds) || []; var pend = all.filter(function (w) { return w.status === 'pending'; });
        var paid7 = all.filter(function (w) { return w.status === 'paid' && Date.now() - (+w.paidTs || 0) < 7 * 86400000; });
        var rows = all.filter(function (w) { return (st.f === 'all' || w.status === st.f) && (!st.q || ((w.who || '') + ' ' + (w.email || '') + ' ' + (w.wallet || '')).toLowerCase().indexOf(st.q) >= 0); }).slice(0, 200);
        var over48 = pend.filter(function (w) { return Date.now() - (+w.ts || 0) > 48 * 3600000; }).length;
        // payout speed from the rows themselves: request → paid, last 30 days; and each account's payout history for the row
        var paid30 = all.filter(function (w) { return w.status === 'paid' && +w.paidTs > 0 && +w.ts > 0 && Date.now() - (+w.paidTs) < 30 * 86400000; }), rej30 = all.filter(function (w) { return w.status === 'rejected' && Date.now() - (+w.ts || 0) < 30 * 86400000; });
        var spd = median(paid30.map(function (w) { return +w.paidTs - +w.ts; })), in24 = paid30.filter(function (w) { return +w.paidTs - +w.ts <= 86400000; }).length;
        var byAcct = {}; all.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (+a.ts || 0) - (+b.ts || 0); }).forEach(function (w) { var k2 = w.acct || w.email || w.who; var h = byAcct[k2] || (byAcct[k2] = { paidN: 0, paidUsd: 0, rejN: 0, before: {} }); h.before[w.id] = { paidN: h.paidN, paidUsd: h.paidUsd, rejN: h.rejN }; if (w.status === 'paid') { h.paidN++; h.paidUsd += +w.usd || 0; } else if (w.status === 'rejected') h.rejN++; });
        var pbd = []; for (var i = 13; i >= 0; i--) { var dd = new Date(Date.now() - i * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10); pbd.push({ day: dd, v: all.filter(function (w) { return w.status === 'paid' && new Date(+w.paidTs || 0).toISOString().slice(0, 10) === dd; }).reduce(function (s, w) { return s + (+w.usd || 0); }, 0) }); }
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(pend.length), 'pending payouts', pend.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', USD(pend.reduce(function (s, w) { return s + (+w.usd || 0); }, 0))) + tile(USD(paid7.reduce(function (s, w) { return s + (+w.usd || 0); }, 0)), 'paid last 7 days', 'dim', paid7.length + ' payouts') + tile(N(over48), 'pending over 48h', over48 ? 'amber' : 'dim') + tile(N(d.uidListCount || 0), 'UIDs on the allowlist', 'dim') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="grid g4" style="margin-top:12px">' + tile(spd != null ? fdur(spd) : '—', 'median request to paid', 'dim', paid30.length + ' payouts, 30 days') + tile(paid30.length ? Math.round(in24 / paid30.length * 100) + '%' : '—', 'paid within 24 hours', paid30.length && in24 / paid30.length < 0.5 ? 'amber' : 'dim', 'the promise on /rewards') + tile(USD(paid30.reduce(function (s, w) { return s + (+w.usd || 0); }, 0), 0), 'paid 30 days', 'dim', N(paid30.length) + ' payouts · ' + N(rej30.length) + ' rejected') + tile(USD(pbd.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.v; }, 0), 0), 'paid 14 days', 'dim', 'per day', spark(pbd, 'v').replace('class="spark"', 'class="spark" style="height:22px;margin-top:6px"')) + '</div>'
          + card('Queue', '<div class="bar"><div class="chips" style="margin:0">' + ['pending', 'paid', 'rejected', 'all'].map(function (f) { return '<span class="chip ' + (st.f === f ? 'on' : '') + '" data-f="' + f + '">' + f + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><input class="in" id="wdq" placeholder="search user, email, wallet" value="' + esc(st.q) + '">' + (pend.length ? '<button class="btn sm" id="wdCopyAll" title="one line per pending payout: wallet, amount, user - paste it into your payment sheet">Copy payment list</button>' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="tblwrap"><table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th>when</th><th>who</th><th>wallet (Bybit UID)</th><th class="r">amount</th><th>status</th><th>UID</th><th>history</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'
          + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (w) { var uid = w.uidCheck === 'ok' ? '<span class="badge ok">UID OK</span>' : w.uidCheck === 'missing' ? '<span class="badge red">NOT ON LIST</span>' : '<span class="badge">' + esc(w.uidCheck || '—') + '</span>';
            var stb = w.status === 'pending' ? '<span class="badge amber">PENDING</span>' : w.status === 'paid' ? '<span class="badge ok">PAID</span>' : '<span class="badge red">REJECTED</span>';
            var act = w.status === 'pending' ? '<button class="btn sm lime" data-pay="' + esc(w.id) + '">Pay</button> <button class="btn sm red" data-rej="' + esc(w.id) + '">Reject + notify</button>' : (w.txid ? '<a href="https://bscscan.com/tx/' + esc(w.txid) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">TXID</a>' : '');
            var ageH = (Date.now() - (+w.ts || 0)) / 3600000; var ageCls = w.status === 'pending' ? (ageH > 168 ? 'dn' : ageH > 48 ? 'amber' : '') : '';
            var hb = ((byAcct[w.acct || w.email || w.who] || {}).before || {})[w.id] || { paidN: 0, paidUsd: 0, rejN: 0 }; var hist = (hb.paidN ? '<span class="up">' + (hb.paidN + 1) + (hb.paidN + 1 === 2 ? 'nd' : hb.paidN + 1 === 3 ? 'rd' : 'th') + ' payout</span> · ' + USD(hb.paidUsd, 0) + ' paid before' : '<span class="badge lime" title="no payout before this one">FIRST</span>') + (hb.rejN ? ' · <span class="dn">' + hb.rejN + ' rejected</span>' : '');
            return '<tr><td class="mono ' + ageCls + '" title="' + esc(dtFull(w.ts)) + (w.status === 'pending' ? ' · waiting ' + ago(w.ts) : w.status === 'paid' && w.paidTs ? ' · paid after ' + fdur(+w.paidTs - +w.ts) : '') + '">' + dt(w.ts) + (w.status === 'pending' ? '<br><span style="font-size:11px">' + ago(w.ts) + ' waiting</span>' : w.status === 'paid' && +w.paidTs > +w.ts ? '<br><span class="meta" style="font-size:11px">paid in ' + fdur(+w.paidTs - +w.ts) + '</span>' : '') + '</td><td><a href="/api/admin/user?u=' + encodeURIComponent(w.who || '') + '" target="_blank">@' + esc(w.who || '?') + '</a><br><span class="meta" style="color:var(--faint);font-size:11px">' + esc(w.email || '') + '</span></td><td class="mono">' + esc(w.wallet) + ' <button class="btn sm" data-copy="' + esc(w.wallet) + '">copy</button></td><td class="r mono">' + USD(w.usd) + '</td><td>' + stb + '</td><td>' + uid + '</td><td class="meta">' + hist + '<br>' + N(w.claims) + ' claims · bal ' + USD(w.balUsd) + (w.banned ? ' · <span class="badge red">BANNED</span>' : '') + '</td><td>' + act + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">nothing here</td></tr>')
          + '</tbody></table></div>', 'Pay = mark paid after you sent USDT on BSC · Reject + notify emails the user', '<a href="#money/faucet">faucet &amp; flags</a>')
          + card('Bybit UID allowlist', '<div class="line">' + N(d.uidListCount || 0) + ' UIDs on the list. A withdrawal to a UID that is not here is auto-rejected with the contact-support message; an empty list turns the gate off.</div><div class="bar" style="margin-top:8px"><input class="in" id="wdUidAdd" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="add UIDs: 123456789 987654321 (space, comma or newline)"><button class="btn lime sm" id="wdUidAddBtn">Add to list</button></div><details class="fold"><summary><b>Edit the whole list</b><span id="wdUidSub">loading…</span></summary><div class="fold-b"><textarea class="in" id="wdUids" rows="8" style="width:100%;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px" placeholder="one UID per line"></textarea><div class="bar" style="margin-top:6px"><button class="btn sm" id="wdUidSave">Save list</button><span class="stat" id="wdUidSt"></span></div></div></details>', 'Bybit Affiliate Portal > referred users > UID · checked on every withdrawal request');
        // the allowlist itself: loaded apart from the queue (KV), added to or replaced in full
        J('/api/admin/bybituids').then(function (u) { var ta = $('wdUids'), sub = $('wdUidSub'); if (ta) ta.value = u.text || ''; if (sub) sub.textContent = N(u.count || 0) + ' UIDs' + (u.updated ? ' · updated ' + ago(u.updated) + ' ago' : ''); }).catch(function () { var sub = $('wdUidSub'); if (sub) sub.textContent = 'could not load'; });
        var saveUids = function (text, label) { return post('/api/admin/bybituids', { text: text }).then(function (j) { if (j && j.ok) { toast(label + ' · ' + N(j.count) + ' UIDs on the list'); paint(); loadTop(); } else toast('failed'); }); };
        var addB = $('wdUidAddBtn'); if (addB) addB.onclick = function () { var v = ($('wdUidAdd').value || '').split(/[^0-9]+/).filter(function (x) { return /^[0-9]{5,15}$/.test(x); }); if (!v.length) { toast('type a numeric Bybit UID (5-15 digits)'); return; } addB.disabled = true; saveUids(($('wdUids').value || '') + '\n' + v.join('\n'), 'added ' + v.length); };
        var svB = $('wdUidSave'); if (svB) svB.onclick = function () { if (!confirm('Replace the whole allowlist with the textarea contents?')) return; svB.disabled = true; saveUids($('wdUids').value || '', 'saved'); };
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { st.f = c.getAttribute('data-f'); remember('money/withdrawals', st, ['f']); paint(); }; });
        var q = $('wdq'); if (q) q.oninput = function () { st.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paint(); q.focus(); };
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-copy]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(b.getAttribute('data-copy')); toast('copied'); } catch (e) {} }; });
        var ca = $('wdCopyAll'); if (ca) ca.onclick = function () { var txt = pend.map(function (w) { return w.wallet + '\t' + (+w.usd || 0).toFixed(2) + '\t@' + (w.who || '') + '\t' + (w.uidCheck || ''); }).join('\n'); try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt); toast('copied ' + pend.length + ' lines: wallet, amount, user, UID check'); } catch (e) { prompt('copy:', txt); } };
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-pay]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var tx = prompt('Mark as PAID. Tx hash (optional):'); if (tx === null) return; b.disabled = true; post('/api/reward/admin/paid', { id: b.getAttribute('data-pay'), txid: tx || '' }).then(function (j) { toast(j && j.ok !== false ? 'marked paid' : 'failed'); paint(); loadTop(); }); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-rej]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var r = prompt('Reject and email the user. Reason:'); if (!r) return; b.disabled = true; post('/api/reward/wd/reject', { id: b.getAttribute('data-rej'), note: r }).then(function (j) { toast(j && j.ok ? 'rejected, user notified' : 'failed: ' + ((j && j.error) || '?')); paint(); loadTop(); }); }; });
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load withdrawals'); });
    }
    paint(); every(30000, paint);
  } };

  // ---------- PEOPLE / USERS ----------
  VIEWS['people/users'] = { render: function (el, arg) {
    var st = { f: '', q: '', sort: 'new', offset: 0, limit: 60 }; recall('people/users', st, ['f', 'sort']);
    var actList = null; // today's active users (tenure buckets), fetched once per view
    function tenure() { var box = $('uTenure'); if (!box || !actList) return; var now = Date.now(); var B = [['under a day', 1], ['1 to 7 days', 7], ['7 to 30 days', 30], ['30 to 90 days', 90], ['90+ days', 1e9]]; var cnt = B.map(function () { return 0; }); actList.forEach(function (u) { var dAge = (now - (u.created || now)) / 86400000; for (var i = 0; i < B.length; i++) { if (dAge < B[i][1]) { cnt[i]++; break; } } }); var tot = actList.length || 1; box.innerHTML = bars(B.map(function (b, i) { return { l: b[0], n: cnt[i] }; }), 'l', 'n', function (v) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">' + Math.round(v / tot * 100) + '%</small>'; }) + '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px">' + N(actList.length) + ' signed-in users active today by account age · veterans coming back is the retention signal, a wall of under-a-day is a traffic spike</div>'; }
    var SEG = [['', 'All'], ['active', 'Active today'], ['new7', 'New 7d'], ['hot', 'Hot new'], ['traders', 'Traders'], ['churn', 'Churn risk'], ['premium', 'Premium'], ['premexp', 'Expiring 7d'], ['tg', 'Telegram'], ['flagged', 'Flagged']];
    var SEGT = { hot: 'signed up this week and already 5+ trades', churn: 'traded before, not seen for 7-30 days', premexp: 'Premium ending within 7 days' };
    function paint() {
      J('/api/auth/admin?limit=' + st.limit + '&offset=' + st.offset + '&sort=' + st.sort + (st.f ? '&f=' + st.f : '') + (st.q ? '&q=' + encodeURIComponent(st.q) : '') + '&_=' + Date.now()).then(function (d) {
        var us = (d && d.users) || [];
        var bd = (d.byDay || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1; }); var n7 = bd.slice(-8, -1).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.n || 0); }, 0), p7 = bd.slice(-15, -8).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.n || 0); }, 0);
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(d.count), 'total users', 'dim', N(d.withUname) + ' with a username · ' + N(d.tgLinked) + ' on Telegram') + tile(N(d.newToday), 'new today', d.newToday ? '' : 'dim', dl(d.newToday, n7 / 7) + (n7 / 7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs 7d avg</span>' : ''), mini(bd, 'n'), 'data-drill="signups"') + tile(N(n7), 'new last 7 days', 'dim', dl(n7, p7) + (p7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs prior 7d</span>' : '')) + tile(N(d.activeToday), 'active today', 'dim', N(d.active7) + ' in 7 days · ' + (d.active7 ? Math.round(d.activeToday / d.active7 * 100) + '% stickiness' : ''), '', 'data-drill="returning"') + '</div>'
          + card('Users', '<div class="chips">' + SEG.map(function (s) { return '<span class="chip ' + (st.f === s[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-f="' + s[0] + '" title="' + esc(SEGT[s[0]] || '') + '">' + s[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="bar"><input class="in" id="uq" placeholder="search email or username" value="' + esc(st.q) + '"><select class="in" id="usort">' + [['new', 'Newest'], ['seen', 'Last seen'], ['trades', 'Most trades'], ['pv', 'Most pageviews'], ['logins', 'Most logins']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.sort === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>'
          + '<div class="tblwrap"><table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th>user</th><th>where</th><th class="r" title="lifetime opens: journal closes + open positions, not the 100-row journal">trades</th><th class="r" title="this 14-day season: closes + positions opened in it">season</th><th class="r">pv</th><th class="r">logins</th><th>last seen</th><th>joined</th><th>status</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
          + (us.length ? us.map(function (u) { var name = u.username || (u.email || '').split('@')[0] || ('#' + u.id); var stt = (u.status && u.status !== 'active') ? '<span class="badge red">' + esc(u.status) + '</span>' : (u.muted ? '<span class="badge amber">muted</span>' : '<span class="badge ok">ok</span>');
            return '<tr><td><a href="/api/admin/user?u=' + encodeURIComponent(u.username || u.email || '') + '" target="_blank"><b>' + esc(name) + '</b></a><br><span style="color:var(--faint);font-size:11px">' + esc(u.email || '') + '</span></td><td>' + flag(u.cc) + ' ' + esc(u.cc || '') + ' <span class="meta">' + esc(u.dev || '') + '</span></td><td class="r mono">' + N(u.trades) + '</td><td class="r mono">' + N(u.sTrades) + '</td><td class="r mono">' + N(u.pv) + '</td><td class="r mono">' + N(u.logins) + '</td><td class="mono">' + agoT(u.last_seen) + ' ago</td><td class="mono" title="' + esc(dtFull(u.created)) + '">' + dt(u.created).slice(0, 10) + '</td><td>' + stt + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">no users match</td></tr>')
          + '</tbody></table></div><div class="bar" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn sm" id="uprev" ' + (st.offset ?           + '<div class="grid g3" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Where users come from <span>all accounts</span></h2>' + bars((d.byCc || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { return { html: flag(x.cc), n: x.n }; }), 'html', 'n', function (v) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">' + Math.round(v / Math.max(1, d.count) * 100) + '%</small>'; }) + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Devices</h2>' + bars((d.byDev || []).map(function (x) { return { l: x.dev === '?' ? 'unknown' : x.dev, n: x.n }; }), 'l', 'n', function (v) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">' + Math.round(v / Math.max(1, d.count) * 100) + '%</small>'; }) + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Active today, by account age</h2><div id="uTenure"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div></div></div>';
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { st.f = c.getAttribute('data-f'); st.offset = 0; remember('people/users', st, ['f', 'sort']); paint(); }; });
        var q = $('uq'); if (q) { var t; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(function () { st.q = q.value.trim(); st.offset = 0; paint(); }, 350); }; }
        var so = $('usort'); if (so) so.onchange = function () { st.sort = so.value; st.offset = 0; remember('people/users', st, ['f', 'sort']); paint(); };
        if (actList) tenure(); else J('/api/auth/admin?limit=300&offset=0&sort=seen&f=active&_=' + Date.now()).then(function (a) { actList = (a && a.users) || []; tenure(); }).catch(function () { var b = $('uTenure'); if (b) b.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">unavailable</div>'; });
        var p = $('uprev'); if (p) p.onclick = function () { st.offset = Math.max(0, st.offset - st.limit); paint(); };
        var nx = $('unext'); if (nx) nx.onclick = function () { st.offset += st.limit; paint(); };
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load users'); });
    }
    if (arg && decodeURIComponent(arg).indexOf('$money=') === 0) { var mq = decodeURIComponent(arg).slice(7); setTimeout(function () { moneyView(/^u:/.test(mq) ? mq : 'u:' + mq, mq); }, 300); } // deep link from the full profile page: #people/users/$money=<uid>
    else if (arg) st.q = decodeURIComponent(arg);
    paint();
  } };

  // ---------- HEALTH / OVERVIEW ----------
  VIEWS['health/overview'] = { render: function (el) {

        var al = r[0] || {}, h = r[1] || {}, m = r[2] || {}, us = r[3] || {}, at = r[4] || {}, hb = r[5] || {};
        var SEV = { red: '#ff8a80', warn: '#ffc371', info: '#9fc4ff' };

        var tiles = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile((h.coll && h.coll.state) || '?', 'collector', (h.coll && /LIVE|OK/.test(h.coll.state)) ? '' : 'red') + tile(h.ae && h.ae.ok ?           + '<div class="grid g4" style="margin-top:12px">' + tile(hb.cronAgeMin != null ?         // the last 24 hours of alarms on one line: a dot per alert, placed by hour, coloured by severity

        var tlC = card('Last 24 hours', tl + '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:4px">' + N(lg24.length) + ' alerts · <span style="color:#ff8a80">red</span> · <span style="color:#ffc371">warning</span> · <span style="color:#9fc4ff">info</span> · hover a dot for the text · a cluster = one incident, a steady drip = a noisy kind to snooze</div>', 'every Telegram alarm, on a timeline');
        var sysl = sys.length ? '<div>' + sys.map(function (a) { return '<div class="al ' + a.cls + '" style="margin:0 6px 6px 0">' + a.html + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="line up">Cron, backups, Telegram and the liquidation venues are all fine.</div>';
        var ks = al.kinds || [];
        var unacked = ks.filter(function (k) { return k.s !== 'info' && !(k.ack && k.ack >= k.last) && !k.snooze; });
        var alh = (unacked.length > 1 ? '<div class="tblwrap"><table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th></th><th>kind</th><th>last</th><th class="r">7d</th><th>state</th><th>last message</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>' + ks.map(function (k) { var stt = k.snooze ? '' : '<button class="btn sm" data-ak="' + esc(k.k) + '" data-op="ack">Ack</button> <button class="btn sm" data-ak="' + esc(k.k) + '" data-op="' + (k.snooze ? 'unsnooze' : 'snooze') + '">' + (k.snooze ? 'Unsnooze' : 'Snooze 24h') + '</button>') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>' + ((al.silent || []).length ?
