        var tabs = (us.tabs || []); var uh = tabs.length ? tabs.map(function (t) { return '<span style="display:inline-block;margin:0 10px 6px 0"><b class="mono" style="color:var(--ink)">' + esc(t.tab) + '</b> ' + N(t.n) + '</span>'; }).join('') : '<span class="meta">no opens recorded yet</span>';
        el.innerHTML = tiles + card('System', sysl, 'what the cron, backups, Telegram and the collector say right now') + tlC + card('Alerts', alh, 'per alarm kind · Ack = seen · Snooze = swallow repeats · same buttons as under each Telegram message') + card('Tab usage', '<div class="line">' + uh + '</div>', 'opens per tab, last 30 days · what you never open is a merge or kill candidate', '<a href="#health/perf">performance</a>');
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-ak]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { b.disabled = true; post('/api/admin/alerts', { op: b.getAttribute('data-op'), kind: b.getAttribute('data-ak'), hours: 24 }).then(paint); }; });
        var aa = $('ackAll'); if (aa) aa.onclick = function () { aa.disabled = true; Promise.all(unacked.map(function (k) { return post('/api/admin/alerts', { op: 'ack', kind: k.k }); })).then(function () { toast('acked ' + unacked.length); paint(); loadTop(); }); };
        stamp();
      });
    }
    paint(); every(60000, paint);
  } };

  // ---------- SETTINGS / SWITCHES ----------
  VIEWS['settings/switches'] = { render: function (el) {
    var SW = [['sessKv', 'Session cache', 'OFF = every session read hits the UserStore DO'], ['srvCandle', 'Server candle settle', 'OFF = the money path settles on the current price only'], ['botApi', 'Bot API', 'OFF = every /api/v1 bot call is refused'], ['xPost', 'X auto-poster', 'OFF = no scheduled posts'], ['alerts', 'Ops alerts', 'OFF = checkOpsAlerts pages nothing (the signal watchdog still does)'], ['brief', 'Morning brief', 'the daily Telegram summary'], ['guestNudge', 'Guest activation card', 'the small "create a free account" card guests see after a winning close, a liquidation or their third close · OFF = never shown (see Growth / Guests)']];
    function paint() {
      Promise.all([J('/api/admin/opscfg'), J('/api/reward/config').catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (r) { var c = r[0] || {}, rc = (r[1] && r[1].config) || null;
        var rows = SW.map(function (s) { var on = c[s[0]] !== false; return '<div class="sw"><div>' + s[1] + '<small>' + s[2] + '</small></div><button class="btn ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off') + '" data-sw="' + s[0] + '" data-to="' + (on ? '0' : '1') + '">' + (on ? 'ON' : 'OFF') + '</button></div>'; }).join('');
        var frows = rc ? [['enabled', 'Faucet claims', 'OFF = nobody can claim; the pause message from Reward config shows'], ['wdEnabled', 'Withdrawals', 'OFF = users cannot request a payout']].map(function (s) { var on = rc[s[0]] !== false; return '<div class="sw"><div>' + s[1] + '<small>' + s[2] + '</small></div><button class="btn ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off') + '" data-rw="' + s[0] + '" data-to="' + (on ? '0' : '1') + '">' + (on ? 'ON' : 'OFF') + '</button></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">reward config unavailable</div>';
        var nsup = !!window.Notification, nperm = nsup ? Notification.permission : 'none', non = !!store('notif') && nperm === 'granted';
        var nrow = '<div class="sw"><div>Desktop notifications<small>' + (nsup ? 'a system notification when a new red or amber item appears while this tab is open (withdrawal request, ticket, alarm) · ' + (nperm === 'denied' ? 'blocked in the browser - allow notifications for marginpad.io first' : nperm === 'granted' ? 'browser permission granted' : 'the browser asks once') : 'this browser has no Notification API') + '</small></div><button class="btn ' + (non ? 'on' : 'off') + '" id="notifBtn"' + (nsup && nperm !== 'denied' ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + (non ? 'ON' : 'OFF') + '</button></div>'
          + '<div class="sw"><div>Times<small>every stamp on the dashboard in UTC (the server, crons and seasons) or in this computer\'s zone · key <b>u</b></small></div><button class="btn on" id="tzBtn2">' + (TZ === 'utc' ? 'UTC' : 'local') + '</button></div>';
        el.innerHTML = card('Kill switches', rows, 'ops:cfg · applies instantly, no deploy · every flip is confirmed') + card('Money switches', frows, 'rwd:cfg · every change also pings Telegram (config audit)') + card('This browser', nrow, 'stored here, not on the server') + card('More', '<div class="line">Claim economics, anti-abuse limits, pause message and site announcement live in <a href="#settings/rewards">Reward config</a>. Kill-switch reference: <span class="mono">sessKv</span>, <span class="mono">srvCandle</span>, <span class="mono">botApi</span>.</div>');
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-sw]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var k = b.getAttribute('data-sw'), to = b.getAttribute('data-to') === '1'; if (!confirm((to ? 'Turn ON ' : 'Turn OFF ') + k + '?')) return; b.disabled = true; var body = {}; body[k] = to; post('/api/admin/opscfg', body).then(function () { toast(k + (to ? ' ON' : ' OFF')); paint(); }); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-rw]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var k = b.getAttribute('data-rw'), to = b.getAttribute('data-to') === '1'; if (!confirm((to ? 'Turn ON ' : 'Turn OFF ') + k + '? (audited on Telegram)')) return; b.disabled = true; var body = {}; body[k] = to; post('/api/reward/config', body).then(function (j) { toast(j && j.ok !== false ? k + (to ? ' ON' : ' OFF') : 'failed'); paint(); }); }; });
        var nb = $('notifBtn'); if (nb) nb.onclick = function () { if (non) { store('notif', false); toast('desktop notifications off'); paint(); return; } var go = function (p) { if (p === 'granted') { store('notif', true); toast('desktop notifications on'); try { new Notification('MarginPad ops', { body: 'Notifications are on. You will hear about new tickets, payouts and alarms while this tab is open.' }); } catch (e) {} } else toast('permission not granted'); paint(); }; try { var pr = Notification.requestPermission(go); if (pr && pr.then) pr.then(go); } catch (e) { toast('not available'); } };
        var tb2 = $('tzBtn2'); if (tb2) tb2.onclick = function () { setTz(TZ === 'utc' ? 'local' : 'utc'); };
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load'); });
    }
    paint();
  } };

  // ---------- shared renderers for the read-mostly views ----------
  // rows[].html (already-safe markup, e.g. a flag) wins over the escaped rows[lk] label
  function bars(rows, lk, vk, fmt, max) { if (!rows || !rows.length) return '<div class="empty">nothing recorded</div>'; var mx = max || Math.max.apply(null, rows.map(function (r) { return +r[vk] || 0; }).concat([1])); return '<div class="hb">' + rows.map(function (r) { var v = +r[vk] || 0; var lbl = r.html || esc(r[lk] == null || r[lk] === '' ? '(unknown)' : r[lk]); return '<div class="hb-r"><span class="hb-l" title="' + (r.html ? '' : esc(r[lk])) + '">' + lbl + '</span><span class="hb-t"><i style="width:' + Math.max(2, v / mx * 100).toFixed(1) + '%"></i></span><span class="hb-v">' + (fmt ? fmt(v, r) : N(v)) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }
  function daysAgg(rows, dk, vk, n) { var m = {}; rows.forEach(function (r) { var d = String(r[dk] || '').slice(0, 10); m[d] = (m[d] || 0) + (+r[vk] || 0); }); var out = []; for (var i = (n || 14) - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var d = new Date(Date.now() - i * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10); out.push({ day: d, v: m[d] || 0 }); } return out; }
  function tbl(cols, rows, empty) { if (!rows || !rows.length) return '<div class="empty">' + (empty || 'nothing here') + '</div>'; return '<div class="tblwrap"><table class="tbl"><thead><tr>' + cols.map(function (c) { return '<th class="' + (c.r ? 'r' : '') + '">' + c.h + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + rows.map(function (r) { return '<tr>' + cols.map(function (c) { return '<td class="' + (c.r ? 'r ' : '') + (c.mono ? 'mono ' : '') + (c.grow ? 'grow' : '') + '">' + c.f(r) + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>'; }
  function userLink(u, e) { var name = u || (e || '').split('@')[0]; return name ? '<a href="/api/admin/user?' + (u ? 'u=' + encodeURIComponent(u) : 'email=' + encodeURIComponent(e)) + '" target="_blank">@' + esc(name) + '</a>' : '<span class="meta">visitor</span>'; }
  // opts.url = one endpoint → html(d); opts.urls = several → html(d0, d1, ...) where the extra ones fail soft (null)
  function simple(key, opts) { VIEWS[key] = { render: function (el) { function paint() { var us = opts.urls || [opts.url]; Promise.all(us.map(function (u, i) { var p = J(u); return i ? p.catch(function () { return null; }) : p; })).then(function (r) { el.innerHTML = opts.html.apply(null, [r[0] || {}].concat(r.slice(1))); if (opts.wire) opts.wire(el, r[0] || {}, paint); stamp(); }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load'); }); } paint(); if (opts.every) every(opts.every, paint); } }; }

  // ---------- MONEY / PREMIUM DESK ----------
  // Members first (who pays, who is about to lapse, who is not using it), the trading detail folded away.
  simple('money/premium', { urls: ['/api/admin/premdesk', '/api/admin/money'], every: 60000, html: function (d, mo) {
    var us = d.users || [], op = d.open || [], cl = d.closed || []; var now = Date.now(); mo = mo || {};
    var paid = us.filter(function (u) { return !u.founder; }), exp7 = us.filter(function (u) { return u.premUntil && u.premUntil < now + 7 * 86400000 && u.premUntil > now; }), idle = us.filter(function (u) { return now - (u.lastSeen || 0) > 7 * 86400000; });
    var realized = cl.reduce(function (s, t) { return s + (+t.pnl || 0); }, 0), liqs = cl.filter(function (t) { return t.liq === true || t.liq === 1; }).length;
    var byWho = {}; op.forEach(function (t) { byWho[t.who] = (byWho[t.who] || 0) + 1; });
    // the subscription math: $3.99 × paying members (column grants + IPN, founders excluded) against the faucet's run rate
    var PRICE = 3.99, mrr = paid.length * PRICE, faucet30 = (+mo.dispensed7dUsd || 0) / 7 * 30, cover = faucet30 > 0 ? Math.round(mrr / faucet30 * 100) : null, risk = exp7.filter(function (u) { return !u.founder; }).length * PRICE;
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(us.length), 'premium members', '', N(paid.length) + ' paying · ' + N(us.length - paid.length) + ' founders') + tile(N(exp7.length), 'expiring within 7 days', exp7.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', exp7.length ? exp7.map(function (u) { return '@' + esc(u.username); }).join(', ') : 'nobody lapses this week') + tile(N(idle.length), 'not seen for 7+ days', idle.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', 'paying, not using') + tile((realized >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + USD(Math.abs(realized), 0), 'realized P&amp;L, last ' + cl.length + ' closes', realized >= 0 ? '' : 'red', N(liqs) + ' liq · paper') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="grid g4" style="margin-top:12px">' + tile(USD(mrr), 'MRR', mrr ? '' : 'dim', N(paid.length) + ' × $3.99 · ' + USD(mrr * 12, 0) + ' a year') + tile(cover != null ? cover + '%' : '—', 'of the faucet covered', cover != null && cover >= 100 ? '' : 'dim', 'MRR vs faucet at the 7-day run rate (' + USD(faucet30, 0) + ' / 30d)') + tile(USD(risk), 'MRR at risk this week', risk ? 'amber' : 'dim', 'paying members lapsing within 7 days') + tile(mo.premiumSubs != null ? N(mo.premiumSubs) : '—', 'paid subscriptions on record', 'dim', mo.premiumMrrUsd != null ? 'ledger MRR ' + USD(mo.premiumMrrUsd) : '') + '</div>'
      + card('Members', tbl([{ h: 'member', f: function (u) { return userLink(u.username, u.email) + (u.founder ? ' <span class="badge lime">FOUNDER</span>' : '') + (u.premUntil && u.premUntil < now + 7 * 86400000 && u.premUntil > now ? ' <span class="badge amber">LAPSES ' + dt(u.premUntil).slice(5, 10) + '</span>' : ''); } }, { h: 'where', f: function (u) { return flag(u.cc); } }, { h: 'until', mono: true, f: function (u) { return u.founder ? 'forever' : (u.premUntil ? dt(u.premUntil).slice(0, 10) : '—'); } }, { h: 'xp', r: true, mono: true, f: function (u) { return N(u.xp); } }, { h: 'open', r: true, mono: true, f: function (u) { return N(u.openN); } }, { h: 'closed', r: true, mono: true, f: function (u) { return N(u.closedN); } }, { h: 'seen', mono: true, f: function (u) { return '<span class="' + (now - (u.lastSeen || 0) > 7 * 86400000 ? 'dn' : '') + '">' + ago(u.lastSeen) + ' ago</span>'; } }], us.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (b.lastSeen || 0) - (a.lastSeen || 0); })), 'click a member for the card · amber = lapses within 7 days')
      + fold('Open positions', tbl([{ h: 'who', f: function (t) { return userLink(t.who); } }, { h: 'market', mono: true, f: function (t) { return esc(t.sym) + ' <span class="' + (t.side === 'short' ? 'dn' : 'up') + '">' + esc(t.side) + '</span> ' + t.lev + 'x'; } }, { h: 'margin', r: true, mono: true, f: function (t) { return USD(t.margin, 0); } }, { h: 'entry', r: true, mono: true, f: function (t) { return N(+(+t.entry).toPrecision(6)); } }, { h: 'liq', r: true, mono: true, f: function (t) { return t.liq ? N(+(+t.liq).toPrecision(6)) : '—'; } }, { h: 'opened', mono: true, f: function (t) { return ago(t.ts) + ' ago'; } }], op.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.ts - a.ts; }), 'no open positions'), op.length + ' live · ' + Object.keys(byWho).length + ' members trading right now', false)
      + fold('Recent closes', tbl([{ h: 'who', f: function (t) { return userLink(t.who); } }, { h: 'market', mono: true, f: function (t) { return esc(t.sym) + ' ' + esc(t.side) + ' ' + t.lev + 'x'; } }, { h: 'margin', r: true, mono: true, f: function (t) { return USD(t.margin, 0); } }, { h: 'P&amp;L', r: true, mono: true, f: function (t) { return '<span class="' + ((+t.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + ((+t.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + USD(Math.abs(+t.pnl || 0)) + '</span>' + (t.liq === true || t.liq === 1 ? ' <span class="badge red">LIQ</span>' : ''); } }, { h: 'closed', mono: true, f: function (t) { return ago(t.ts) + ' ago'; } }], cl.slice(0, 60)), 'last ' + Math.min(60, cl.length) + ' of ' + cl.length, false);
  } });

  // ---------- MONEY / REVENUE ----------
  simple('money/revenue', { urls: ['/api/admin/revenue', '/api/admin/funnel'], every: 120000, html: function (d, fn) {
    var usd = +d.usdPerClick || 0.45, days = daysAgg(d.byDayEx || [], 'd', 'n', 14), t14 = days.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.v; }, 0), t7 = days.slice(7).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.v; }, 0), tPrev7 = t14 - t7, tToday = days[13].v, avg7 = days.slice(6, 13).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.v; }, 0) / 7;
    // $ per 1,000 visitors: the one number that tells whether traffic quality moved, not just volume
    var sn = ((fn && fn.snaps) || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.day < b.day ? -1 : 1; }); var uvOf = function (from, to) { return sn.slice(from, to).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.uv || 0); }, 0); }; var uv7 = uvOf(-7), uvP = uvOf(-14, -7); var rpm = uv7 ? t7 * usd / uv7 * 1000 : null, rpmP = uvP ? tPrev7 * usd / uvP * 1000 : null; var ctr7 = uv7 ? t7 / uv7 * 100 : null, ctrP = uvP ? tPrev7 / uvP * 100 : null;
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(tToday), 'money clicks today', tToday ? '' : 'dim', dl(tToday, avg7) + (avg7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs 7d avg</span>' : ''), mini(days, 'v'), 'data-drill="affiliate"') + tile(N(t7), 'clicks 7 days', 'dim', '~' + USD(t7 * usd, 0) + dl(t7, tPrev7) + (tPrev7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs prior 7d</span>' : '')) + tile(rpm != null ? USD(rpm) : '—', 'est. $ per 1,000 visitors', 'dim', (rpmP != null ? 'prior 7d ' + USD(rpmP) + dl(rpm, rpmP) : '') + (uv7 ? ' · ' + N(uv7) + ' uv in 7d' : '')) + tile(ctr7 != null ? ctr7.toFixed(2) + '%' : '—', 'visitors who click an exchange', 'dim', ctrP != null ? 'prior 7d ' + ctrP.toFixed(2) + '%' + dl(ctr7 * 100, ctrP * 100) : '') + '</div>'
      + card('Clicks per day', spark(days, 'v') + '<div class="line" style="margin-top:6px">last 14 days · est. $ = clicks × ~' + USD(usd) + ', NOT real commission</div>')
      + '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>By exchange <span>30 days</span></h2>' + bars(d.byEx || [], 'ex', 'n', function (v) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">~' + USD(v * usd, 0) + '</small>'; }) + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>By page <span>which page drives the clicks</span></h2>' + bars(d.byPage || [], 'path', 'n') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>By source <span>CTR = share of that source&#39;s pageviews that clicked out</span></h2>' + bars(d.bySrc || [], 'src', 'clicks', function (v, r) { return N(v) + (r.ctr != null ? ' <small class="meta">' + r.ctr + '% of ' + N(r.pv) + ' pv</small>' : ''); }) + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>By country <span>30 days</span></h2>' + bars((d.byCc || []).filter(function (x) { return x.cc; }).slice(0, 12).map(function (x) { return { html: flag(x.cc), n: x.n }; }), 'html', 'n') + '</div></div>'
      + card('Latest click-outs', tbl([{ h: 'when', mono: true, f: function (c) { return ago(c.ts) + ' ago'; } }, { h: 'who', f: function (c) { return userLink(c.u); } }, { h: 'exchange', mono: true, f: function (c) { return esc(String(c.e || '?').toUpperCase()); } }, { h: 'from page', mono: true, grow: true, f: function (c) { return esc(c.p || '/'); } }, { h: 'source', f: function (c) { return esc(c.src || 'direct'); } }, { h: 'where', f: function (c) { return flag(c.cc); } }], d.clicks || []), 'ring buffer, most recent first');
  } });

  // ---------- MONEY / AFFILIATE ----------
  simple('money/affiliate', { url: '/api/admin/affiliate', every: 120000, html: function (d) {
    var t = d.totals || {}, ser = d.series || []; var sum = function (k, a, b) { return ser.slice(a, b).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x[k] || 0); }, 0); }; var n = ser.length, c7 = sum('clicks', n - 7, n), cP = sum('clicks', n - 14, n - 7), s7 = sum('signups', n - 7, n), sP = sum('signups', n - 14, n - 7);
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(t.referrers), 'people sharing', 'dim') + tile(N(t.clicks), 'link clicks', '', N(c7) + ' in 7d' + dl(c7, cP) + (cP >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs prior 7d</span>' : ''), mini(ser, 'clicks')) + tile(N(t.invited), 'sign-ups referred', 'dim', (t.clicks ? Math.round(t.invited / t.clicks * 100) + '% of clicks · ' : '') + N(s7) + ' in 7d' + dl(s7, sP), mini(ser, 'signups')) + tile(N(t.qualified), 'active (3+ trades)', 'dim', t.invited ? Math.round(t.qualified / t.invited * 100) + '% of sign-ups' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="grid g4" style="margin-top:12px">' + tile(USD(d.referralUsd), 'paid to affiliates', 'dim') + tile(N(t.pending), 'pending activation', t.pending ? 'amber' : 'dim') + tile(ser.length ? N(ser[ser.length - 1].clicks) : '—', 'clicks today', 'dim', dl(ser.length ? ser[ser.length - 1].clicks : 0, c7 / 7) + (c7 / 7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs 7d avg</span>' : '')) + tile(ser.length ? N(ser[ser.length - 1].signups) : '—', 'sign-ups today', 'dim', dl(ser.length ? ser[ser.length - 1].signups : 0, s7 / 7) + (s7 / 7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs 7d avg</span>' : '')) + '</div>'
      + card('Last 14 days', spark(ser, 'clicks') + '<div class="line" style="margin-top:6px">clicks · ' + spark(ser, 'signups').replace('class="spark"', 'class="spark" style="height:26px"') + ' sign-ups</div>')
      + '<div class="grid g3" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Traffic sources</h2>' + bars(d.sources || [], 'k', 'n') + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Campaigns <span>&amp;c= tags</span></h2>' + bars(d.campaigns || [], 'k', 'n') + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Referred countries</h2>' + bars((d.countries || []).map(function (x) { return { html: flag(x.k), n: x.n }; }), 'html', 'n') + '</div></div>'
      + card('Affiliates', tbl([{ h: 'affiliate', f: function (a) { return userLink(a.username, a.email); } }, { h: 'clicks', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return N(a.clicks); } }, { h: 'sign-ups', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return N(a.invited); } }, { h: 'active', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return N(a.qualified); } }, { h: 'pending', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return N(a.pending); } }, { h: 'conv', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return (a.conv != null ? Math.round(+a.conv * (a.conv <= 1 ? 100 : 1)) + '%' : '—'); } }, { h: 'last', mono: true, f: function (a) { return a.lastTs ? ago(a.lastTs) + ' ago' : '—'; } }], (d.affiliates || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (b.clicks || 0) - (a.clicks || 0); })), 'click an affiliate for the full profile')
      + card('Recent activity', (d.recent || []).slice(0, 30).map(function (r) { return '<div class="row"><span class="who">' + userLink(r.who) + '</span><span class="grow">' + esc(r.t || '') + (r.inv ? ' · ' + esc(r.inv) : '') + ' ' + flag(r.cc) + '</span><span class="meta">' + ago(r.ts) + ' ago</span></div>'; }).join('') || '<div class="empty">quiet</div>');
  } });

  // ---------- MONEY / SHOP ----------
  // /api/admin/shop: cosmetics (who got what, how, from whom), the DURABLE cash ledger (buys + refunds, consumables included),
  // AE history for cash purchases older than the durable log (buyer unknown), KV per-day counters (cash / XP / ticks, 60 days).
  VIEWS['money/shop'] = { render: function (el) {
    var S = { f: 'all', q: '' }, D = null;
    var SRCN = { usd: 'cash', xp: 'XP', ticks: 'Ticks', gift: 'gift', earn: 'earned', streak: 'streak', wk: 'season winner', grant: 'granted', refund: 'refund' };
    var KINDN = { frame: 'frame', bg: 'background', t: 'ticket skin', c: 'consumable', refund: 'refund' };
    var FILT = [['all', 'All'], ['usd', 'Cash'], ['c', 'Consumables'], ['xp', 'XP'], ['ticks', 'Ticks'], ['gift', 'Gifts'], ['earn', 'Earned'], ['streak', 'Streak'], ['wk', 'Season winners'], ['refund', 'Refunds']];
    function amount(f) { if (f.cents) return '<span class="mono ' + (f.cents < 0 ? 'dn' : 'up') + '">' + (f.cents < 0 ? '-' : '') + USD(Math.abs(f.cents) / 100) + '</span>'; if (f.xp) return '<span class="mono">' + N(f.xp) + ' XP</span>'; if (f.ticks) return '<span class="mono">' + N(f.ticks) + ' ticks</span>'; return '<span class="meta">free</span>'; }
    function who(f) { if (f.un) return userLink(f.un); if (f.uid) return '<span class="mono meta">' + esc(f.uid.slice(0, 8)) + '</span>'; return '<span class="meta">unknown buyer</span>' + (f.cc ? ' ' + flag(f.cc) : ''); }
    function rows() { var q = S.q; return (D.feed || []).filter(function (f) { if (S.f === 'c') { if (f.kind !== 'c') return false; } else if (S.f !== 'all' && f.src !== S.f) return false; if (q && ((f.un || '') + ' ' + (f.name || '') + ' ' + (f.item || '') + ' ' + (f.via || '')).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false; return true; }); }
    function paintList() { var r = rows(); var box = $('shList'); if (!box) return; $('shCount').textContent = N(r.length) + ' of ' + N((D.feed || []).length);
      box.innerHTML = tbl([{ h: 'when', mono: true, f: function (f) { return dt(f.ts); } }, { h: 'who', f: who }, { h: 'item', grow: true, f: function (f) { return '<b>' + esc(f.name) + '</b> ' + (f.tier ? '<span class="badge ' + (f.tier === 'legendary' ? 'amber' : f.tier === 'epic' ? 'lime' : '') + '">' + esc(f.tier) + '</span> ' : '') + '<span class="meta">' + esc(KINDN[f.kind] || f.kind) + '</span>'; } }, { h: 'how', f: function (f) { return '<span class="badge ' + (f.src === 'usd' ? 'ok' : f.src === 'refund' ? 'red' : f.src === 'gift' ? 'lime' : '') + '">' + esc(SRCN[f.src] || f.src) + '</span>' + (f.via ? ' <span class="meta">from ' + userLink(f.via) + '</span>' : '') + (f.hist ? ' <span class="meta" title="the ledger kept only 200 live rows before 2026-09-04; this row comes from Analytics Engine and has no buyer">history</span>' : ''); } }, { h: 'amount', r: true, f: amount }], r.slice(0, 300), 'nothing matches'); }
    function paint() {
      J('/api/admin/shop').then(function (d) {
        D = d; var f30 = (d.feed || []).filter(function (f) { return Date.now() - f.ts < 30 * 86400000; });
        var cash30 = d.cash30 || 0, cons = (d.feed || []).filter(function (f) { return f.kind === 'c'; });
        var kv = (d.kvByDay || []).slice().reverse();
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(USD(cash30 / 100), 'cash 30 days', cash30 ? '' : 'dim', dl(cash30, d.cashPrev30) + ' <span class="meta">vs prior 30d</span>', mini(kv.slice(-30), 'cents')) + tile(N(f30.filter(function (f) { return f.cents > 0; }).length), 'cash purchases 30d', 'dim', N(cons.filter(function (f) { return Date.now() - f.ts < 30 * 86400000; }).length) + ' consumables') + tile(N(d.xp30), 'XP spent 30 days', 'dim', dl(d.xp30, d.xpPrev30) + ' <span class="meta">vs prior 30d</span>', mini(kv.slice(-30), 'xp')) + tile(N(d.ticks30), 'ticks spent 30 days', 'dim', dl(d.ticks30, d.ticksPrev30) + ' <span class="meta">vs prior 30d</span>', mini(kv.slice(-30), 'ticks'))
          + tile(N(d.grants), 'items owned', 'dim', N(d.buyers) + ' owners') + tile(USD((d.durableCents || 0) / 100), 'cash on the durable ledger', 'dim', d.durableSince ? 'since ' + dt(d.durableSince).slice(0, 10) : 'starts with the next purchase') + tile(N(d.refunds || 0), 'refunds', d.refunds ? 'amber' : 'dim', 'failed grants, money returned') + tile(N((d.feed || []).filter(function (f) { return f.src === 'gift'; }).length), 'gifts', 'dim') + '</div>'
          + card('Every purchase and grant', '<div class="bar"><div class="chips" style="margin:0">' + FILT.map(function (x) { return '<span class="chip ' + (S.f === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-f="' + x[0] + '">' + x[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><input class="in" id="shq" placeholder="search user, item, gifter" value="' + esc(S.q) + '"></div><div id="shList"></div>', '<span id="shCount"></span>', 'newest first · cash rows since 2026-09-04 are the durable ledger; older cash rows are Analytics Engine history')
          + '<div class="grid g2">' + card('By item', bars((d.items || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 14).map(function (i) { return { l: i.name + (i.tier ? ' · ' + i.tier : '') + ' (' + (SRCN[i.src] || i.src) + ')', n: i.n }; }), 'l', 'n'), 'all time, per source') + card('Top spenders', bars((d.topSpenders || []).map(function (s) { return { html: s.un ? userLink(s.un) : '<span class="mono meta">' + esc(String(s.uid).slice(0, 8)) + '</span>', n: s.cents }; }), 'html', 'n', function (v) { return USD(v / 100); }), 'cash, from the rows above') + '</div>'
          + fold('Catalogue', tbl([{ h: 'item', f: function (c) { return '<b>' + esc(c.name) + '</b> <span class="mono meta">' + esc(c.id) + '</span>'; } }, { h: 'kind', f: function (c) { return esc(KINDN[c.kind] || c.kind); } }, { h: 'tier', f: function (c) { return esc(c.tier || ''); } }, { h: 'price', r: true, mono: true, f: function (c) { return [c.cents ? USD(c.cents / 100) : '', c.xp ? N(c.xp) + ' XP' : '', c.ticks ? N(c.ticks) + ' ticks' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' / ') || (c.earn ? 'earn: ' + esc(c.earn) : 'free'); } }, { h: 'sold', r: true, mono: true, f: function (c) { return N((d.items || []).filter(function (i) { return i.item === c.id; }).reduce(function (s, i) { return s + i.n; }, 0)); } }, { h: 'until', mono: true, f: function (c) { return esc(c.until || ''); } }], d.catalogue || []), (d.catalogue || []).length + ' items');
        paintList();
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { S.f = c.getAttribute('data-f'); el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.toggle('on', x === c); }); paintList(); }; });
        var q = $('shq'); if (q) { var tm; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(tm); tm = setTimeout(function () { S.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paintList(); }, 250); }; }
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load the shop'); });
    }
    paint(); every(120000, paint);
  } };

  // ---------- PEOPLE / RETENTION ----------
  simple('people/retention', { urls: ['/api/admin/retention', '/api/auth/admin?limit=12&offset=0&sort=seen&f=churn'], every: 300000, html: function (d, ch) {
    // heat cells: the greener, the more came back; the weighted average row is the number to quote
    var pc = function (v) { if (v == null) return '<span class="meta">—</span>'; var a = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v / 60)); return '<span class="heat" style="background:rgba(46,189,133,' + (0.08 + a * 0.55).toFixed(2) + ')">' + Math.round(v) + '%</span>'; };
    var co = (d.cohorts || []); var wavg = function (k) { var n = 0, s = 0; co.forEach(function (c) { if (c[k] != null && c.size) { n += c.size; s += c[k] * c.size; } }); return n ? s / n : null; };
    var rows = co.slice(); if (co.length) rows.push({ weekStart: 'all', size: co.reduce(function (s, c) { return s + (+c.size || 0); }, 0), d1: wavg('d1'), d7: wavg('d7'), d30: wavg('d30'), _avg: true });
    var wb = (ch && ch.users) || [];
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(d.dau), 'active today (DAU)', '', dl(d.dau, d.wau / 7) + (d.wau / 7 >= 5 ? ' <span class="meta">vs WAU/7</span>' : ''), '', 'data-drill="returning"') + tile(N(d.wau), 'active 7 days (WAU)', 'dim', dl(d.wau, d.mau / 30 * 7) + (d.mau >= 30 ? ' <span class="meta">vs MAU pace</span>' : '')) + tile(N(d.mau), 'active 30 days (MAU)', 'dim') + tile(d.wau ? Math.round(d.dau / d.wau * 100) + '%' : '—', 'stickiness DAU/WAU', 'dim', d.mau ? Math.round(d.wau / d.mau * 100) + '% WAU/MAU' : '') + '</div>'
      + card('Weekly cohorts', tbl([{ h: 'week of', mono: true, f: function (c) { return c._avg ?       + card('Win-back list', wb.length ? wb.map(function (u) { return '<div class="row"><span class="who">' + userLink(u.username, u.email) + '</span><span class="grow meta">' + flag(u.cc) + ' ' + N(u.trades) + ' trades · ' + N(u.pv) + ' pv · joined ' + ago(u.created) + ' ago</span><span class="meta dn">gone ' + agoT(u.last_seen) + '</span>' + (u.email ? '<a class="btn sm" href="#inbox/mail/' + encodeURIComponent(u.email) + '">Email</a>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="line up">nobody who traded went quiet in the last 7-30 days</div>', 'traded before, not seen for 7-30 days, most recent first · one personal mail brings back more than any banner')
      + '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Power users <span>most active, seen this week</span></h2>' + (d.power || []).map(function (u) { return '<div class="row"><span class="who">' + userLink(u.username, u.email) + '</span><span class="grow meta">' + flag(u.cc) + ' ' + N(u.pv) + ' pv · ' + N(u.trades) + ' trades · ' + N(u.logins) + ' logins</span><span class="meta">seen ' + ago(u.last_seen) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Slipping away <span>were engaged, not seen in 14-60 days</span></h2>' + (d.churned || []).map(function (u) { return '<div class="row"><span class="who">' + userLink(u.username, u.email) + '</span><span class="grow meta">' + flag(u.cc) + ' ' + N(u.pv) + ' pv · ' + N(u.trades) + ' trades</span><span class="meta dn">gone ' + ago(u.last_seen) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>';

  // ---------- PEOPLE / MULTI-ACCOUNT ----------
  // Compact by design (owner 2026-09-04: this was a wall of scroll): what needs a look first, then every cluster as ONE line
  // that opens on click, the sign-in log folded away with its own search.
  VIEWS['people/security'] = { render: function (el) {
    var S = { q: '' };
    function paint() {
      J('/api/admin/security').then(function (d) {
        var did = d.didClusters || [], ip = d.ipClusters || [], em = d.emailClusters || [], log = d.authlog || [];
        var hot = did.concat(ip).filter(function (c) { return c.recent > 0; });
        var mixed = did.concat(ip).filter(function (c) { return (c.members || []).some(function (m) { return m.banned; }) && (c.members || []).some(function (m) { return !m.banned; }); });
        var claimers = did.filter(function (c) { return (c.members || []).reduce(function (s, m) { return s + (+m.claims || 0); }, 0) >= 5; });
        var member = function (m) { return '<span style="display:inline-block;margin:0 10px 4px 0">' + userLink(m.username, m.email) + (m.banned ? ' <span class="badge red">BANNED</span>' : '') + ' <span class="meta">' + flag(m.cc) + ' ' + N(m.trades) + 't · ' + N(m.claims) + 'c · ' + USD(m.balanceUsd) + (m.vpn ? ' · VPN' : '') + ' · ' + ago(m.created) + '</span></span>'; };
        var cluster = function (c, kind) { var ms = c.members || []; var cl = ms.reduce(function (s, m) { return s + (+m.claims || 0); }, 0), bal = ms.reduce(function (s, m) { return s + (+m.balanceUsd || 0); }, 0), bn = ms.filter(function (m) { return m.banned; }).length;
          return '<details class="cl' + (c.recent ? ' hot' : '') + '"><summary><b>' + c.count + '</b> on the same ' + kind + (c.key && kind !== 'device' ? ' <span class="mono">' + esc(String(c.key).slice(0, 18)) + '</span>' : '') + ' ' + flag(c.cc) + (c.vpn ? ' <span class="badge">VPN</span>' : '') + (c.recent ? ' <span class="badge amber">' + c.recent + ' in the last hour</span>' : '') + (bn ? ' <span class="badge red">' + bn + ' banned</span>' : '') + '<span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + N(cl) + ' claims · ' + USD(bal) + ' · last ' + ago(c.lastTs) + ' ago</span></summary><div class="cl-b">' + ms.map(member).join('') + '</div></details>'; };
        var K = { signup: 'NEW', login: 'IN', logout: 'OUT' };
        var lg = log.filter(function (a) { return !S.q || ((a.u || '') + ' ' + (a.ip || '') + ' ' + (a.cc || '')).toLowerCase().indexOf(S.q) >= 0; });
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(did.length), 'same-device clusters', did.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', 'one person, several accounts') + tile(N(ip.length), 'same-IP clusters', 'dim', 'family, office or one person') + tile(N(hot.length), 'active in the last hour', hot.length ? 'red' : 'dim', 'clusters that just moved') + tile(N(d.vpnCount), 'VPN / datacenter users', 'dim', N(em.length) + ' look-alike emails') + '</div>'
          + card('Needs a look', (hot.length || mixed.length || claimers.length) ? '<div class="line" style="margin-bottom:6px;font-size:12px">' + (hot.length ? '<b>' + hot.length + '</b> cluster' + (hot.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' active in the last hour · ' : '') + (mixed.length ? '<b>' + mixed.length + '</b> with a banned member next to a live one · ' : '') + (claimers.length ? '<b>' + claimers.length + '</b> same-device cluster' + (claimers.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' with 5+ faucet claims' : '') + '</div>' + hot.concat(mixed, claimers).filter(function (c, i, a) { return a.indexOf(c) === i; }).slice(0, 8).map(function (c) { return cluster(c, c.kind === 'ip' ? 'IP' : 'device'); }).join('') : '<div class="line up">nothing moved in the last hour and no cluster mixes banned with live accounts</div>', 'the clusters worth opening first · click a row to see the accounts')
          + fold('Same device', did.map(function (c) { return cluster(c, 'device'); }).join('') || '<div class="empty">none</div>', did.length + ' clusters · one person, multiple accounts', false)
          + fold('Same IP', ip.map(function (c) { return cluster(c, 'IP'); }).join('') || '<div class="empty">none</div>', ip.length + ' clusters · can be a shared network', false)
          + fold('Look-alike emails', em.map(function (c) { return '<details class="cl"><summary><b>' + esc(c.key) + '</b>' + (c.exact ? ' <span class="badge red">SAME NORMALIZED ADDRESS</span>' : '') + '<span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + (c.members || []).length + ' accounts</span></summary><div class="cl-b">' + (c.members || []).map(member).join('') + '</div></details>'; }).join('') || '<div class="empty">none</div>', em.length + ' groups', false)
          + fold('Sign-in / sign-out log', '<div class="bar"><input class="in" id="seq" placeholder="filter by user, IP or country" value="' + esc(S.q) + '"></div><div id="seLog">' + tbl([{ h: '', f: function (a) { return '<span class="badge ' + (a.k === 'signup' ? 'lime' : a.k === 'logout' ? 'red' : 'ok') + '">' + (K[a.k] || esc(a.k)) + '</span>'; } }, { h: 'who', f: function (a) { return userLink(a.u); } }, { h: 'where', f: function (a) { return flag(a.cc) + ' <span class="mono meta">' + esc(a.ip || '') + '</span>'; } }, { h: 'device', mono: true, f: function (a) { return esc(String(a.did || '').slice(0, 8)) + (a.devN > 1 ? ' <span class="badge red">' + a.devN + ' accounts @ device</span>' : '') + (a.ipN > 1 ? ' <span class="badge amber">' + a.ipN + ' @ IP</span>' : ''); } }, { h: 'when', mono: true, f: function (a) { return ago(a.ts) + ' ago'; } }], lg.slice(0, 80)) + '</div>', log.length + ' events · logout then login of another account on the same device = the multi-account pattern', !!S.q);
        var q = $('seq'); if (q) { var tm; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(tm); tm = setTimeout(function () { S.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paint(); var q2 = $('seq'); if (q2) { q2.focus(); q2.value = S.q; } }, 300); }; }
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load'); });
    }
    paint(); every(120000, paint);
  } };

  // ---------- GROWTH / FUNNEL ----------
  simple('growth/funnel', { url: '/api/admin/funnel', every: 300000, html: function (d) {
    var sn = (d.snaps || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.day < b.day ? -1 : 1; }); var last = sn[sn.length - 1] || {};
    var trades = {}; (d.evByDay || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.ty === 'paper') { var k = String(e.d || '').slice(0, 10); trades[k] = (trades[k] || 0) + (+e.n || 0); } }); // 'paper' = an opened practice trade; a visitor opens many, so it is shown per visitor, never as a %
    var avg = function (k) { return sn.length >= 8 ? sn.slice(-8, -1).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x[k] || 0); }, 0) / 7 : 0; }; var tAvg = sn.length >= 8 ? sn.slice(-8, -1).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (trades[x.day] || 0); }, 0) / 7 : 0;
    // conversion, not volume: 7 full days vs the 7 before, so a traffic spike does not read as growth
    var full = sn.slice(0, -1); var w = function (arr, k) { return arr.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (k === 'tr' ? (trades[x.day] || 0) : (+x[k] || 0)); }, 0); }; var A = full.slice(-7), B = full.slice(-14, -7); var rate = function (arr, k) { var uv = w(arr, 'uv'); return uv ? w(arr, k) / uv * 100 : null; };
    var cv = [['visitor to trade', 'tr', 'x'], ['visitor to exchange click', 'aff', '%'], ['visitor to sign-up', 'signup', '%'], ['visitor to faucet claim', 'claim', '%']].map(function (c) { var a = rate(A, c[1]), b = rate(B, c[1]); var fmt = function (v) { return v == null ? '—' : c[2] === 'x' ? (v / 100).toFixed(2) + ' per visitor' : v.toFixed(2) + '%'; }; return tile(fmt(a), c[0], 'dim', b != null && a != null ? 'prior 7d ' + fmt(b) + dl(a * 100, b * 100) : ''); }).join('');
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(last.uv), 'visitors ' + (last.day || 'today'), '', (avg('uv') ? '7d avg ' + N(Math.round(avg('uv'))) : '') + dl(last.uv, avg('uv')), mini(sn, 'uv'), 'data-drill="visitors"') + tile(N(trades[last.day] || 0), 'practice trades', 'dim', (last.uv ? ((trades[last.day] || 0) / last.uv).toFixed(1) + ' per visitor' : '') + dl(trades[last.day] || 0, tAvg)) + tile(N(last.aff), 'exchange clicks', last.aff ? '' : 'dim', (last.uv ? Math.round((last.aff || 0) / last.uv * 100) + '% of uv' : '') + dl(last.aff, avg('aff')), mini(sn, 'aff'), 'data-drill="affiliate"') + tile(N(last.signup), 'sign-ups', 'dim', N(last.claim) + ' claims' + dl(last.signup, avg('signup')), mini(sn, 'signup'), 'data-drill="signups"') + '</div>'
      + card('Conversion, 7 full days vs the 7 before', '<div class="grid g4">' + cv + '</div>', 'rates per visitor · arrows compare the rate, so a traffic spike alone does not move them')
      + card('Daily funnel', tbl([{ h: 'day', mono: true, f: function (x) { return String(x.day).slice(5); } }, { h: 'uv', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return N(x.uv); } }, { h: 'pv', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return N(x.pv); } }, { h: 'trades', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return N(trades[x.day] || 0); } }, { h: 'clicks', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return '<span class="' + (x.aff ? '' : 'meta') + '">' + N(x.aff) + '</span>'; } }, { h: 'claims', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return N(x.claim); } }, { h: 'sign-ups', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return N(x.signup); } }, { h: 'new / returning', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return N(x.nw) + ' / ' + N(x.rt); } }, { h: 'trades / uv', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return x.uv ? ((trades[x.day] || 0) / x.uv).toFixed(1) : '—'; } }, { h: 'uv→click', r: true, mono: true, f: function (x) { return x.uv ? Math.round((x.aff || 0) / x.uv * 100) + '%' : '—'; } }], sn.slice().reverse()), 'visitors → practice trades → money clicks · uv/pv from daily snapshots, actions from Analytics Engine');
  } });

  // ---------- GROWTH / TRAFFIC & SEO ----------
  simple('growth/seo', { url: '/api/admin/seo', every: 300000, html: function (d) {
    var prev = {}; (d.pagesPrev || []).forEach(function (p) { prev[p.p] = p.n; }); var sprev = {}; (d.srcPrev || []).forEach(function (p) { sprev[p.s] = p.n; });
    var pv7 = (d.pages7 || []).reduce(function (s, p) { return s + (+p.n || 0); }, 0), pvP = (d.pagesPrev || []).reduce(function (s, p) { return s + (+p.n || 0); }, 0);
    var delta = function (n, p) { if (!p) return '<span class="badge lime">NEW</span>'; var x = Math.round((n - p) / p * 100); return '<span class="' + (x >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + ' mono">' + (x >= 0 ? '+' : '') + x + '%</span>'; };
    var byDay = (d.pvByDay || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1; });
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(pv7), 'pageviews 7d', '', pvP ? delta(pv7, pvP) + ' w/w' : '') + tile((d.pages7 || [])[0] ? esc(d.pages7[0].p) : '—', 'top page 7d', 'dim') + tile((d.src7 || [])[0] ? esc(d.src7[0].s) : '—', 'top source 7d', 'dim') + tile(N((d.clicksPage7 || []).reduce(function (s, p) { return s + (+p.n || 0); }, 0)), 'exchange clicks 7d', 'dim') + '</div>'
      + card('Pageviews per day', spark(byDay.map(function (x) { return { day: x.d, v: x.n }; }), 'v') + '<div class="line" style="margin-top:6px">last ' + byDay.length + ' days · Analytics Engine</div>')
      + (function () { var mv = (d.pages7 || []).map(function (p) { var pr = prev[p.p] || 0; return { p: p.p, n: +p.n || 0, pr: pr, d: (+p.n || 0) - pr }; }).filter(function (x) { return x.n >= 20 || x.pr >= 20; }); var up = mv.filter(function (x) { return x.d > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.d - a.d; }).slice(0, 6), dn = mv.filter(function (x) { return x.d < 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.d - b.d; }).slice(0, 6); var row = function (x) { return '<div class="row"><span class="mono grow">' + esc(x.p) + '</span><span class="mono ' + (x.d > 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + (x.d > 0 ? '+' : '') + N(x.d) + '</span><span class="meta">' + N(x.pr) + ' to ' + N(x.n) + '</span></div>'; }; return '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Movers up <span>7d vs prior 7d · pages with 20+ pv</span></h2>' + (up.map(row).join('') || '<div class="empty">no page gained this week</div>') + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Movers down <span>where traffic went missing</span></h2>' + (dn.map(row).join('') || '<div class="empty">no page lost this week</div>') + '</div></div>'; })()
      + (function () { var cur = {}; (d.pages7 || []).forEach(function (p) { cur[p.p] = +p.n || 0; }); var np = (d.pages7 || []).filter(function (p) { return !prev[p.p] && (+p.n || 0) >= 5; }).slice(0, 8), lp = (d.pagesPrev || []).filter(function (p) { return (+p.n || 0) >= 20 && !cur[p.p]; }).slice(0, 8); var row = function (p, n, cls) { return '<div class="row"><span class="mono grow">' + esc(p) + '</span><span class="mono ' + cls + '">' + N(n) + ' pv</span></div>'; }; return '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>New this week <span>pages with views now and none the week before</span></h2>' + (np.map(function (p) { return row(p.p, p.n, 'up'); }).join('') || '<div class="empty">nothing new got traffic (5+ pv)</div>') + '</div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Gone quiet <span>had 20+ pv last week, none this week</span></h2>' + (lp.map(function (p) { return row(p.p, p.n, 'dn'); }).join('') || '<div class="empty">every page that had traffic still has some</div>') + '</div></div>'; })()
      + '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Top pages <span>7d vs prior 7d</span></h2>' + tbl([{ h: 'page', mono: true, grow: true, f: function (p) { return esc(p.p); } }, { h: 'pv', r: true, mono: true, f: function (p) { return N(p.n); } }, { h: 'w/w', r: true, f: function (p) { return delta(p.n, prev[p.p]); } }], (d.pages7 || []).slice(0, 30)) + '</div><div><div class="card" style="margin:0"><h2>Sources <span>7d</span></h2>' + tbl([{ h: 'source', grow: true, f: function (p) { return esc(p.s); } }, { h: 'pv', r: true, mono: true, f: function (p) { return N(p.n); } }, { h: 'w/w', r: true, f: function (p) { return delta(p.n, sprev[p.s]); } }], (d.src7 || []).slice(0, 15)) + '</div><div class="card"><h2>Pages that print money <span>exchange clicks by page · 7d</span></h2>' + bars(d.clicksPage7 || [], 'p', 'n') + '</div><div class="card"><h2>Countries <span>7d</span></h2>' + bars((d.cc7 || []).slice(0, 12).map(function (x) { return { html: flag(x.cc), n: x.n }; }), 'html', 'n') + '</div></div></div>';
  } });

  // ---------- GROWTH / GUESTS ----------
  // Who trades without an account and whether the activation card (mp-auth.js: first winning close, liquidation or third
  // close; max 3 times, 7 days apart; kill switch ops:cfg.guestNudge) turns them into accounts. Data: /api/admin/guests.
  simple('growth/guests', { url: '/api/admin/guests', every: 120000, html: function (d) {
    var days = []; for (var i = 13; i >= 0; i--) days.push(new Date(Date.now() - i * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10));
    var by = function (rows, key) { var m = {}; (rows || []).forEach(function (r) { var dd = String(r.d).slice(0, 10); (m[dd] = m[dd] || {})[r[key] || ''] = (m[dd][r[key] || ''] || 0) + (+r.n || 0); }); return m; };
    var tr = by(d.trades, 'who'), cl = by(d.closes, 'who'), nu = by(d.nudge, 'label'); var su = {}; (d.signups || []).forEach(function (r) { su[String(r.d).slice(0, 10)] = +r.n || 0; });
    var sum = function (m, k, from) { var s = 0; days.slice(from).forEach(function (dd) { s += ((m[dd] || {})[k] || 0); }); return s; };
    var g7 = sum(tr, 'guest', 7), u7 = sum(tr, 'user', 7), gP = sum(tr, 'guest', 0) - g7, uP = sum(tr, 'user', 0) - u7;
    var shown = 0, click = 0, dism = 0, conv = 0, tout = 0; days.forEach(function (dd) { var x = nu[dd] || {}; Object.keys(x).forEach(function (k) { if (k.indexOf('shown') === 0) shown += x[k]; else if (k === 'click') click += x[k]; else if (k === 'dismiss') dism += x[k]; else if (k === 'converted' || k === 'signup') conv += x[k]; else if (k === 'timeout') tout += x[k]; }); });
    var s14 = days.reduce(function (s, dd) { return s + (su[dd] || 0); }, 0);
    var guestsToday = Math.max(0, (d.uvToday || 0) - (d.activeToday || 0));
    return '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(d.guestsTradingNow), 'guests with an open position', d.guestsTradingNow ? '' : 'dim', N(d.signedInTradingNow) + ' signed-in traders') + tile(N(guestsToday), 'guests today', 'dim', 'visitors ' + N(d.uvToday) + ' minus ' + N(d.activeToday) + ' signed-in active · approximate') + tile(N(g7), 'guest trade opens 7d', g7 ? '' : 'dim', (u7 ? Math.round(g7 / (g7 + u7) * 100) + '% of all opens' : '') + dl(g7, gP), '', '') + tile(N(d.newToday), 'sign-ups today', d.newToday ? '' : 'dim', N(s14) + ' in 14 days' + dl(d.newToday, s14 / 14), '', 'data-drill="signups"') + '</div>'
      + card('Activation card funnel', '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(shown), 'shown', shown ? '' : 'dim', 'last 14 days') + tile(N(click), 'clicked', click ? '' : 'dim', shown ?       { h: 'away', r: true, mono: true, f: function (o) { return o.awayPct == null ? '—' : '<span class="' + (Math.abs(o.awayPct) <= 1 ? 'green' : '') + '">' + (o.awayPct >= 0 ? '+' : '') + o.awayPct.toFixed(2) + '%</span>'; } },
      { h: 'margin', r: true, mono: true, f: function (o) { return '$' + N(Math.round(+o.margin || 0)); } },
      { h: 'sl / tp', mono: true, f: function (o) { return (o.sl != null ? (+o.sl).toPrecision(6) : '—') + ' / ' + (o.tp != null ? (+o.tp).toPrecision(6) : '—'); } },
      { h: 'waiting', mono: true, f: function (o) { return ago(o.ts); } },

  } });

  simple('health/perf', { url: '/api/admin/perf', every: 30000, html: function (d) {

      + card('Latency by group', tbl([{ h: 'group', mono: true, f: function (r) { return esc(r.k); } }, { h: 'samples', r: true, mono: true, f: function (r) { return '<span class="' + (r.n < 30 ?   }, wire: function (el) {
    // day-over-day p95 per group (Analytics Engine): the only way to tell drift from a one-off before touching a budget
    J('/api/admin/perfhist?days=7').then(function (rows) { var box = $('perfTrend'); if (!box) return; rows = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : (rows && rows.data) || []; if (!rows.length) { box.innerHTML = card('7-day trend', '<div class="empty">no history yet (Analytics Engine)</div>'); return; }
      var G = {}, days = {}; rows.forEach(function (r) { days[r.d] = 1; (G[r.grp] = G[r.grp] || {})[r.d] = { n: +r.n || 0, p95: +r.p95 || 0, p50: +r.p50 || 0 }; }); var dl = Object.keys(days).sort();
      var groups = Object.keys(G).map(function (k) { var tot = 0; dl.forEach(function (d) { tot += (G[k][d] || {}).n || 0; }); return { k: k, tot: tot }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.tot - a.tot; }).slice(0, 10);
      var cols = [{ h: 'group', mono: true, f: function (g) { return esc(g.k); } }].concat(dl.map(function (d) { return { h: d.slice(5), r: true, mono: true, f: function (g) { var x = G[g.k][d]; if (!x) return '<span class="meta">—</span>'; return '<span class="' + (x.n < 30 ? 'meta' : '') + '" title="n=' + x.n + ' · p50 ' + Math.round(x.p50) + 'ms">' + N(Math.round(x.p95)) + '</span>'; } }; })).concat([{ h: 'trend', r: true, f: function (g) { var vals = dl.map(function (d) { var x = G[g.k][d]; return x && x.n >= 30 ? x.p95 : null; }).filter(function (v) { return v != null; }); if (vals.length < 3) return '<span class="meta">needs 3 days with n≥30</span>'; var first = vals.slice(0, Math.ceil(vals.length / 2)), lastH = vals.slice(Math.ceil(vals.length / 2)); var a = first.reduce(function (s, v) { return s + v; }, 0) / first.length, b = lastH.reduce(function (s, v) { return s + v; }, 0) / lastH.length; var p = a ? Math.round((b - a) / a * 100) : 0; return '<span class="' + (p > 25 ? 'dn' : p < -25 ? 'up' : 'meta') + ' mono">' + (p >= 0 ? '+' : '') + p + '%</span>'; } }]);
      box.innerHTML = card('7-day trend', tbl(cols, groups, 'nothing'), 'p95 in ms per day · greyed = fewer than 30 samples that day · trend = second half vs first half of the week; red above +25% = drift, fix the page, not the budget'); }).catch(function () { var box = $('perfTrend'); if (box) box.innerHTML = card('7-day trend', '<div class="empty">history unavailable</div>'); });
  } });

  // ---------- TRADING / DEMO SPOT ----------
  VIEWS['trading/spot'] = { render: function (el) {
    var st = { f: 'all', q: '', sort: 'equity' };
    function paint() {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">loading accounts and live prices…</div>';
      J('/api/admin/spot').then(function (d) {
        var t = d.totals || {}, acc = d.accounts || [];
        var F = { all: function () { return true; }, active: function (a) { return a.txN > 0 || a.xferN > 0; }, holders: function (a) { return a.holdN > 0; }, profit: function (a) { return a.sinceStart > 0; }, untouched: function (a) { return !(a.txN > 0); }, stuck: function (a) { return a.stuck; } };
        var rows = acc.filter(F[st.f] || F.all).filter(function (a) { return !st.q || ((a.name || '') + ' ' + (a.email || '') + ' ' + a.uid).toLowerCase().indexOf(st.q) >= 0; });
        rows.sort(function (a, b) { return st.sort === 'since' ? (b.sinceStart || 0) - (a.sinceStart || 0) : st.sort === 'tx' ? (b.txN || 0) - (a.txN || 0) : st.sort === 'last' ? (b.last || 0) - (a.last || 0) : (b.equity || 0) - (a.equity || 0); });
        var who = function (a) { return a.name ? userLink(a.name) : (a.email ? userLink('', a.email) : '<span class="mono meta">' + esc(String(a.uid).slice(0, 10)) + '</span>'); };
        // the six-step journey (card → USDT → exchange → wallet → meme → cash out): how far accounts get, cumulative
        var ST = ['started', 'bought USDT', 'on the exchange', 'in the wallet', 'holds a meme', 'cashed out']; var real = acc.filter(function (a) { return !/^spottest|^e2e/i.test(String(a.uid || '')); }); var reach = ST.map(function (_, i) { return real.filter(function (a) { return (+a.onb || 0) >= i + 1; }).length; }); var onbH = real.length ? card('Onboarding funnel', bars(ST.map(function (s, i) { return { l: (i + 1) + '. ' + s, n: reach[i] }; }), 'l', 'n', function (v, r) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">' + Math.round(v / Math.max(1, real.length) * 100) + '%</small>'; }, real.length) + '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px">' + N(real.length) + ' real accounts · each bar = accounts that reached at least that step · the first big drop is where the product loses people</div>', 'where accounts stall on the six-step journey') : '';
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(t.accounts), 'accounts', 'dim', N(t.active) + ' active') + tile(USD(t.equity, 0), 'total equity', 'dim', N(t.holders) + ' holding coins') + tile(N(t.inProfit), 'in profit', 'dim', 'of ' + N(t.active) + ' active') + tile(N(t.stuck), 'stuck in wallet', t.stuck ? 'amber' : 'dim', 'money parked 7+ days') + '</div>' + onbH
          + card('Accounts', '<div class="bar"><input class="in" id="spq" placeholder="search name / email / uid" value="' + esc(st.q) + '"><select class="in" id="spsort" style="flex:0 0 auto">' + [['equity', 'Equity'], ['since', 'Since start'], ['tx', 'Trades'], ['last', 'Last active']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.sort === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div><div class="chips">' + [['all', 'All'], ['active', 'Active'], ['holders', 'Holders'], ['profit', 'In profit'], ['untouched', 'Untouched'], ['stuck', 'Stuck in wallet']].map(function (f) { return '<span class="chip ' + (st.f === f[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-f="' + f[0] + '">' + f[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
          + tbl([{ h: 'account', f: function (a) { return who(a) + (a.stuck ? ' <span class="badge amber">STUCK</span>' : ''); } }, { h: 'step', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return (a.onb || 0) + '/6'; } }, { h: 'equity', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return USD(a.equity); } }, { h: 'since $10k', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return '<span class="' + ((a.sinceStart || 0) >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + ((a.sinceStart || 0) >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + USD(Math.abs(a.sinceStart || 0)) + '</span>'; } }, { h: 'card / exchange / wallet', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return USD(a.cardUsd, 0) + ' / ' + USD(a.exUsd, 0) + ' / ' + USD((a.walUsd || 0) + (a.gasUsd || 0), 0); } }, { h: 'coins', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return N(a.holdN) + (a.memeN ? ' (' + a.memeN + ' memes)' : ''); } }, { h: 'moves', r: true, mono: true, f: function (a) { return N(a.txN); } }, { h: 'last', mono: true, f: function (a) { return a.last ? ago(a.last) + ' ago' : 'never'; } }], rows, 'no accounts match'), 'equity = card + exchange + wallet + gas + live holdings · ' + rows.length + ' shown');
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { st.f = c.getAttribute('data-f'); paint(); }; });
        var q = $('spq'); if (q) { var tm; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(tm); tm = setTimeout(function () { st.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paint(); }, 300); }; }
        var so = $('spsort'); if (so) so.onchange = function () { st.sort = so.value; paint(); };
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load'); });
    }
    paint();
  } };

  // ---------- PEOPLE / ACTIVITY ----------
  // ---------- PEOPLE / ACTIVITY (rewritten 2026-09-06 — the owner's daily read: "ko sta radi, sta se abjuza, gde puca") ----------
  // /api/admin/activity?h=&n=&pv=1&actor= : the 24h ring (server-written trade rows since 2026-09-06, client clicks, sign-ins,
  // pageviews on demand), one actor per person (username, else the device-derived guest id), and the abuse radar computed
  // server-side from plain counts. The stream refreshes by PREPENDING new rows (never a rebuild — the scroll stays put).
  VIEWS['people/activity'] = { render: function (el, arg) {
    var st = { h: 24, g: 'all', who: 'all', q: '', pv: false, live: true }; recall('people/activity', st, ['h', 'g', 'who', 'pv', 'live']);
    var GROUPS = {
      trades: ['open', 'close', 'liq', 'trim', 'sltp', 'order', 'sync', 'paper', 'limitorder', 'ratelimit'],
      money: ['claim', 'withdraw', 'wdpaid', 'wdreject', 'wdcancel', 'mission', 'shopbuy', 'gift', 'sale', 'checkout', 'refpaid', 'promopaid', 'lbpaid', 'exsignpaid', 'pass', 'premgrant', 'refclick', 'exchange', 'tool', 'promo', 'exsign'],
      social: ['chatmsg', 'chat', 'dm', 'follow', 'duel', 'post', 'comment', 'like', 'save', 'profile', 'myprofile', 'share', 'gift'],
      account: ['signup', 'login', 'logout', 'signin', 'otp', 'otpfail', 'username', 'profile_edit', 'push', 'alert', 'support', 'telegram', 'lang', 'frame', 'admin'],
      progress: ['academy', 'goal', 'predict', 'xpboost', 'pass', 'premview', 'premgate', 'brief', 'ai', 'ind', 'draw', 'watch', 'search', 'coin', 'screener', 'tab', 'nav', 'prod', 'hotpair', 'nudge', 'grad', 'sect'],
      spot: ['spottrade'],
      problems: ['otpfail', 'ratelimit', 'jserr', 'liq', 'wdreject', 'admin', 'premgate'],
      system: ['backup', 'wrap', 'signal', 'sigresult', 'digest', 'liqalert', 'alertfired', 'cpaper', 'bot', 'cfgchange', 'subkick']
    };
    var SEV = { liq: 'red', otpfail: 'amber', ratelimit: 'amber', jserr: 'amber', wdreject: 'amber', admin: 'info', withdraw: 'gold', claim: 'gold', sale: 'gold', shopbuy: 'gold', gift: 'gold', exchange: 'gold', signup: 'ok', login: 'ok' };
    function sev(e) { return SEV[e.t] || ''; }
    function L(e) { // one sentence per type, the label in bold; unknown types still read
      var t = e.t || '', x = esc(e.e || ''), X = x ? '<b>' + x + '</b>' : '', srv = e.v === 'srv' || !!e.x;
      var M = {
        pv: 'viewed <b>' + esc(e.p || '/') + '</b>' + (e.s && e.s !== 'direct' ? ' <span class="meta">from ' + esc(e.s) + '</span>' : e.f ? ' <span class="meta">from ' + esc(e.f) + '</span>' : ''),
        open: 'opened ' + X, close: (srv ? 'closed ' : 'closed a trade ') + X, liq: '<b class="dn">LIQUIDATED</b> ' + X, trim: 'reduced ' + X, sltp: 'set SL/TP ' + X, order: 'limit order ' + X, sync: X,
        paper: 'opened a paper trade ' + X + ' <span class="meta">(guest, browser-side)</span>', limitorder: 'placed a limit order ' + X + ' <span class="meta">(browser-side)</span>',
        claim: 'claimed ' + X + ' from the faucet', withdraw: 'requested a withdrawal ' + X, wdpaid: 'withdrawal marked <b>paid</b>', wdreject: 'withdrawal <b>rejected</b> ' + X, wdcancel: 'withdrawal cancelled ' + X,
        mission: 'completed mission ' + X, shopbuy: 'bought ' + X + ' in the Vault', gift: 'gift ' + X, sale: '<b class="up">PAID</b> ' + X, checkout: 'opened checkout ' + X, refpaid: 'referral paid ' + X, promopaid: 'promo paid ' + X, lbpaid: 'board prize paid ' + X, exsignpaid: 'sign-up bonus paid ' + X,
        pass: 'season pass ' + X, premgrant: 'Premium granted ' + X, refclick: 'arrived through a referral link ' + X, exchange: 'clicked <b class="gold">' + x + '</b> (exchange)', tool: 'clicked tool ' + X, promo: 'submitted a promo post ' + X, exsign: 'exchange sign-up ' + X,
        chatmsg: 'chat: <i>' + x + '</i>', chat: 'opened the chat', dm: 'sent a DM to <b>@' + x + '</b>', follow: 'followed <b>@' + x + '</b>', duel: 'duel: ' + X, post: 'posted in Community', comment: 'commented ' + X, like: 'liked a post', save: 'saved a post',
        profile: 'viewed <b>@' + x + '</b>', myprofile: 'opened their own profile', share: 'shared ' + X,
        signup: '<b class="up">created an account</b> ' + X, login: 'signed in', logout: 'signed out', signin: 'opened the sign-in box', otp: 'asked for a sign-in code ' + X, otpfail: '<b class="dn">wrong or expired code</b> ' + X, username: 'chose the username <b>@' + x + '</b>', profile_edit: 'edited profile (' + x + ')',
        push: 'enabled push notifications', alert: 'set a price alert ' + X, support: 'wrote to support', telegram: 'clicked Telegram', lang: 'switched language to ' + X, frame: 'equipped frame ' + X, admin: '<b>owner:</b> ' + x,
        academy: 'Academy lesson ' + X, goal: 'season goal ' + X, predict: 'daily call ' + X, xpboost: 'XP boost ' + X, premview: 'viewed Premium', premgate: 'hit the Premium gate ' + X, brief: 'read the daily brief', ai: 'asked the AI ' + X,
        ind: 'added indicator ' + X, draw: 'drew on the chart', watch: 'watchlist ' + X, search: 'searched ' + X, coin: 'opened coin ' + X, screener: 'screener ' + X, tab: 'calculator ' + X, nav: 'nav ' + X, prod: 'opened ' + X, hotpair: 'hot pair ' + X, nudge: 'activation card ' + X, grad: X, sect: 'scrolled to ' + X,
        spottrade: 'Demo Spot ' + X, jserr: '<b class="dn">JS error</b> ' + X, ratelimit: '<b class="dn">rate limit</b> ' + X,
        backup: 'backup ' + X, wrap: 'daily wrap ' + X, signal: 'signal ' + X, sigresult: 'signal result ' + X, digest: 'digest ' + X, liqalert: 'liquidation alert ' + X, alertfired: 'price alert fired ' + X, cpaper: 'signal paper trade ' + X, bot: 'Telegram bot ' + X, cfgchange: 'config changed ' + X, subkick: 'subscription ended ' + X
      };
      var s = M[t] || (esc(t) + ' ' + X);
      if (x && s.indexOf(x) < 0) s += ' <span class="meta">' + x + '</span>'; // a template that has no slot for the label still shows it (search matched on it, the eye must see it too)
      return s;
    }
    function who(e) { if (e.u) return userLink(e.u); if (e.v && e.v !== 'srv') return '<button type="button" class="lnk" data-actor="v:' + esc(e.v) + '" title="guest device ' + esc(e.v) + ' — click for everything this device did">guest ' + esc(e.v) + '</button>'; if (e.uid) return '<button type="button" class="lnk" data-actor="uid:' + esc(e.uid) + '">#' + esc(String(e.uid).slice(0, 6)) + '</button>'; return '<span class="meta">system</span>'; }
    function tcol(ts) { var d = dt(ts); var today = dt(Date.now()).slice(0, 10) === d.slice(0, 10); return '<span class="meta mono t" title="' + esc(dtFull(ts)) + '">' + esc(today ? d.slice(11) : d.slice(5)) + '</span>'; }
    function rowKey(e) { return (e.ts || 0) + '|' + (e.t || '') + '|' + (e.u || e.v || '') + '|' + String(e.e || e.p || '').slice(0, 40); }
    function rowHtml(e, fresh) { var m = []; if (e.p && e.t !== 'pv') m.push(esc(e.p)); if (e.cc) m.push(flag(e.cc)); if (e.d) m.push(esc(e.d)); if (st.q && e.ip && st.q === String(e.ip).toLowerCase()) m.push('<span class="mono">' + esc(e.ip) + '</span>');
      return '<div class="row ev sev-' + sev(e) + (fresh ? ' new' : '') + (e.t === 'pv' ? ' pvrow' : '') + '" data-k="' + esc(rowKey(e)) + '" data-ts="' + (+e.ts || 0) + '">' + tcol(e.ts) + '<span class="who">' + who(e) + '</span><span class="grow">' + L(e) + (m.length ? ' <span class="meta">' + m.join(' · ') + '</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="meta">' + ago(e.ts) + '</span></div>'; }
    function pass(e) {
      if (st.g !== 'all' && (GROUPS[st.g] || []).indexOf(e.t) < 0) return false;
      if (st.who === 'users' && !e.u) return false; if (st.who === 'guests' && (e.u || !e.v || e.v === 'srv')) return false; if (st.who === 'system' && (e.u || (e.v && e.v !== 'srv'))) return false;
      if (st.q) { var s = ((e.u || '') + ' ' + (e.e || '') + ' ' + (e.t || '') + ' ' + (e.p || '') + ' ' + (e.cc || '') + ' ' + (e.ip || '') + ' ' + (e.di || '') + ' ' + (e.v || '') + ' ' + (e.x && e.x.sym ? e.x.sym : '')).toLowerCase(); if (s.indexOf(st.q) < 0) return false; }
      return true;
    }
    var data = null, rendered = {}, MAXROWS = 700;
    function actorName(k) { if (!k) return ''; return k.indexOf('u:') === 0 ? '@' + k.slice(2) : k.indexOf('v:') === 0 ? 'guest ' + k.slice(2) : k.indexOf('d:') === 0 ? 'device ' + k.slice(2) : k.indexOf('ip:') === 0 ? 'ip ' + k.slice(3) : k; }
    function actorBtn(k, label) { return k ? '<button type="button" class="lnk" data-actor="' + esc(k) + '">' + (label || esc(actorName(k))) + '</button>' : ''; }
    function head() {
      var chips = function (list, cur, attr) { return '<div class="chips" style="margin:0">' + list.map(function (f) { return '<span class="chip ' + (cur === f[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-' + attr + '="' + f[0] + '">' + f[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; };
      return '<div class="bar">' + chips([['all', 'All'], ['trades', 'Trades'], ['money', 'Money'], ['social', 'Social'], ['account', 'Account'], ['progress', 'Progress'], ['spot', 'Spot'], ['problems', 'Problems'], ['system', 'System']], st.g, 'g')
        + chips([['all', 'Everyone'], ['users', 'Users'], ['guests', 'Guests'], ['system', 'System']], st.who, 'who')
        + '<input class="in" id="acq" placeholder="filter: @user, guest id, coin, page, country, ip" value="' + esc(st.q) + '" style="flex:1 1 200px"><select class="in" id="ach" style="flex:0 0 auto">' + [[1, '1h'], [3, '3h'], [6, '6h'], [12, '12h'], [24, '24h']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.h === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
        + '<label class="chip ' + (st.pv ? 'on' : '') + '" id="acpv" title="include every pageview in the stream">pageviews</label><label class="chip ' + (st.live ? 'on' : '') + '" id="aclive" title="new rows arrive at the top every 15 s">live</label></div>';
    }
    function shell() {
      el.innerHTML = head() + '<div class="grid g4" id="acTiles"></div><div class="jm" style="margin-top:12px"><div>' + card('Activity <span id="acN"></span>', '<div class="feed acfeed" id="acStream"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>', 'newest first · click a guest id or @user for everything that person did') + '</div><div class="jm-rail" id="acRail"></div></div>';
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-g]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { st.g = c.getAttribute('data-g'); remember('people/activity', st, ['h', 'g', 'who', 'pv', 'live']); shell(); paintAll(true); }; });
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-who]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { st.who = c.getAttribute('data-who'); remember('people/activity', st, ['h', 'g', 'who', 'pv', 'live']); shell(); paintAll(true); }; });
      var q = $('acq'); if (q) { var tm; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(tm); tm = setTimeout(function () { st.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paintStream(true); }, 220); }; }
      var hs = $('ach'); if (hs) hs.onchange = function () { st.h = +hs.value || 24; remember('people/activity', st, ['h', 'g', 'who', 'pv', 'live']); load(true); };
      var pvb = $('acpv'); if (pvb) pvb.onclick = function () { st.pv = !st.pv; remember('people/activity', st, ['h', 'g', 'who', 'pv', 'live']); load(true); };
      var lv = $('aclive'); if (lv) lv.onclick = function () { st.live = !st.live; remember('people/activity', st, ['h', 'g', 'who', 'pv', 'live']); lv.classList.toggle('on', st.live); };
    }
    function paintTiles() { var d = data; var users = d.actors.filter(function (a) { return a.key.indexOf('u:') === 0; }).length, guests = d.actors.filter(function (a) { return a.key.indexOf('v:') === 0; }).length, red = d.radar.filter(function (r) { return r.sev === 'red'; }).length, amber = d.radar.filter(function (r) { return r.sev === 'amber'; }).length;
      $('acTiles').innerHTML = tile(N(d.online), 'online now') + tile(N(d.nEv), 'events in ' + d.h + 'h', 'dim', d.ringOldest ? 'ring reaches back ' + ago(d.ringOldest) : '') + tile(N(users) + ' <small class="meta">+ ' + N(guests) + ' guests</small>', 'people seen', 'dim') + tile(N(red + amber), 'radar hits', red ? 'bad' : amber ? 'warn' : 'dim', red ? N(red) + ' red' : ''); }
    function paintStream(full) { var box = $('acStream'); if (!box || !data) return; var list = data.rows.filter(pass);
      if (full) { rendered = {}; var top = list.slice(0, MAXROWS); box.innerHTML = top.map(function (e) { return rowHtml(e, false); }).join('') || '<div class="empty">nothing matches in this window</div>'; top.forEach(function (e) { rendered[rowKey(e)] = 1; }); }
      else { var fresh = list.filter(function (e) { return !rendered[rowKey(e)]; }); if (fresh.length) { var emp = box.querySelector('.empty'); if (emp) emp.remove();
          // a row that arrives late (the ring batches up to a few seconds; the DO writes its own rows) carries its REAL time — it is slotted
          // where it belongs by timestamp, never pinned to the top out of order (owner 2026-09-06: "ne izlaze redom")
          fresh.slice(0, 300).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.ts || 0) - (a.ts || 0); }).forEach(function (e) { var tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = rowHtml(e, true); var node = tmp.firstChild, ts = +e.ts || 0, kids = box.children, at = null; for (var i = 0; i < kids.length; i++) { if ((+kids[i].getAttribute('data-ts') || 0) <= ts) { at = kids[i]; break; } } box.insertBefore(node, at); });
          fresh.forEach(function (e) { rendered[rowKey(e)] = 1; }); while (box.children.length > MAXROWS + 200) box.removeChild(box.lastChild); setTimeout(function () { box.querySelectorAll('.row.new').forEach(function (r) { r.classList.remove('new'); }); }, 2500); } }
      var n = $('acN'); if (n) n.textContent = N(list.length) + (list.length > MAXROWS ? ' (showing ' + N(MAXROWS) + ')' : ''); }
    function bars2(items) { var mx = 1; items.forEach(function (x) { if (x.n > mx) mx = x.n; }); return items.map(function (x) { return '<div class="row" style="padding:5px 0"><span class="grow">' + x.html + '</span><span class="mono" style="min-width:34px;text-align:right">' + N(x.n) + '</span></div><div class="bar2"><i style="width:' + Math.round(x.n / mx * 100) + '%"></i></div>'; }).join(''); }
    function paintRail() { var d = data, rail = $('acRail'); if (!rail) return;
      var radar = d.radar.length ? d.radar.slice(0, 40).map(function (r) { return '<div class="rd ' + r.sev + '"><b>' + esc(r.title) + '</b><div class="meta">' + esc(r.detail) + (r.ts ? ' · ' + ago(r.ts) + ' ago' : '') + '</div>' + (r.actor ? '<div>' + actorBtn(r.actor, 'open ' + esc(actorName(r.actor))) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">nothing suspicious in this window — every rule is a plain count (shared device, shared IP with money, 30 trade actions in 10 min, 15 chat lines in 10 min, 3 failed codes, rate limits, claims from one device, guests with 10+ closes, 5 JS errors on a page, money within an hour of sign-up)</div>';
      var types = Object.keys(d.types).map(function (k) { return { html: esc(k), n: d.types[k] }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 14);
      var act = d.actors.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 14).map(function (a) { return { html: actorBtn(a.key) + ' ' + flag(a.cc) + (a.problems ? ' <span class="dn">' + a.problems + ' problems</span>' : '') + (a.money ? ' <span class="gold">' + a.money + ' money</span>' : ''), n: a.n }; });
      var guests = d.actors.filter(function (a) { return a.key.indexOf('v:') === 0 && a.trades; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.trades - a.trades; }).slice(0, 8).map(function (a) { return { html: actorBtn(a.key) + ' ' + flag(a.cc) + ' <span class="meta">' + esc(a.d || '') + ' · ' + N(a.pv) + ' pv</span>', n: a.trades }; });
      var auth = (d.auth || []).slice(0, 25).map(function (x) { return '<div class="row" style="padding:5px 0"><span class="meta mono t">' + esc(dt(x.ts).slice(11)) + '</span><span class="who">' + (x.u ? userLink(x.u) : '<span class="meta">?</span>') + '</span><span class="grow meta">' + esc(x.k) + ' · <span class="mono">' + esc(x.ip || '') + '</span>' + (x.did ? ' · ' + actorBtn('d:' + x.did, 'dev ' + esc(x.did)) : '') + '</span>' + flag(x.cc) + '</div>'; }).join('');
      rail.innerHTML = card('Radar', '<div class="radar">' + radar + '</div>', d.radar.length ? d.radar.length + ' hits' : '') + card('What happened', bars2(types), 'by type') + card('Most active', bars2(act) || '<div class="empty">nobody yet</div>', 'events per person') + (guests.length ? card('Guests trading', bars2(guests), 'no account, browser-side trades') : '') + card('Sign-ins', auth || '<div class="empty">none in this window</div>', 'ip + device'); }
    function paintAll(full) { if (!data) return; paintTiles(); paintStream(full); paintRail(); }
    function load(full) { var u = '/api/admin/activity?h=' + st.h + '&n=' + (st.pv ? 4000 : 2500) + (st.pv ? '&pv=1' : '') + '&_=' + Date.now();
      J(u).then(function (d) { if (!d || d.error) throw new Error(d && d.error); data = d; if (full || !$('acStream')) { shell(); paintAll(true); } else paintAll(false); stamp(); }).catch(function () { if (!data) el.innerHTML = head() + failHtml('could not load the activity ring'); }); }
    // one person, everything: timeline WITH pageviews, devices, ips, sign-ins, what kind of things they do
    function actorView(key) {
      var m = modal('<div class="mhead"><b>' + esc(actorName(key)) + '</b><span class="meta" id="avSub"></span><button class="btn sm x" id="avX">close</button></div><div id="avBody"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>'); $('avX').onclick = closeModal;
      J('/api/admin/activity?h=' + st.h + '&n=1200&actor=' + encodeURIComponent(key) + '&_=' + Date.now()).then(function (d) { var b = $('avBody'); if (!b) return; var a = d.actorInfo;
        var info = a ? '<div class="line">' + (a.u ? userLink(a.u) + ' · ' : '') + flag(a.cc) + ' ' + esc(a.d || '') + ' · first seen ' + ago(a.first) + ' ago · last ' + ago(a.last) + ' ago · <b>' + N(a.n) + '</b> events · <b>' + N(a.pv) + '</b> pageviews on ' + N(a.pages) + ' pages' + (a.trades ? ' · <b>' + N(a.trades) + '</b> trade actions' : '') + (a.money ? ' · <b class="gold">' + N(a.money) + '</b> money events' : '') + (a.problems ? ' · <b class="dn">' + N(a.problems) + '</b> problems' : '') + '</div><div class="line" style="font-size:12px">devices: ' + (a.dids.length ? a.dids.map(function (x) { return actorBtn('d:' + x, esc(x)); }).join(' ') : '<span class="meta">none</span>') + ' · ips: ' + (a.ips.length ? a.ips.map(function (x) { return actorBtn('ip:' + x, esc(x)); }).join(' ') : '<span class="meta">none</span>') + (a.vids > 1 ? ' · ' + a.vids + ' visitor ids' : '') + '</div>' : '<div class="line meta">no summary for this key in the window</div>';
        var auth = (d.auth || []).slice(0, 12).map(function (x) { return '<span class="meta">' + esc(x.k) + ' ' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago · ' + esc(x.ip || '') + (x.u ? ' · @' + esc(x.u) : '') + '</span>'; }).join('<br>');
        var rows = (d.rows || []).slice(0, 600).map(function (e) { return rowHtml(e, false); }).join('') || '<div class="empty">nothing in this window</div>';
        b.innerHTML = info + (auth ? '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:4px">sign-ins: <br>' + auth + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="feed acfeed" style="max-height:60vh;margin-top:8px">' + rows + '</div>'; var sub = $('avSub'); if (sub) sub.textContent = N((d.rows || []).length) + ' rows · ' + st.h + 'h';
      }).catch(function () { var b = $('avBody'); if (b) b.innerHTML = failHtml(); });
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', function onA(e) { if (!document.body.contains(el)) { document.removeEventListener('click', onA); return; } var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-actor]'); if (!b) return; e.preventDefault(); actorView(b.getAttribute('data-actor')); });
    el.innerHTML = head() + SKEL;
    load(true);
    if (arg) setTimeout(function () { actorView(decodeURIComponent(arg)); }, 400);
    every(15000, function () { if (st.live) load(false); });
  } };
  // ---------- INBOX / TRADER CHAT ----------
  // /chat/admin/history (messages newest-last), /chat/admin/post {text}, /chat/admin/delete {ts}, /chat/admin/poll {q,opts}|{close:1}|{clear:1},
  // /chat/reset (GET) wipes the room, /api/admin/broadcast {text,photo} posts to the Telegram channel, /api/news feeds the news picker.
  VIEWS['inbox/chat'] = { render: function (el) {
    var MP_SIG = 'Trade it free on <a href="https://marginpad.io">MarginPad</a> - crypto futures tools, no sign-up.';
    function list() {
      J('/chat/admin/history').then(function (d) {
        var ms = ((d && d.messages) || []).slice().reverse(); var day = ms.filter(function (m) { return Date.now() - (m.ts || 0) < 86400000; });
        var who = {}; day.forEach(function (m) { if (!m.admin) who[m.u || 'anon'] = 1; });
        $('chatTiles').innerHTML = tile(N(ms.length), 'messages kept', 'dim', 'global room') + tile(N(day.length), 'last 24h', day.length ? '' : 'dim') + tile(N(Object.keys(who).length), 'people talking 24h', 'dim') + tile(N(ms.filter(function (m) { return m.admin; }).length), 'posted as MarginPad', 'dim');
        // when the room talks (24 hourly beats) and who carries it
        var hb = []; for (var i = 23; i >= 0; i--) { var t0 = Date.now() - (i + 1) * 3600000, t1 = Date.now() - i * 3600000; hb.push({ d: dt(t0).slice(11, 13) + ':00', v: ms.filter(function (m) { return m.ts >= t0 && m.ts < t1; }).length }); }
        var tk = {}; day.forEach(function (m) { if (m.admin) return; tk[m.u || 'anon'] = (tk[m.u || 'anon'] || 0) + 1; }); var talkers = Object.keys(tk).map(function (k) { return { html: userLink(k), n: tk[k] }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 6);
        var rc = $('chatRhythm'); if (rc) rc.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g2"><div><div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">messages per hour, last 24h</div>' + spark(hb, 'v') + '</div><div><div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">who talks, last 24h</div>' + (talkers.length ? bars(talkers, 'html', 'n') : '<div class="empty">quiet day</div>') + '</div></div>';
        $('chatList').innerHTML = ms.length ? ms.slice(0, 200).map(function (m) { return '<div class="chatrow"><span class="who ' + (m.admin ? 'mp' : '') + '"' + (m.admin ? '' : ' data-mention="' + esc(m.u || '') + '" style="cursor:pointer" title="reply to @' + esc(m.u || '') + '"') + '>' + (m.admin ? 'MarginPad' : esc(m.u || 'anon')) + '</span><span class="txt">' + esc(m.t || '') + '</span><span class="meta">' + ago(m.ts) + ' ago</span><button class="btn sm" data-del="' + (+m.ts || 0) + '" title="delete this message">del</button></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no messages yet</div>';
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-del]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { b.disabled = true; post('/chat/admin/delete', { ts: +b.getAttribute('data-del') }).then(function () { toast('deleted'); list(); }); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-mention]').forEach(function (w) { w.onclick = function () { var inp = $('chatIn'); var tag = '@' + w.getAttribute('data-mention') + ' '; if (inp.value.indexOf(tag) < 0) inp.value = tag + inp.value; inp.focus(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); }; });
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { $('chatList').innerHTML = failHtml('could not load the room'); });
    }
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4" id="chatTiles"></div>'
      + card('Rhythm', '<div id="chatRhythm"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>', 'a dead room at 20:00 UTC is where a MarginPad post lands best')
