      + card('Post as MarginPad', '<div class="bar"><input class="in" id="chatIn" maxlength="280" placeholder="say something to the room"><button class="btn lime" id="chatSend">Send</button></div>', 'lands in the global room with the MarginPad badge')
      + card('Room', '<div class="feed" id="chatList" style="max-height:480px"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>', 'newest first · refreshes every 8s · del removes a message for everyone', '<button class="btn sm red" id="chatClear">Clear all messages</button>')
      + card('Chat poll', '<div class="bar"><input class="in" id="pollQ" maxlength="140" placeholder="Question, e.g. Where does BTC close this week?"><input class="in" id="pollOpts" maxlength="300" placeholder="Options separated by | e.g. Above 70k | 65-70k | Below 65k"></div><div class="bar"><button class="btn lime" id="pollStart">Start poll</button><button class="btn" id="pollClose">Close (keep results pinned)</button><button class="btn red" id="pollClear">Remove from chat</button><span class="stat" id="pollSt"></span></div>', 'pinned in the trader chat · users vote live')
      + card('Telegram channel', '<textarea class="in" id="tgBc" rows="4" maxlength="1000" placeholder="Announcement for the channel. Basic HTML works: <b>, <a href>."></textarea><div class="bar" style="margin-top:8px"><input class="in" id="tgBcImg" placeholder="Image URL (optional) - attaches a photo above the post"></div><div class="bar"><button class="btn" id="tgNewsBtn">Insert a news article</button><button class="btn" id="tgSig">Add the MarginPad footer</button><button class="btn lime" id="tgBcSend">Post to channel</button><span class="stat" id="tgBcSt"></span></div><div id="tgNews" hidden></div>', 'one post to the public channel · confirmed before sending');
    $('chatSend').onclick = function () { var t = ($('chatIn').value || '').trim(); if (!t) return; $('chatSend').disabled = true; post('/chat/admin/post', { text: t }).then(function () { $('chatSend').disabled = false; $('chatIn').value = ''; toast('posted'); list(); }); };
    $('chatIn').onkeydown = function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') $('chatSend').click(); };
    $('chatClear').onclick = function () { if (!confirm('Clear ALL chat messages for everyone?')) return; J('/chat/reset').then(function () { toast('room cleared'); list(); }); };
    $('pollStart').onclick = function () { var q = ($('pollQ').value || '').trim(), o = ($('pollOpts').value || '').split('|').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); if (!q || o.length < 2) { setStat('pollSt', 'need a question + at least 2 options (separate with |)', false); return; } post('/chat/admin/poll', { q: q, opts: o }).then(function (d) { setStat('pollSt', d && d.ok ? 'poll is live in chat' : 'failed: ' + ((d && d.error) || '?'), !!(d && d.ok)); }); };
    $('pollClose').onclick = function () { post('/chat/admin/poll', { close: 1 }).then(function () { setStat('pollSt', 'poll closed - final results stay pinned', true); }); };
    $('pollClear').onclick = function () { post('/chat/admin/poll', { clear: 1 }).then(function () { setStat('pollSt', 'poll removed from chat', true); }); };
    $('tgSig').onclick = function () { var ta = $('tgBc'); var v = ta.value.replace(/\s+$/, ''); ta.value = v + (v ? '\n\n' : '') + MP_SIG; ta.focus(); };
    $('tgNewsBtn').onclick = function () { var box = $('tgNews'); if (!box.hidden) { box.hidden = true; return; } box.hidden = false; box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">loading news…</div>'; J('/api/news').then(function (d) { var it = (d && d.items) || []; box.innerHTML = it.length ? it.slice(0, 12).map(function (x, i) { return '<div class="row" style="cursor:pointer" data-news="' + i + '"><span class="grow">' + esc(x.title || '') + '</span><span class="meta">' + esc(x.source || '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no news right now</div>'; box.querySelectorAll('[data-news]').forEach(function (r) { r.onclick = function () { var x = it[+r.getAttribute('data-news')]; var ta = $('tgBc'); ta.value = '<b>' + String(x.title || '').replace(/[<>&]/g, '') + '</b>\n' + (x.url || '') + (ta.value ? '\n\n' + ta.value : ''); box.hidden = true; ta.focus(); }; }); }).catch(function () { box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty err">news unavailable</div>'; }); };
    $('tgBcSend').onclick = function () { var t = ($('tgBc').value || '').trim(); if (!t) { setStat('tgBcSt', 'write something first', false); return; } if (!confirm('Post this announcement to the public Telegram channel?')) return; $('tgBcSend').disabled = true; post('/api/admin/broadcast', { text: t, photo: ($('tgBcImg').value || '').trim() }).then(function (d) { $('tgBcSend').disabled = false; if (d && d.ok) { $('tgBc').value = ''; $('tgBcImg').value = ''; setStat('tgBcSt', 'posted to ' + (d.channel || 'the channel'), true); } else setStat('tgBcSt', 'failed: ' + ((d && d.error) || 'unknown') + (d && d.error === 'no_channel' ? ' (add the bot to the channel as admin first)' : ''), false); }); };
    list(); every(8000, list);
  } };

  // ---------- INBOX / EMAIL ----------
  // GET /api/admin/sendmail = {addresses:[{key,name,addr}], log:[...]}; POST one recipient at a time {from,fromName,replyTo,subject,text,to}.
  VIEWS['inbox/mail'] = { render: function (el, arg) {
    var addrs = [];
    function paint(keepForm) {
      J('/api/admin/sendmail').then(function (d) {
        addrs = d.addresses || []; var log = d.log || [];
        var sent24 = log.filter(function (x) { return Date.now() - (x.ts || 0) < 86400000; }), fails = log.filter(function (x) { return !x.ok; });
        var logHtml = tbl([{ h: 'when', mono: true, f: function (x) { return dt(x.ts); } }, { h: 'from', mono: true, f: function (x) { return esc(String(x.from || '').split('@')[0]) + '@'; } }, { h: 'to', f: function (x) { return esc(x.to || ''); } }, { h: 'subject', grow: true, f: function (x) { return esc(x.subject || ''); } }, { h: 'status', f: function (x) { return x.ok ? '<span class="badge ok">SENT</span>' : '<span class="badge red" title="' + esc(x.err || '') + '">FAIL</span>'; } }], log.slice(0, 100), 'nothing sent yet - every support reply and manual mail lands here');
        if (keepForm && $('mlLog')) { $('mlLog').innerHTML = logHtml; $('mlTiles').innerHTML = tiles(); stamp(); return; }
        function tiles() { return tile(N(sent24.length), 'sent last 24h', sent24.length ? '' : 'dim') + tile(N(log.length), 'in the log', 'dim', 'last 100') + tile(N(fails.length), 'failed', fails.length ? 'red' : 'dim') + tile(N(addrs.length), 'identities', 'dim', addrs.map(function (a) { return a.key; }).join(', ')); }
        var opt = function (sel) { return addrs.map(function (a) { return '<option value="' + esc(a.key) + '"' + (a.key === sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(a.name) + ' · ' + esc(a.addr) + '</option>'; }).join(''); };
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="grid g4" id="mlTiles">' + tiles() + '</div>'
          + card('Compose', '<div class="fr"><label>From</label><div class="bar" style="margin:0;flex:1;justify-content:flex-end"><select class="in" id="mlFrom" style="flex:0 1 260px">' + opt('hello') + '</select><input class="in w" id="mlName" maxlength="40" placeholder="display name (optional), e.g. Marko from MarginPad" style="flex:1 1 200px"></div></div>'
            + '<div class="fr"><label>To</label><input class="in w" id="mlTo" autocomplete="off" placeholder="one@address.com, or several, comma@separated.com"></div>'
            + '<div class="fr"><label>Reply-To</label><select class="in" id="mlReply" style="width:auto">' + opt('support') + '</select></div>'
            + '<div class="fr"><label>Subject</label><input class="in w" id="mlSubj" maxlength="200" placeholder="subject"></div>'
            + '<textarea class="in" id="mlBody" rows="9" style="margin-top:10px" placeholder="Plain text. A matching HTML part is generated automatically. Plain, personal mail is what builds domain reputation."></textarea>'
            + '<div class="bar" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn lime" id="mlSend">Send</button><span class="stat" id="mlSt"></span></div>', 'manual sends through Resend · replies land in Support')
          + card('Sent', '<div id="mlLog">' + logHtml + '</div>', 'last 100 manual sends and support replies');
        if (arg) { $('mlTo').value = decodeURIComponent(arg); $('mlBody').focus(); arg = ''; } // #inbox/mail/<email> from a user card or ticket
        $('mlSend').onclick = function () {
          var to = ($('mlTo').value || '').split(',').map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); var body = $('mlBody').value || '';
          if (!to.length) { setStat('mlSt', 'enter at least one recipient', false); return; } if (!body.trim()) { setStat('mlSt', 'write a message first', false); return; }
          var base = { from: $('mlFrom').value, fromName: ($('mlName').value || '').trim(), replyTo: $('mlReply').value, subject: $('mlSubj').value || '', text: body };
          $('mlSend').disabled = true; var ok = 0, fail = 0, i = 0;
          (function next() { if (i >= to.length) { $('mlSend').disabled = false; setStat('mlSt', 'sent ' + ok + '/' + to.length + (fail ? ' · ' + fail + ' failed' : ''), !fail); if (!fail) { $('mlBody').value = ''; $('mlTo').value = ''; } paint(true); return; } var one = {}; for (var k in base) one[k] = base[k]; one.to = to[i++]; setStat('mlSt', 'sending ' + i + '/' + to.length + '…'); post('/api/admin/sendmail', one).then(function (r) { if (r && r.ok) ok++; else fail++; next(); }).catch(function () { fail++; next(); }); })();
        };
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load'); });
    }
    paint(); every(60000, function () { paint(true); });
  } };

  // ---------- TRADING / LIVE TRADES ----------
  // /api/auth/opentrades = every open paper position (signed-in + active guests) with whose it is; /api/auth/tradeev?since= = 7d close events
  // for realized P&L; prices from /api/prices plus /api/price?symbol= for anything the base set misses. Margins above the $100k cap are
  // shown but excluded from every sum (an oversized legacy position would otherwise drown the totals).
  VIEWS['trading/live'] = { render: function (el) {
    var S = { pos: [], traders: 0, srv: [], PR: {}, miss: {}, view: 'traders', sort: 'pnl', open: {}, q: '' }; recall('trading/live', S, ['view', 'sort']);
    var CAP = 100000;
    function calc(t) { var long = t.side !== 'short', dir = long ? 1 : -1; var sy = String(t.sym || '').toUpperCase().replace(/USDT$/, ''); var live = S.PR[sy]; var hasLive = isFinite(live) && live > 0; var entry = +t.entry, margin = +t.margin || 0, lev = (+t.lev > 0) ? +t.lev : 1; var qty = (t.qty != null && isFinite(+t.qty)) ? +t.qty : ((margin && entry) ? margin * lev / entry : 0); var pnl = hasLive ? qty * (live - entry) * dir : null; if (pnl != null && margin > 0 && pnl < -margin) pnl = -margin; var roe = (pnl != null && margin > 0) ? pnl / margin * 100 : null; var liq = +t.liq || (long ? entry * (1 - (1 - (t.mmr || 0.005)) / lev) : entry * (1 + (1 - (t.mmr || 0.005)) / lev)); var liqDist = hasLive && live > 0 ? (live - liq) / live * 100 * dir : null; var bad = hasLive && entry > 0 && (live / entry > 20 || live / entry < 0.05); // the ticker now resolves to a different asset than the one the trade was opened on (BANK, T...): P&L would be fiction, so it is shown as unknown and kept out of every sum
      if (!bad && roe != null && Math.abs(roe) > 10000) bad = true; // 1000x on a coin that then moved 8x = +87,000% ROE on one row: real by the sim's rules, but it drowns every total, so beyond 10,000% (a 10% move at 1000x) it is an outlier kept out of sums (same reason margins above the cap are)
      if (bad) { pnl = null; roe = null; liqDist = null; }
      var dead = !bad && hasLive && isFinite(liq) && (long ? live <= liq : live >= liq); return { long: long, sy: sy, live: live, hasLive: hasLive && !bad, entry: entry, margin: margin, lev: lev, qty: qty, pnl: pnl, roe: roe, liq: liq, liqDist: liqDist, dead: dead, over: margin > CAP || bad, bad: bad }; }
    function who(t) { return t.username || (t.email || '').split('@')[0] || 'guest'; }
    function fmn(x) { return (+x).toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: x >= 100 ? 2 : 6 }); }
    function money(v, d) { return (v >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + '$' + Math.abs(v).toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: d == null ? 2 : d }); }
    function posRow(t) { var c = calc(t); var bcol = c.liqDist == null ? 'var(--faint)' : c.liqDist < 3 ? '#ff6258' : c.liqDist < 10 ? '#ffb347' : '#2ebd85'; var bw = c.liqDist == null ? 0 : Math.max(4, Math.min(100, c.liqDist * 10));
      return '<div class="pos' + (c.dead ? ' dead' : '') + '"><span class="sd" style="color:' + (c.long ? '#2ebd85' : '#ff6258') + '">' + (c.long ? 'LONG' : 'SHORT') + '</span><span class="sym">' + esc(c.sy || '-') + ' <small>' + c.lev + 'x</small>' + (c.dead ? ' <span class="badge red">LIQ</span>' : '') + (c.bad ? ' <span class="badge amber" title="outlier: the live price is 20x away from the entry (a different asset under this ticker) or the ROE is beyond 10,000%; shown, but kept out of every sum">OUTLIER</span>' : c.over ? ' <span class="badge amber" title="above the $100k margin cap, excluded from sums">&gt;CAP</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="mono">$' + N(Math.round(c.margin)) + '</span><span class="mono" style="color:var(--dim)">' + (isFinite(c.entry) ? fmn(c.entry) : '?') + ' <span style="color:var(--faint)">to</span> <b style="color:var(--ink)">' + (c.hasLive ? fmn(c.live) : '—') + '</b></span><span class="mono" style="color:' + (c.pnl == null ? 'var(--faint)' : c.pnl >= 0 ? '#2ebd85' : '#ff6258') + '">' + ((c.pnl != null && !c.over) ? money(c.pnl) + (c.roe != null ? ' <small>' + (c.roe >= 0 ? '+' : '') + c.roe.toFixed(0) + '%</small>' : '') : '—') + '</span><span class="liqb" title="distance to liquidation"><i style="width:' + bw + '%;background:' + bcol + '"></i></span><span class="mono" style="color:' + bcol + ';min-width:44px;text-align:right">' + (c.liqDist == null ? '—' : c.liqDist.toFixed(1) + '%') + '</span><span class="meta">' + ago(t.ts) + '</span></div>'; }
    function sortKey(t) { var c = calc(t); if (S.sort === 'age') return -(t.ts || 0); if (S.sort === 'margin') return -(c.margin || 0); if (S.sort === 'liq') return (c.liqDist == null ? 1e9 : c.liqDist); return -(c.pnl == null ? -1e9 : c.pnl); }
    function paint() {
      var alive = [], dead = []; S.pos.forEach(function (t) { (calc(t).dead ? dead : alive).push(t); });
      var totM = 0, totP = 0, nl = 0, ns = 0, worst = null, nOver = 0;
      alive.forEach(function (t) { var c = calc(t); if (c.over) nOver++; else { totM += c.margin; if (c.pnl != null) totP += c.pnl; } if (c.long) nl++; else ns++; if (!c.over && c.liqDist != null && (worst == null || c.liqDist < worst.d)) worst = { d: c.liqDist, t: t }; });
      var k = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(alive.length), 'live positions', alive.length ? '' : 'dim', N(S.traders) + ' traders') + tile('<span class="up">' + nl + '</span> <small>/</small> <span class="dn">' + ns + '</span>', 'long / short', 'dim') + tile('$' + N(Math.round(totM)), 'margin at risk', 'dim', nOver ? 'paper · ' + nOver + ' outliers out' : 'paper money') + tile('<span class="' + (totP >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + money(totP, 0) + '</span>', 'unrealized P&amp;L', 'dim', 'paper' + (alive.filter(function (t) { return calc(t).bad; }).length ? ' · ' + alive.filter(function (t) { return calc(t).bad; }).length + ' outliers out' : ''))
        + tile(worst ? '<span style="color:' + (worst.d < 3 ? '#ff8a80' : worst.d < 10 ? 'var(--amber)' : '#7fe7bd') + '">' + worst.d.toFixed(1) + '%</span>' : '—', 'nearest liquidation', 'dim', worst ? esc(who(worst.t)) + ' · ' + esc(calc(worst.t).sy) : '') + tile(N(dead.length), 'crossed liq, close pending', dead.length ? 'red' : 'dim', 'waiting for the owner\'s browser') + tile(N(S.srv.filter(function (e) { return e.kind === 'close'; }).length), 'closes 7d', 'dim') + tile(N(Object.keys(S.PR).length), 'live prices', 'dim') + '</div>';
      var chips = '<div class="bar"><div class="chips" style="margin:0">' + [['traders', 'Traders'], ['markets', 'Markets'], ['list', 'Raw list']].map(function (v) { return '<span class="chip ' + (S.view === v[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-v="' + v[0] + '">' + v[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><input class="in" id="lvq" placeholder="find trader" value="' + esc(S.q) + '"><select class="in" id="lvsort" style="flex:0 0 auto">' + [['pnl', 'Sort: P&amp;L'], ['liq', 'Sort: closest to liq'], ['age', 'Sort: newest'], ['margin', 'Sort: margin']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (S.sort === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>';
      var body;
      if (!S.pos.length) body = '<div class="empty">no open positions right now (signed-in users + active guests)</div>';
      else if (S.view === 'list') body = alive.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return sortKey(a) - sortKey(b); }).map(posRow).join('');
      else if (S.view === 'markets') {
        var by = {}; alive.forEach(function (t) { var c = calc(t); if (c.over || !(c.margin > 0)) return; var b = by[c.sy] || (by[c.sy] = { sy: c.sy, n: 0, l: 0, s: 0, p: 0, tr: {} }); b.n++; if (c.long) b.l += c.margin; else b.s += c.margin; if (c.pnl != null) b.p += c.pnl; b.tr[who(t)] = 1; });
        var rows = Object.keys(by).map(function (k2) { return by[k2]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.l + b.s) - (a.l + a.s); }); var mx = Math.max.apply(null, rows.map(function (r) { return r.l + r.s; }).concat([1]));
        body = rows.length ? '<div class="hb">' + rows.map(function (r) { var tot = r.l + r.s; return '<div class="hb-r"><span class="hb-l"><b>' + esc(r.sy) + '</b> <span class="meta" style="color:var(--faint);font-size:11px">' + r.n + ' pos · ' + Object.keys(r.tr).length + (Object.keys(r.tr).length === 1 ? ' trader' : ' traders') + '</span></span><span class="hb-t" style="display:flex;background:#0a0d12"><i style="width:' + (r.l / mx * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:#2ebd85;border-radius:4px 0 0 4px" title="long $' + N(Math.round(r.l)) + '"></i><i style="width:' + (r.s / mx * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;background:#ff6258;border-radius:0 4px 4px 0" title="short $' + N(Math.round(r.s)) + '"></i></span><span class="hb-v" style="min-width:150px">$' + N(Math.round(tot)) + ' <span class="' + (r.p >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + money(r.p, 0) + '</span></span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="line" style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px">green = long margin, red = short margin · bar length = total margin on that market · P&amp;L is unrealized paper</div>' : '<div class="empty">nothing sized under the cap</div>';
      } else {
        var G = {}; function ent(k2) { return G[k2] || (G[k2] = { key: k2, name: '', email: '', cc: '', uid: '', open: [], ev: [], last: 0 }); }
        S.pos.forEach(function (t) { var g = ent(String(t.uid || t.email || t.username || '?')); if (!g.name) g.name = who(t); if (!g.email) g.email = t.email || ''; if (!g.cc) g.cc = t.cc || ''; if (!g.uid) g.uid = t.uid || ''; g.open.push(t); if ((t.ts || 0) > g.last) g.last = t.ts || 0; });
        S.srv.forEach(function (ev) { var g = ent(String(ev.uid || ev.user_id || ev.email || ev.username || '?')); if (!g.name) g.name = ev.username || (ev.email || '').split('@')[0] || 'anon'; if (!g.email) g.email = ev.email || ''; if (!g.cc) g.cc = ev.cc || ''; if (!g.uid) g.uid = ev.uid || ev.user_id || ''; g.ev.push(ev); if ((ev.ts || 0) > g.last) g.last = ev.ts || 0; });
        var users = Object.keys(G).map(function (k2) { return G[k2]; });
        users.forEach(function (g) { var m = 0, up = 0, hasP = false; g.open.forEach(function (t) { var c = calc(t); if (c.over) return; m += c.margin; if (c.pnl != null) { up += c.pnl; hasP = true; } }); g.margin = m; g.unreal = hasP ? up : null; var rp = 0, w = 0, l = 0, lq = 0; g.ev.forEach(function (ev) { if (ev.kind === 'close') { rp += (+ev.pnl || 0); if ((+ev.pnl || 0) > 0) w++; else l++; if (ev.liq) lq++; } }); g.realized = rp; g.wins = w; g.losses = l; g.liqs = lq; });
        users.sort(function (a, b) { return b.last - a.last; }); if (S.q) users = users.filter(function (g) { return (g.name + ' ' + g.email).toLowerCase().indexOf(S.q) >= 0; });
        body = '<div class="line" style="margin-bottom:8px">' + users.length + ' traders with an open position or a close in the last 7 days · click a row to expand</div>' + (users.slice(0, 80).map(function (g) { var open = !!S.open[g.key]; var wr = (g.wins + g.losses) ? Math.round(g.wins / (g.wins + g.losses) * 100) : null;
          return '<div class="trd"><div class="trd-h" data-tr="' + esc(g.key) + '">' + flag(g.cc) + '<b>' + (g.uid ? userLink(g.name !== 'guest' ? g.name : '', g.email) : '@' + esc(g.name)) + '</b>' + (g.open.length ? '<span class="t up">' + g.open.length + ' open · $' + N(Math.round(g.margin)) + '</span>' : '<span class="t">flat</span>') + (g.unreal != null ? '<span class="t" style="color:' + (g.unreal >= 0 ? '#2ebd85' : '#ff6258') + '">live ' + money(g.unreal, 0) + '</span>' : '') + (g.ev.length ? '<span class="t">7d ' + money(g.realized, 0) + ' · ' + g.wins + 'W-' + g.losses + 'L' + (wr != null ? ' · ' + wr + '%' : '') + (g.liqs ? ' · <span class="dn">' + g.liqs + ' liq</span>' : '') + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="t" style="margin-left:auto">' + ago(g.last) + ' ago</span>' + (g.uid && g.open.length ? '<button class="btn sm" data-ping="' + esc(g.uid) + '" title="email with live ROE + a nudge to manage the position">Ping</button>' : '') + '</div>'
            + (open ? '<div class="trd-b">' + (g.open.length ? g.open.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return sortKey(a) - sortKey(b); }).map(posRow).join('') : '<div class="empty">no open positions</div>') + (g.ev.length ? '<div class="sec-h">closes, last 7 days</div>' + g.ev.filter(function (e) { return e.kind === 'close'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.ts || 0) - (a.ts || 0); }).slice(0, 20).map(function (e) { return '<div class="row"><span class="mono" style="width:120px">' + esc(e.sym || '') + ' ' + esc(e.side || '') + '</span><span class="mono ' + ((+e.pnl || 0) >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + money(+e.pnl || 0) + '</span>' + (e.liq ? '<span class="badge red">LIQ</span>' : '') + '<span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + ago(e.ts) + ' ago</span></div>'; }).join('') : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') || '<div class="empty">no traders match</div>');
      }
      var deadH = dead.length ? card('Crossed liquidation, close pending', dead.map(posRow).join(''), 'price is past the liq level; the owner\'s browser closes it on their next visit (server sweep also catches it)') : '';
      // the crowd: how much leverage people really use, which way the money leans, and how much of it is guests
      var LB = [['1-5x', 5], ['6-20x', 20], ['21-50x', 50], ['51-100x', 100], ['over 100x', 1e9]]; var lc = LB.map(function () { return { n: 0, m: 0 }; }); var lm = 0, sm = 0, gN = 0, uN = 0, gM = 0;
      alive.forEach(function (t) { var c = calc(t); if (c.over) return; for (var i = 0; i < LB.length; i++) { if (c.lev <= LB[i][1]) { lc[i].n++; lc[i].m += c.margin; break; } } if (c.long) lm += c.margin; else sm += c.margin; if (t.uid) uN++; else { gN++; gM += c.margin; } });
      var lTot = lm + sm; var crowdH = alive.length ? card('The crowd', '<div class="grid g3"><div><div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">leverage in use</div>' + bars(LB.map(function (b, i) { return { l: b[0], n: lc[i].n, m: lc[i].m }; }), 'l', 'n', function (v, r) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">$' + N(Math.round(r.m)) + '</small>'; }) + '</div><div><div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">which way the money leans</div><div class="lsb" title="long ' + USD(lm, 0) + ' · short ' + USD(sm, 0) + '"><i style="width:' + (lTot ? lm / lTot * 100 : 50).toFixed(1) + '%"></i></div><div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px"><span class="up">' + (lTot ? Math.round(lm / lTot * 100) : 0) + '% long</span> · <span class="dn">' + (lTot ? Math.round(sm / lTot * 100) : 0) + '% short</span> by margin' + (lTot && Math.max(lm, sm) / lTot >= 0.75 ? ' · <b>one-sided crowd</b>' : '') + '</div></div><div><div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">guests vs accounts</div>' + bars([{ l: 'signed in', n: uN }, { l: 'guests', n: gN }], 'l', 'n', function (v) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">' + Math.round(v / Math.max(1, uN + gN) * 100) + '%</small>'; }) + '<div class="line" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:6px">guests hold ' + USD(gM, 0) + ' of margin · <a href="#growth/guests">activation card</a></div></div></div>', 'open positions under the cap · leverage tells you how the sim is being played') : '';
      // today's closes: realized results since 00:00 UTC (server close events), the biggest win and loss with who
      var t0 = Date.UTC(new Date().getUTCFullYear(), new Date().getUTCMonth(), new Date().getUTCDate()); var tc = S.srv.filter(function (e) { return e.kind === 'close' && (e.ts || 0) >= t0; }); var tw = tc.filter(function (e) { return (+e.pnl || 0) > 0; }), tl = tc.filter(function (e) { return (+e.pnl || 0) <= 0; }), tlq = tc.filter(function (e) { return e.liq; }); var big = tc.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (+b.pnl || 0) - (+a.pnl || 0); }); var bw = big[0], bl = big[big.length - 1]; var tp = tc.reduce(function (s, e) { return s + (+e.pnl || 0); }, 0); var evWho = function (e) { return e.username ? userLink(e.username, e.email) : (e.email ? userLink('', e.email) : '<span class="meta">guest</span>'); };
      var tdH = card('Today\'s closes', tc.length ? '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(tc.length), 'closes today', 'dim', '<span class="up">' + tw.length + ' won</span> · <span class="dn">' + tl.length + ' lost</span>' + (tlq.length ? ' · ' + tlq.length + ' liquidated' : '')) + tile('<span class="' + (tp >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + money(tp, 0) + '</span>', 'realized today, paper', 'dim', tc.length ? Math.round(tw.length / tc.length * 100) + '% winners' : '') + tile(bw && (+bw.pnl || 0) > 0 ? '<span class="up">' + money(+bw.pnl, 0) + '</span>' : '—', 'biggest win', 'dim', bw && (+bw.pnl || 0) > 0 ? evWho(bw) + ' · ' + esc(bw.sym) + ' ' + esc(bw.side) + ' ' + bw.lev + 'x' : '') + tile(bl && (+bl.pnl || 0) < 0 ? '<span class="dn">' + money(+bl.pnl, 0) + '</span>' : '—', 'biggest loss', 'dim', bl && (+bl.pnl || 0) < 0 ? evWho(bl) + ' · ' + esc(bl.sym) + ' ' + esc(bl.side) + ' ' + bl.lev + 'x' + (bl.liq ? ' · LIQ' : '') : '') + '</div>' : '<div class="empty">no close since 00:00 UTC yet</div>', 'server close events since 00:00 UTC · the same rows feed the season boards');
      // danger zone: within 2% of liquidation right now, under the cap - the people worth a ping before the candle does it
      var dz = alive.map(function (t) { return { t: t, c: calc(t) }; }).filter(function (x) { return !x.c.over && x.c.liqDist != null && x.c.liqDist > 0 && x.c.liqDist < 2; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.c.liqDist - b.c.liqDist; }).slice(0, 12);
      var dzH = dz.length ? card('Danger zone', '<div class="dz">' + dz.map(function (x) { return '<div class="row">' + flag(x.t.cc) + '<span class="who">' + (x.t.uid ? userLink(x.t.username, x.t.email) : esc(who(x.t))) + '</span><span class="mono">' + esc(x.c.sy) + ' ' + (x.c.long ? 'long' : 'short') + ' ' + x.c.lev + 'x · $' + N(Math.round(x.c.margin)) + '</span><span class="mono dn" style="margin-left:auto">' + x.c.liqDist.toFixed(2) + '% to liq</span>' + (x.t.uid ? '<button class="btn sm" data-ping="' + esc(x.t.uid) + '">Ping</button>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>', dz.length + ' within 2% of liquidation · Ping emails the live ROE and a nudge (20h cooldown per user)') : '';
      el.innerHTML = k + dzH + tdH + crowdH + card('Open positions', chips + body, 'paper positions · prices live · refreshes every 12s · outlier = margin above $100k, ticker now a different asset, or ROE beyond 10,000% (shown with a badge, kept out of the tiles)') + deadH;
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-v]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { S.view = c.getAttribute('data-v'); remember('trading/live', S, ['view', 'sort']); paint(); }; });
      var q = $('lvq'); if (q) { var tm; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(tm); tm = setTimeout(function () { S.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); paint(); }, 250); }; }
      var so = $('lvsort'); if (so) so.onchange = function () { S.sort = so.value; remember('trading/live', S, ['view', 'sort']); paint(); };
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-tr]').forEach(function (h) { h.onclick = function (e) { if (e.target.closest('a,button')) return; var k2 = h.getAttribute('data-tr'); S.open[k2] = !S.open[k2]; paint(); }; });
      el.querySelectorAll('[data-ping]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { b.disabled = true; post('/api/admin/pingpos', { uid: b.getAttribute('data-ping') }).then(function (d) { b.textContent = d && d.ok ? 'sent' : (d && d.error === 'cooldown' ? 'cooldown 20h' : 'failed'); }); }; });
      stamp();
    }
    function prices() { J('/api/prices').then(function (d) { var P = (d && (d.prices || d.pairs || d)) || {}; var put = function (k2, v) { var n = typeof v === 'number' ? v : (v && +(v.price || v.p || v.last || v.c)); if (n > 0) S.PR[String(k2).toUpperCase().replace(/USDT$/, '')] = n; }; if (Array.isArray(P)) P.forEach(function (row) { put(row.symbol || row.sym || '', row); }); else Object.keys(P).forEach(function (k2) { put(k2, P[k2]); }); paint(); }).catch(function () {}); }
    var pt = null; function qpaint() { clearTimeout(pt); pt = setTimeout(paint, 80); } // ~25 price lookups resolve within a second: one repaint, not 25
    function fill() { var now = Date.now(), need = {}; S.pos.forEach(function (t) { var sy = String(t.sym || '').toUpperCase().replace(/USDT$/, ''); if (sy && !(S.PR[sy] > 0) && (!S.miss[sy] || now - S.miss[sy] > 30000)) need[sy] = 1; }); Object.keys(need).slice(0, 24).forEach(function (sy) { S.miss[sy] = now; J('/api/price?symbol=' + encodeURIComponent(sy)).then(function (j) { if (j && +j.price > 0) { S.PR[sy] = +j.price; qpaint(); } }).catch(function () {}); }); }
    function load() { J('/api/auth/opentrades').then(function (d) { S.pos = (d && d.positions) || []; S.traders = (d && d.traders) || 0; paint(); fill(); }).catch(function () { if (!S.pos.length) el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load positions'); }); }
    function loadEv() { J('/api/auth/tradeev?since=' + (Date.now() - 604800000)).then(function (d) { S.srv = (d && d.events) || []; paint(); }).catch(function () {}); }
    load(); prices(); loadEv(); every(12000, load); every(20000, prices); every(60000, loadEv);
  } };

  // ---------- PEOPLE / JOURNEY MAP ----------
  // Live: /api/stats?format=json visitors[] (one row per recent pageview) + feed[] (actions), grouped per visitor, merged per signed-in user.
  // Trace: /api/admin/journey?u=&h= = one user's trail (pageviews + actions) split into sessions on a 30-minute gap.
  VIEWS['people/journeys'] = { render: function (el, arg) {
    var S = { f: 'all', view: 'journeys', mode: 'live', user: null, q: '', h: 24 };
    var STN = { home: 'Homepage', plan: 'Paper Trade', charts: 'Charts', calc: 'Calculators', scr: 'Screener', heat: 'Heatmap', rekt: 'Rekt', comm: 'Community', cal: 'Calendar', rwd: 'Rewards', blog: 'Blog', api: 'Bot API', acad: 'Academy', spot: 'Demo Spot', coin: 'Coin pages', lvl: 'Levels', news: 'News', other: 'Other', $$: 'Exchange click' };
    function stn(p) { var s = String(p || ''); if (s.length > 3 && s.charAt(0) === '/' && s.charAt(3) === '/' && /^[a-z]{2}$/.test(s.slice(1, 3))) s = '/' + s.slice(4); if (s === '' || s === '/' || s.indexOf('/demo-home') === 0) return 'home'; var pre = function (x) { return s.indexOf(x) === 0; }; if (pre('/paper-trade')) return 'plan'; if (pre('/charts')) return 'charts'; if (pre('/calculators') || s.indexOf('-calculator') > 0) return 'calc'; if (pre('/screener')) return 'scr'; if (pre('/heatmap')) return 'heat'; if (pre('/rekt') || pre('/liquidations')) return 'rekt'; if (pre('/community')) return 'comm'; if (pre('/calendar')) return 'cal'; if (pre('/rewards') || pre('/vault')) return 'rwd'; if (pre('/blog')) return 'blog'; if (pre('/trading-api')) return 'api'; if (pre('/academy')) return 'acad'; if (pre('/spot')) return 'spot'; if (pre('/coin/') || pre('/coins')) return 'coin'; if (pre('/levels')) return 'lvl'; if (pre('/news')) return 'news'; return 'other'; }
    function dev(d) { return String(d || '').toLowerCase() === 'mobile' ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'; }
    function pageShort(p) { var s = String(p || '').replace(/^\/[a-z]{2}\//, '/'); if (s === '/' || s === '') return 'home'; return s.length > 26 ? s.slice(0, 24) + '…' : s; }
    function ckLbl(it) { var e = String(it.e || ''), t = it.t; var M = { tab: 'calc: ' + e, exchange: e, paper: 'trade ' + e, close: 'close ' + e, sltp: 'SL/TP ' + e, tool: 'tool: ' + e, chat: 'chat', ai: 'AI', search: e, watch: 'watch ' + e, ind: 'ind: ' + e, draw: 'draw', profile: '@' + e, coin: e + ' mkt', signin: 'sign-in', signup: 'signup', login: 'login', click: 'click: ' + e, share: 'share', like: 'like', comment: 'comment', follow: '+follow', post: 'post', claim: e || 'claim', withdraw: e || 'withdraw', promo: 'promo', mission: 'mission', academy: 'lesson', spottrade: 'spot ' + e, checkout: 'checkout', premgate: 'premium gate' }; return M[t] || (t + (e ? ' ' + e : '')); }
    function chain(items) { return '<div class="jc-p">' + items.map(function (it, i) { return (i ? '<span class="ar">&rsaquo;</span>' : '') + (it.k === 'pg' ? '<span class="pg" title="' + esc(it.p) + '">' + esc(pageShort(it.p)) + '</span>' : '<span class="ck ' + (it.t === 'exchange' ? 'gold' : '') + '">' + esc(ckLbl(it)) + '</span>'); }).join('') + '</div>'; }
    function group(d) { var by = {}, order = []; (d.visitors || []).forEach(function (v) { var id = v.v || '?'; if (!by[id]) { by[id] = { v: id, cc: v.cc, u: v.u, d: v.d || '', s: v.s, last: v.ts, items: [] }; order.push(id); } var g = by[id]; if (v.ts > g.last) g.last = v.ts; g.items.push({ k: 'pg', p: v.p || '/', ts: v.ts }); if (v.u) g.u = v.u; });
      (d.feed || []).forEach(function (ev) { if (!ev.v || !by[ev.v]) return; var g = by[ev.v]; if (ev.ts > g.last) g.last = ev.ts; g.items.push({ k: 'ck', t: ev.t, e: ev.e || ev.t, ts: ev.ts }); });
      var merged = {}, mo = []; order.forEach(function (id) { var g = by[id]; if (S.f === 'desktop' && dev(g.d) === 'mobile') return; if (S.f === 'mobile' && dev(g.d) !== 'mobile') return; var mk = g.u ? 'u:' + String(g.u).toLowerCase() : 'v:' + g.v; if (!merged[mk]) { merged[mk] = { v: g.v, u: g.u, cc: g.cc, d: g.d, s: g.s, last: g.last, items: [] }; mo.push(mk); } var M = merged[mk]; M.items = M.items.concat(g.items); if (g.last > M.last) { M.last = g.last; M.d = g.d; M.cc = g.cc; } if (g.u) M.u = g.u; });
      return mo.map(function (k) { var M = merged[k]; M.items.sort(function (a, b) { return a.ts - b.ts; }); return M; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.last - a.last; }); }
    function jcard(g, hdr) { var now = Date.now(); var live = now - g.last < 60000; var its = g.items.slice(-16); return '<div class="jc ' + (live ? 'live' : '') + '"><div class="jc-h">' + (g.u ? '<b>' + userLink(g.u) + '</b>' : '<b style="color:var(--dim)">visitor ' + esc(String(g.v).slice(0, 6)) + '</b>') + flag(g.cc) + '<span class="meta">' + esc(dev(g.d)) + (g.s ? ' · ' + esc(String(g.s).slice(0, 24)) : '') + '</span>' + (hdr ? '<span class="meta">' + hdr + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + (live ? '<span class="up">live</span> · ' : '') + ago(g.last) + ' ago · ' + g.items.length + ' steps</span></div>' + chain(its) + '</div>'; }
    function rail(groups) { var pages = {}, acts = []; groups.forEach(function (g) { g.items.forEach(function (it) { if (it.k === 'pg') { var s = stn(it.p); pages[s] = (pages[s] || 0) + 1; } else acts.push({ g: g, it: it }); }); }); acts.sort(function (a, b) { return b.it.ts - a.it.ts; });
      var top = Object.keys(pages).map(function (k) { return { l: STN[k] || k, n: pages[k] }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 10);
      // where journeys end: the last page of every visitor idle for 10+ minutes (the session is over)
      var ex = {}, exN = 0; groups.forEach(function (g) { if (Date.now() - g.last < 600000) return; var pgs = g.items.filter(function (it) { return it.k === 'pg'; }); if (!pgs.length) return; var s2 = stn(pgs[pgs.length - 1].p); ex[s2] = (ex[s2] || 0) + 1; exN++; }); var exit = Object.keys(ex).map(function (k) { return { l: STN[k] || k, n: ex[k] }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 8);
      return card('Top products', bars(top, 'l', 'n'), 'pageviews in the window') + card('Where sessions end', exit.length ? bars(exit, 'l', 'n', function (v) { return N(v) + ' <small class="meta">' + Math.round(v / Math.max(1, exN) * 100) + '%</small>'; }) : '<div class="empty">no finished session in the window yet</div>', 'last page of visitors idle 10+ min · the exit that is not the homepage or an exchange is the leak') + card('Live actions', acts.slice(0, 20).map(function (a) { return '<div class="row"><span class="who">' + (a.g.u ? '@' + esc(a.g.u) : '<span style="color:var(--faint)">visitor</span>') + '</span><span class="grow"><span class="ck ' + (a.it.t === 'exchange' ? 'gold' : '') + '" style="font:600 11px var(--mono)">' + esc(ckLbl(a.it)) + '</span></span><span class="meta">' + ago(a.it.ts) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') || '<div class="empty">no actions yet</div>', 'what people tap, newest first'); }
    function flows(groups) { var trans = {}, entries = {}, tot = 0; groups.forEach(function (g) { var prev = null, first = true; g.items.forEach(function (it) { if (it.k === 'pg') { var st = stn(it.p); if (first) { var src = String(g.s || 'direct').split(' ')[0].slice(0, 18); entries[src] = (entries[src] || 0) + 1; first = false; } if (prev !== null && prev !== st) { var key = prev + '>' + st; trans[key] = (trans[key] || 0) + 1; tot++; } prev = st; } else if (it.t === 'exchange' && prev !== null) { var k2 = prev + '>$$'; trans[k2] = (trans[k2] || 0) + 1; tot++; } }); });
      if (!tot) return '<div class="empty">not enough movement in the sample yet - flows appear once visitors hop between products</div>';
      var rows = Object.keys(trans).map(function (k) { var p = k.split('>'); return { l: (STN[p[0]] || p[0]) + '  to  ' + (STN[p[1]] || p[1]), n: trans[k], gold: p[1] === '$$' }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 24);
      rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.gold) r.html = '<span style="color:var(--amber)">' + esc(r.l) + '</span>'; });
      var ent = Object.keys(entries).map(function (k) { return { l: k, n: entries[k] }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 10);
      return '<div class="grid g2"><div>' + '<div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">hops between products</div>' + bars(rows, 'l', 'n') + '</div><div><div class="sec-h" style="margin-top:0">where journeys start</div>' + bars(ent, 'l', 'n') + '</div></div><div class="line" style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px">' + tot + ' hops in the window · amber = a hop that ended in an exchange click</div>'; }
    var NOISE = { hb: 1, scroll: 1, time: 1, grid: 1, frame: 1, plev: 1, cliqhold: 1, cliq: 1, el: 0 }; // heartbeats, scroll depth, dwell buckets, click-grid cells: telemetry, not steps a person took
    function sessions(U) { var out = [], cur = null, GAP = 1800000, u = U.user || {}; (U.events || []).forEach(function (ev) { if (NOISE[ev.type]) return; if (ev.type === 'el') { ev = { type: 'click', label: String(ev.label || '').replace(/^(Link|Button|Field):\s*/, ''), ts: ev.ts, cc: ev.cc, dev: ev.dev, path: ev.path }; } var it = ev.type === 'pageview' ? { k: 'pg', p: ev.path || '/', ts: ev.ts } : { k: 'ck', t: ev.type, e: ev.label || ev.type, ts: ev.ts }; if (!cur || ev.ts - cur.last > GAP) { cur = { v: u.id, u: u.username || String(u.email || '').split('@')[0], cc: ev.cc || u.cc, d: dev(ev.dev), s: '', last: ev.ts, items: [], n: out.length + 1 }; out.push(cur); } cur.items.push(it); if (ev.ts > cur.last) cur.last = ev.ts; }); return out; }
    function head() { return '<div class="bar"><div class="chips" style="margin:0">' + [['journeys', 'Journeys'], ['flows', 'Flows']].map(function (v) { return '<span class="chip ' + (S.view === v[0] && S.mode === 'live' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-jv="' + v[0] + '">' + v[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="chips" style="margin:0">' + [['all', 'All'], ['desktop', 'Desktop'], ['mobile', 'Mobile']].map(function (f) { return '<span class="chip ' + (S.f === f[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-jf="' + f[0] + '">' + f[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><input class="in" id="jmU" placeholder="@username or email" value="' + esc(S.q) + '" style="flex:1 1 160px"><select class="in" id="jmH" style="flex:0 0 auto">' + [[6, '6h'], [24, '24h'], [72, '3 days'], [168, '7 days'], [720, '30 days']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (S.h === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><button class="btn lime" id="jmGo">Trace user</button>' + (S.mode === 'user' ? '<button class="btn" id="jmLive">Back to live</button>' : '') + '</div>'; }
    function wire() { el.querySelectorAll('[data-jv]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { S.view = c.getAttribute('data-jv'); S.mode = 'live'; paint(); }; }); el.querySelectorAll('[data-jf]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { S.f = c.getAttribute('data-jf'); paint(); }; }); var go = $('jmGo'); if (go) go.onclick = function () { S.q = ($('jmU').value || '').trim(); S.h = +$('jmH').value || 24; if (!S.q) return; trace(); }; var u = $('jmU'); if (u) u.onkeydown = function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') $('jmGo').click(); }; var lv = $('jmLive'); if (lv) lv.onclick = function () { S.mode = 'live'; S.user = null; paint(); }; }
    function trace() { S.mode = 'user'; el.innerHTML = head() + '<div class="empty">tracing ' + esc(S.q) + '…</div>'; wire(); J('/api/admin/journey?u=' + encodeURIComponent(S.q) + '&h=' + S.h + '&_=' + Date.now()).then(function (U) { S.user = U; paint(); }).catch(function () { S.user = { error: 'transient' }; paint(); }); }
    var last = null;
    function paint() {
      if (S.mode === 'user') { var U = S.user; var body;
        if (!U || U.error) body = '<div class="empty">' + (U && U.error === 'not_found' ? 'no user matches "' + esc(S.q) + '"' : 'could not load the trail') + '</div>';
        else { var u = U.user || {}, ss = sessions(U).reverse(); var pg = 0, ck = 0, gold = 0; (U.events || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.type === 'pageview') pg++; else { ck++; if (e.type === 'exchange') gold++; } });
          body = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(pg), 'pages', 'dim', 'last ' + S.h + 'h') + tile(N(ck), 'actions', 'dim') + tile(N(gold), 'exchange clicks', gold ? '' : 'dim') + tile(N(ss.length), 'sessions', 'dim', '30-min gap') + '</div>' + card((u.username ? '@' + esc(u.username) : esc(u.email || '')), '<div class="line">' + esc(u.email || '') + ' · ' + flag(u.cc) + ' ' + esc(u.dev || '?') + ' · joined ' + ago(u.created) + ' ago · seen ' + ago(u.last_seen) + ' ago · ' + N(u.pv) + ' pv · ' + N(u.logins) + ' logins · ' + (U.evTotal != null ? N(U.evTotal) + ' events on record' + (U.oldest ? ', oldest ' + ago(U.oldest) + ' ago' : '') : '') + ' · <a href="/api/admin/user?email=' + encodeURIComponent(u.email || '') + '" target="_blank">full profile</a></div>' + (ss.length ? ss.map(function (g) { return jcard(g, 'session ' + g.n); }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no tracked activity in this window</div>'), 'newest session first'); }
        el.innerHTML = head() + body; wire(); return; }
      var d = last; if (!d) { el.innerHTML = head() + '<div class="empty">loading…</div>'; wire(); return; }
      var groups = group(d);
      var main = S.view === 'flows' ? card('Flows', flows(groups), 'how visitors move between products in the window') : card('Journeys', groups.length ? groups.slice(0, 60).map(function (g) { return jcard(g); }).join('') : '<div class="empty">waiting for live journeys</div>', 'last ~200 pageviews · one card per visitor, signed-in users merged · green border = active in the last minute');
      el.innerHTML = head() + '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(d.online), 'online now') + tile(N(groups.length), 'visitors in the window', 'dim', S.f === 'all' ? '' : S.f) + tile(N(groups.filter(function (g) { return g.u; }).length), 'signed in', 'dim') + tile(N(groups.filter(function (g) { return g.items.some(function (it) { return it.k === 'ck' && it.t === 'exchange'; }); }).length), 'clicked an exchange', 'dim') + '</div><div class="jm" style="margin-top:12px"><div>' + main + '</div><div class="jm-rail">' + rail(groups) + '</div></div>';
      wire(); stamp();
    }
    function load() { if (S.mode !== 'live') return; J('/api/stats?format=json').then(function (d) { last = d || {}; paint(); }).catch(function () { if (!last) el.innerHTML = head() + '<div class="empty err">could not load</div>'; }); }
    if (arg) { S.q = decodeURIComponent(arg); trace(); } else { paint(); load(); }
    every(12000, load);
  } };

  // ---------- MONEY / FAUCET & REWARDS ----------
  // /api/reward/accounts, /admin, /log, /lbtop, /lbhistory, /moonsign/list, /promo/list, /api/admin/missionstats?d=7; account card from
  // /api/reward/detail + /earnings with adjust/note/message/ban/unban/unlock/remove/lbban. Pending payouts live in Withdrawals (one queue, not two).
  VIEWS['money/faucet'] = { render: function (el) {
    var S = { q: '', sort: 'new', f: 'all', accounts: [], hist: [] };
    function risk(x) { var s = 0; if (x.banned) s += 100; if (x.sameDid > 1) s += 55; if (x.sameIp > 2) s += 40; else if (x.sameIp > 1) s += 20; var ageH = x.created ? (Date.now() - x.created) / 3600000 : 999; if (ageH < 1 && x.claims >= 5) s += 30; var cph = ageH > 0.05 ? x.claims / ageH : x.claims; if (cph > 9) s += 25; return s; }
    function flags(x) { var f = []; if (x.banned) f.push('banned'); if (x.sameDid > 1) f.push(x.sameDid + ' on device'); if (x.sameIp > 1) f.push(x.sameIp + ' on IP'); var ageH = x.created ? (Date.now() - x.created) / 3600000 : 999; if (ageH < 1 && x.claims >= 5) f.push('new, ' + x.claims + ' claims'); var cph = ageH > 0.05 ? x.claims / ageH : x.claims; if (cph > 9) f.push(cph.toFixed(0) + ' claims/h'); return f; }
    function name(x) { return x.username ? '@' + x.username : (x.email || (/^u:/.test(String(x.address || '')) ? 'user ' + String(x.address).slice(2, 10) : 'wallet ' + String(x.address || '').slice(0, 8) + '…')); } // pre-account wallet rows have no name at all; say what the thing is instead of printing a hash
    function acctRow(x) { var fl = flags(x); return '<div class="row" style="cursor:pointer" data-acct="' + esc(x.address) + '">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who" style="' + (x.banned ? 'text-decoration:line-through;opacity:.65' : '') + '">' + esc(name(x)) + '</span><span class="meta">' + esc(x.dev || '?') + ' · ' + N(x.claims) + ' claims</span><span class="grow">' + fl.map(function (f) { return '<span class="badge ' + (f === 'banned' ? 'red' : /IP|device/.test(f) ? 'amber' : 'lime') + '" style="margin-right:4px">' + esc(f) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</span><span class="mono" style="color:var(--lime)">' + USD(x.balanceUsd) + '</span></div>'; }
    function whoRow(x) { return x.username ? userLink(x.username) : (x.email ? userLink('', x.email) : '<span class="mono meta">' + esc(String(x.address || '').replace(/^u:/, '').slice(0, 10)) + '</span>'); }
    function accounts() { var list = S.accounts.slice(); if (S.q) list = list.filter(function (x) { return ((x.address || '') + ' ' + (x.cc || '') + ' ' + (x.username || '') + ' ' + (x.email || '')).toLowerCase().indexOf(S.q) >= 0; }); if (S.f === 'flagged') list = list.filter(function (x) { return risk(x) > 0; }); else if (S.f === 'banned') list = list.filter(function (x) { return x.banned; }); else if (S.f === 'balance') list = list.filter(function (x) { return (+x.balanceUsd || 0) > 0; });
      if (S.sort === 'new') list.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.created || 0) - (a.created || 0); }); else if (S.sort === 'claims') list.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.claims || 0) - (a.claims || 0); }); else if (S.sort === 'bal') list.sort(function (a, b) { return (+b.balanceUsd || 0) - (+a.balanceUsd || 0); }); else list.sort(function (a, b) { return risk(b) - risk(a); });
      var cap = 120; var box = $('rwAccts'); if (!box) return; $('rwCount').textContent = list.length === S.accounts.length ? N(S.accounts.length) + ' accounts' : N(list.length) + ' of ' + N(S.accounts.length); box.innerHTML = list.length ? list.slice(0, cap).map(acctRow).join('') + (list.length > cap ? '<div class="empty">+ ' + N(list.length - cap) + ' more - refine the search</div>' : '') : '<div class="empty">no accounts match</div>'; bind(box); }
    function bind(box) { box.querySelectorAll('[data-acct]').forEach(function (r) { r.onclick = function (e) { if (e.target.closest('a')) return; openAcct(r.getAttribute('data-acct')); }; }); }
    function hist(i) { var w = S.hist[i]; var box = $('rwHist'); if (!box) return; box.innerHTML = w && w.top && w.top.length ? w.top.map(function (r, k) { var who = r.who || 'anon'; return '<div class="row"><span class="mono meta" style="width:28px">' + (k + 1) + '.</span><span class="who">' + (/…$/.test(who) ? esc(who) : '@' + esc(who)) + '</span><span class="mono ' + ((+r.roe >= 0) ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + ((+r.roe >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (+r.roe).toFixed(0)) + '%</span><span class="meta">' + esc(r.symbol || '') + ' ' + esc(r.side || '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no entries in that season</div>'; }
    function paint() {
      Promise.all([J('/api/reward/accounts'), J('/api/reward/admin'), J('/api/reward/log'), J('/api/reward/lbtop').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/reward/lbhistory').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/reward/moonsign/list').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/reward/promo/list').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/admin/missionstats?d=7').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/admin/money').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/reward/config').catch(function () { return {}; }), J('/api/auth/admin?limit=1&offset=0&sort=new').catch(function () { return {}; })]).then(function (r) {
        var a = r[0] || {}, ad = r[1] || {}, lg = r[2] || {}, lb = r[3] || {}, lh = r[4] || {}, moon = r[5] || {}, pr = r[6] || {}, ms = r[7] || {}, mo = r[8] || {}, cfg = (r[9] && r[9].config) || {}, ua = r[10] || {};
        // unit economics: what a claimer and a sign-up cost, and who can ask for money next
        var minWd = +cfg.minWdUsd || 5; var su7 = (ua.byDay || []).slice().sort(function (x, y) { return x.d < y.d ? -1 : 1; }).slice(-7).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.n || 0); }, 0);
        var ready = (a.accounts || []).filter(function (x) { return !x.banned && (+x.balanceUsd || 0) >= minWd; }).sort(function (x, y) { return (+y.balanceUsd || 0) - (+x.balanceUsd || 0); }), readySum = ready.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.balanceUsd || 0); }, 0);
        var near = (a.accounts || []).filter(function (x) { return !x.banned && (+x.balanceUsd || 0) >= minWd * 0.6 && (+x.balanceUsd || 0) < minWd; }).length;
        var byDay = (mo.dispensedByDay || []).slice().reverse();
        S.accounts = a.accounts || []; S.hist = lh.weeks || [];
        var pend = ad.pending || []; var pendSum = pend.reduce(function (s, w) { return s + (+w.amountUsd || 0); }, 0);
        var flagged = S.accounts.filter(function (x) { return risk(x) > 0 && !x.banned; }).sort(function (x, y) { return risk(y) - risk(x); }); var banned = a.bannedCount != null ? a.bannedCount : S.accounts.filter(function (x) { return x.banned; }).length;
        var claims = (lg.log || []).filter(function (e) { return e.type === 'claim'; });
        var dAvg = byDay.length >= 8 ? byDay.slice(-8, -1).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.usd || 0); }, 0) / 7 : 0;
        var tiles = '<div class="grid g4">' + tile(N(a.count), 'faucet accounts', 'dim', N(ad.newToday || 0) + ' new today') + tile(N(ad.activeToday || 0), 'active today', 'dim', N(ad.claimsToday || 0) + ' claims' + (dAvg ? ' · spend' + dl(ad.dispensedTodayUsd, dAvg) : '')) + tile(USD(ad.dispensedTodayUsd), 'dispensed today', (ad.dailyCapUsd && ad.dispensedTodayUsd >= ad.dailyCapUsd * 0.9) ? 'amber' : 'dim', '/ ' + USD(ad.dailyCapUsd, 0) + ' budget' + (byDay.length ? ' · 14d ' + USD(byDay.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (+x.usd || 0); }, 0), 0) : ''), mini(byDay, 'usd')) + tile(USD(a.totalBalanceUsd), 'unpaid balance', 'dim', 'what the ledger owes')
          + tile(N(pend.length), 'pending payouts', pend.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', pend.length ? USD(pendSum) + ' · <a href="#money/withdrawals">pay in Withdrawals</a>' : '') + tile(USD(ad.totalPaidUsd), 'paid all time', 'dim') + tile(N(flagged.length), 'flagged', flagged.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', N(banned) + ' banned') + tile(N(a.sharedIpWallets != null ? a.sharedIpWallets : 0), 'wallets sharing an IP', 'dim', a.ipDistinct != null ? N(a.ipDistinct) + ' distinct IPs' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="grid g4" style="margin-top:12px">' + tile(ad.activeToday ? USD((+ad.dispensedTodayUsd || 0) / ad.activeToday) : '—', 'cost per claimer today', 'dim', ad.activeToday ? (ad.claimsToday / ad.activeToday).toFixed(1) + ' claims each' : '') + tile(su7 ? USD((+mo.dispensed7dUsd || 0) / su7) : '—', 'faucet cost per sign-up, 7d', 'dim', N(su7) + ' sign-ups · ' + USD(mo.dispensed7dUsd, 0) + ' dispensed') + tile(N(ready.length), 'can withdraw now', ready.length ? 'amber' : 'dim', USD(readySum) + ' at or above the ' + USD(minWd, 0) + ' minimum') + tile(N(near), 'close to the minimum', 'dim', 'between 60% and 100% of ' + USD(minWd, 0)) + '</div>';
        var sel = S.hist.map(function (w, i) { var O = { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }; return '<option value="' + i + '">' + (w.current ? 'This season · ' : '') + new Date(w.weekStart).toLocaleDateString('en-US', O) + ' - ' + new Date(w.weekEnd - 1).toLocaleDateString('en-US', O) + ' (' + w.entries + ')</option>'; }).join('');
        var lbRows = (lb.top || []); var moonUsd = moon.moonUsd != null ? moon.moonUsd : 1, xUsd = pr.promoXUsd != null ? pr.promoXUsd : 0.1, rBase = pr.redditUsd != null ? pr.redditUsd : 0.5, rMax = pr.redditMaxUsd != null ? pr.redditMaxUsd : 5;
        var act = +ms.activeClaimers || 0;
        el.innerHTML = tiles
          + card('Needs a look', flagged.length ? flagged.slice(0, 15).map(acctRow).join('') : '<div class="line up">nothing flagged that is not already banned</div>', 'possible multi-accounting or farming, not yet banned · click to inspect', '<a href="#people/security">clusters</a>')
          + (ready.length ? fold('Ready to withdraw', ready.slice(0, 20).map(acctRow).join('') + (ready.length > 20 ? '<div class="empty">+ ' + N(ready.length - 20) + ' more</div>' : ''), N(ready.length) + ' accounts · ' + USD(readySum) + ' · the cash you may be asked for next', false) : '')
          + '<div class="grid g2">' + card('Season leaderboard', lbRows.length ? lbRows.map(function (r) { var who = r.name ? '@' + esc(r.name) : esc(String(r.address || '').replace(/^u:/, '').slice(0, 10)); return '<div class="row"' + (r.banned ? ' style="opacity:.55"' : '') + '><span class="mono meta" style="width:28px">#' + r.rank + '</span><span class="who">' + who + '</span><span class="meta">' + esc(r.symbol || '') + ' ' + esc(r.side || '') + '</span><span class="mono ' + (+r.roe >= 0 ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + (+r.roe >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (+r.roe).toFixed(0) + '%</span><button class="btn sm ' + (r.banned ? '' : 'red') + '" data-lbej="' + esc(r.address) + '" data-on="' + (r.banned ? 1 : 0) + '" style="margin-left:auto">' + (r.banned ? 'reinstate' : 'eject') + '</button></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no entries this season yet</div>', 'top ROE · eject wipes the entry for this season')
          + card('Past seasons', (S.hist.length ? '<select class="in" id="rwHistSel" style="width:100%;margin-bottom:8px">' + sel + '</select><div id="rwHist"></div>' : '<div class="empty">no history yet</div>'), 'season winners') + '</div>'
          + card('All accounts', '<div class="bar"><input class="in" id="rwq" placeholder="search username, email, country" value="' + esc(S.q) + '"><select class="in" id="rwsort" style="flex:0 0 auto">' + [['new', 'Newest'], ['claims', 'Most claims'], ['bal', 'Highest balance'], ['risk', 'Highest risk']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (S.sort === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div><div class="chips">' + [['all', 'All'], ['flagged', 'Flagged'], ['banned', 'Banned'], ['balance', 'Has balance']].map(function (f) { return '<span class="chip ' + (S.f === f[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-f="' + f[0] + '">' + f[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="feed" id="rwAccts" style="max-height:560px"></div>', '<span id="rwCount"></span>', 'click an account for the card')
          + '<div class="grid g2">' + fold('Moon sign-ups', (moon.pending || []).map(function (x) { return '<div class="row">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoRow(x) + '</span><span class="meta">Moon user <b class="mono" style="color:var(--ink)">' + esc(x.uid) + '</b></span><span class="meta">' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span><span style="margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:6px"><button class="btn sm lime" data-moon="' + esc(x.id) + '" data-act="approve">Approve +' + USD(moonUsd) + '</button><button class="btn sm red" data-moon="' + esc(x.id) + '" data-act="reject">Reject</button></span></div>'; }).join('') + (moon.decided || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { return '<div class="row" style="opacity:.65">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoRow(x) + '</span><span class="meta">' + esc(x.uid) + '</span><span class="mono ' + (x.status === 'approved' ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + esc(x.status) + (x.status === 'approved' ? ' +' + USD(x.amount) : '') + '</span><span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span></div>'; }).join('') || '<div class="empty">no sign-ups waiting</div>', (moon.pending || []).length + ' waiting · bonus paid after your manual check in the Moon dashboard', (moon.pending || []).length > 0)
          + fold('Content posts', (pr.pending || []).map(function (x) { var rd = x.platform === 'reddit'; return '<div class="row">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoRow(x) + '</span><span class="badge ' + (rd ? 'amber' : 'lime') + '">' + esc(x.platform) + '</span><span class="meta">' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span><a class="grow lnkline" href="' + esc(x.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="' + esc(x.url) + '">' + esc(x.url) + '</a><span class="acts" style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">' + (rd ? '<input class="in" type="number" step="0.1" min="0.1" max="' + rMax + '" value="' + rBase + '" data-amt="' + esc(x.id) + '" style="width:64px">' : '') + '<button class="btn sm lime" data-promo="' + esc(x.id) + '" data-act="approve">Approve' + (rd ? '' : ' +' + USD(xUsd)) + '</button><button class="btn sm red" data-promo="' + esc(x.id) + '" data-act="reject">Reject</button></span></div>'; }).join('') + (pr.decided || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (x) { return '<div class="row" style="opacity:.65">' + flag(x.cc) + '<span class="who">' + whoRow(x) + '</span><span class="badge">' + esc(x.platform) + '</span><span class="mono ' + (x.status === 'approved' ? 'up' : 'dn') + '">' + esc(x.status) + (x.status === 'approved' ? ' +' + USD(x.amount) : '') + '</span><span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + ago(x.ts) + ' ago</span></div>'; }).join('') || '<div class="empty">no posts waiting</div>', (pr.pending || []).length + ' waiting · X flat ' + USD(xUsd) + ' · Reddit base ' + USD(rBase) + ' up to ' + USD(rMax), (pr.pending || []).length > 0) + '</div>'
          + '<div class="grid g2">' + fold('Mission stats', (ms.rows || []).length ? (ms.rows || []).map(function (x) { var pct = act ? Math.round((x.users / act) * 100) : 0; return '<div class="row"><span class="grow">' + esc(x.title || x.mid) + ' <span class="meta">' + esc(x.cat || '') + ' · ' + USD((x.cents || 0) / 100) + '</span></span><span class="meta">' + N(x.claims) + ' claims · ' + N(x.users) + ' users</span><span class="mono" style="min-width:44px;text-align:right;color:' + (pct >= 80 ? '#ff8a80' : pct < 20 ? 'var(--faint)' : '#7fe7bd') + '">' + pct + '%</span></div>'; }).join('') + '<div class="line" style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">' + N(act) + ' users claimed at least one mission in 7 days · red 80%+ = too easy, grey under 20% = dead or broken</div>' : '<div class="empty">no mission claims in the window</div>', 'last 7 days · % = share of active claimers', false)
          + fold('Live claims', '<div class="feed" style="max-height:420px">' + (claims.length ? claims.slice(0, 60).map(function (e) { var who = e.username ? '@' + esc(e.username) : (e.email ? esc(e.email) : esc(String(e.address || '').replace(/^u:/, '').slice(0, 10))); return '<div class="row" style="cursor:pointer" data-acct="' + esc(e.address || '') + '">' + flag(e.cc) + '<span class="who">' + who + '</span><span class="mono up">+' + USD(e.amountUsd) + '</span><span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + ago(e.ts) + ' ago</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no claims yet</div>') + '</div>', claims.length + ' latest · refreshes every 20s', false) + '</div>';
        accounts(); bind(el); if (S.hist.length) { hist(0); $('rwHistSel').onchange = function () { hist(+this.value); }; }
        var q = $('rwq'); if (q) { var tm; q.oninput = function () { clearTimeout(tm); tm = setTimeout(function () { S.q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase(); accounts(); }, 250); }; } var so = $('rwsort'); if (so) so.onchange = function () { S.sort = so.value; accounts(); };
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (c) { c.onclick = function () { S.f = c.getAttribute('data-f'); el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.toggle('on', x === c); }); accounts(); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-lbej]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var on = b.getAttribute('data-on') === '1'; if (!on && !confirm('Eject this account from the season leaderboard? Their entries are wiped for this season.')) return; b.disabled = true; post('/api/reward/lbban', { address: b.getAttribute('data-lbej'), unban: on ? 1 : 0 }).then(function () { toast(on ? 'reinstated' : 'ejected'); paint(); }); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-moon]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var actn = b.getAttribute('data-act'); var note = actn === 'reject' ? (prompt('Reason shown to the user (optional):') || '') : ''; b.disabled = true; post('/api/reward/moonsign/review', { id: b.getAttribute('data-moon'), action: actn, note: note }).then(function (j) { toast(j && j.ok !== false ? actn + 'd' : 'failed'); paint(); }); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-promo]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var actn = b.getAttribute('data-act'), id = b.getAttribute('data-promo'); var payload = { id: id, action: actn }; if (actn === 'reject') payload.note = prompt('Reason shown to the user:') || ''; else { var ai = el.querySelector('[data-amt="' + id + '"]'); if (ai) payload.amount = +ai.value || 0; } (function send() { b.disabled = true; post('/api/reward/promo/review', payload).then(function (j) { if (j && j.error === 'too_early') { b.disabled = false; if (confirm('Post is younger than 24h. Approve anyway?')) { payload.force = 1; send(); } return; } toast(j && j.ok !== false ? actn + 'd' : 'failed: ' + ((j && j.error) || '?')); paint(); }); })(); }; });
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load the faucet'); });
    }
    function openAcct(a) {
      if (!a) return; var m = modal('<div class="mhead"><b id="amTitle">' + esc(a) + '</b><button class="btn sm x" id="amX">close</button></div><div id="amBody"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>'); $('amX').onclick = closeModal;
      J('/api/reward/detail?address=' + encodeURIComponent(a)).then(function (d) { renderAcct(a, d || {}); }).catch(function () { $('amBody').innerHTML = '<div class="empty err">could not load</div>'; });
    }
    function renderAcct(a, d) {
      var b = $('amBody'); if (!b) return; if (!d.exists) { b.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">no account for this address (already removed?)</div>'; return; }
      $('amTitle').textContent = d.username ? '@' + d.username : (d.email || d.address); var fr = d.fraud || { flags: [], riskLevel: 'low' }; var rc = { high: '#ff8a80', med: 'var(--amber)', low: '#7fe7bd' }[fr.riskLevel] || '#7fe7bd';
      var kv = function (k, v) { return '<div class="kv"><div class="k">' + k + '</div><div class="v">' + v + '</div></div>'; };
      var wl = function (w, warn) { return '<div class="row" style="cursor:pointer" data-acct="' + esc(w.address) + '"><span class="mono grow" style="font-size:11px;color:' + (warn ? '#ffb3ac' : 'var(--dim)') + '">' + esc(w.address) + '</span><span class="meta">' + N(w.claims) + ' claims</span>' + (w.banned ? '<span class="badge red">BANNED</span>' : '') + '<span class="mono" style="color:var(--lime)">' + USD(w.balanceUsd) + '</span></div>'; };
      b.innerHTML = (d.banned ? '<div class="flagbox" style="border-left-color:#ff6258;color:#ff9d95">This account is BANNED from the faucet - claims are blocked, balance kept for review.</div>' : '')
        + '<div class="kvgrid">' + kv('user', d.username ? userLink(d.username) : (d.email ? userLink('', d.email) : '—')) + kv('email', esc(d.email || '—')) + kv('telegram', d.tgLinked ? 'linked' : '—') + kv('balance', USD(d.balanceUsd)) + kv('earned', USD(d.earnedUsd)) + kv('claims', N(d.claims)) + kv('device lock', d.locked ? 'locked' : 'free') + kv('from', flag(d.cc) + ' ' + esc(d.dev || '?')) + kv('IP', esc(d.ip || '?') + (d.sameIp > 1 ? ' · ' + d.sameIp + ' wallets' : '')) + kv('created', d.created ? ago(d.created) + ' ago' : '—') + kv('last claim', d.lastClaim ? ago(d.lastClaim) + ' ago' : 'never') + kv('payout wallet', d.payoutAddr ? '<span class="mono" style="font-size:10.5px">' + esc(d.payoutAddr) + '</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--faint)">none linked</span>') + '</div>'
        + '<div class="sec-h">earnings - where the money came from</div><div id="amEarn"><div class="empty">loading…</div></div>'
        + '<div class="sec-h">fraud signals · risk <span style="color:' + rc + '">' + esc(String(fr.riskLevel || 'low').toUpperCase()) + '</span></div>' + ((fr.flags || []).length ? fr.flags.map(function (f) { return '<div class="flagbox" style="border-left-color:' + rc + '">' + esc(f) + '</div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="line up">no risk signals - looks clean</div>')
        + ((fr.didWallets || []).length ? '<div class="sec-h" style="color:#ff9d95">same device - other accounts (' + fr.didWallets.length + ')</div>' + fr.didWallets.map(function (w) { return wl(w, true); }).join('') : '') + ((fr.ipWallets || []).length ? '<div class="sec-h">other accounts on this IP (' + fr.ipWallets.length + ')</div>' + fr.ipWallets.map(function (w) { return wl(w); }).join('') : '')
        + (d.lb ? '<div class="sec-h">best trade this season</div><div class="line">' + ((+d.lb.roe) >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (+d.lb.roe).toFixed(0) + '% on ' + esc(d.lb.symbol || '?') + ' ' + esc(d.lb.side || '') + '</div>' : '')
        + ((d.withdrawals || []).length ? '<div class="sec-h">withdrawals</div>' + d.withdrawals.map(function (w) { var tx = String(w.txid || '').replace(/[^0-9a-fA-Fx]/g, ''); return '<div class="row"><span class="mono">' + USD(w.amountUsd) + '</span><span class="badge ' + (w.status === 'paid' ? 'ok' : w.status === 'pending' ? 'amber' : 'red') + '">' + esc(w.status) + '</span>' + (tx ? '<a href="https://bscscan.com/tx/' + tx + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="margin-left:auto">tx</a>' : '<span class="meta" style="margin-left:auto">' + ago(w.ts) + ' ago</span>') + '</div>'; }).join('') : '')
        + '<div class="sec-h">adjust balance</div><div class="bar"><input class="in" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" id="amAmt" placeholder="USD amount" style="flex:0 1 140px"><button class="btn sm" id="amAdd" style="color:#7fe7bd">Add</button><button class="btn sm red" id="amSub">Subtract</button><span class="line" style="font-size:11.5px">tournament and claim corrections · clamps at $0 · logged</span></div>'
        + '<div class="sec-h">private note</div><textarea class="in" id="amNote" rows="2" placeholder="only you see this">' + esc(d.note || '') + '</textarea><div class="bar" style="margin-top:6px"><button class="btn sm" id="amNoteSave">Save note</button></div>'
        + '<div class="sec-h">message to the user <span style="color:var(--faint);font-weight:500;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0">shown on /rewards · ' + (d.msg ? (d.msgSeen ? 'read by the user' : 'not read yet') : 'none set') + '</span></div><textarea class="in" id="amMsg" rows="2" placeholder="e.g. Your withdrawal was paid, check your wallet">' + esc(d.msg || '') + '</textarea><div class="bar" style="margin-top:6px"><button class="btn sm" id="amMsgSend">Send</button><span class="line" style="font-size:11.5px">empty + Send clears it</span></div>'
        + '<div class="sec-h">actions</div><div class="bar"><button class="btn sm ' + (d.banned ? '' : 'red') + '" id="amBan">' + (d.banned ? 'Unban' : 'Ban from faucet') + '</button><button class="btn sm ' + (d.lbBanned ? '' : 'red') + '" id="amLb">' + (d.lbBanned ? 'Reinstate to leaderboard' : 'Remove from leaderboard') + '</button><button class="btn sm" id="amUnlock">Unlock device</button><button class="btn sm red" id="amRemove">Remove account</button><button type="button" class="btn sm lime" data-money="' + esc(a) + '" data-money-t="' + esc(d.username ? '@' + d.username : (d.email || a)) + '">Money history</button><span class="stat" id="amSt"></span></div>';
      b.querySelectorAll('[data-acct]').forEach(function (r) { r.onclick = function () { openAcct(r.getAttribute('data-acct')); }; });
      J('/api/reward/earnings?address=' + encodeURIComponent(a)).then(function (e) { var box = $('amEarn'); if (!box) return; if (!e || !e.exists) { box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">no earnings on record</div>'; return; } var TL = { exsign: 'Exchange sign-up', promo: 'Promo post', lbprize: 'Season prize', welcome: 'Welcome bonus', mission: 'Daily mission', claim: 'Faucet claim', moon: 'Moon sign-up' }; var sm = e.summary || {}; var chips = Object.keys(sm).map(function (k) { return '<span class="badge lime" style="margin:0 6px 6px 0">' + esc(TL[k] || k) + ' · ' + N(sm[k].n) + ' · ' + USD(sm[k].usd) + '</span>'; }).join(''); var items = (e.items || []).slice(0, 30); box.innerHTML = (chips || '<div class="empty">nothing yet</div>') + (items.length ? '<div class="feed" style="max-height:200px">' + items.map(function (it) { return '<div class="row"><span class="meta" style="width:60px">' + ago(it.ts) + ' ago</span><span class="grow">' + esc(TL[it.type] || it.type) + (it.title || it.label ? ' · ' + esc(it.title || it.label) : '') + '</span><span class="mono up">+' + USD(it.usd != null ? it.usd : it.amountUsd) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : ''); }).catch(function () { var box = $('amEarn'); if (box) box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">earnings unavailable</div>'; });
      function done(msg, ok) { setStat('amSt', msg, ok); openAcct(a); paint(); }
      $('amAdd').onclick = function () { var v = parseFloat($('amAmt').value); if (!(v > 0)) { setStat('amSt', 'enter a USD amount', false); return; } post('/api/reward/adjust', { address: a, deltaUsd: v }).then(function (j) { done(j && j.ok ? 'added ' + USD(v) : 'failed: ' + ((j && j.error) || '?'), !!(j && j.ok)); }); };
      $('amSub').onclick = function () { var v = parseFloat($('amAmt').value); if (!(v > 0)) { setStat('amSt', 'enter a USD amount', false); return; } if (!confirm('Subtract ' + USD(v) + ' from this balance?')) return; post('/api/reward/adjust', { address: a, deltaUsd: -v }).then(function (j) { done(j && j.ok ? 'subtracted ' + USD(v) : 'failed: ' + ((j && j.error) || '?'), !!(j && j.ok)); }); };
      $('amNoteSave').onclick = function () { post('/api/reward/note', { address: a, note: $('amNote').value }).then(function () { setStat('amSt', 'note saved', true); }); };
      $('amMsgSend').onclick = function () { var mtxt = $('amMsg').value; post('/api/reward/message', { address: a, message: mtxt }).then(function () { setStat('amSt', mtxt.trim() ? 'message set - the user sees it on /rewards' : 'message cleared', true); }); };
      $('amBan').onclick = function () { if (!d.banned && !confirm('Ban this account from the faucet? Future claims are blocked, balance is kept for review.')) return; post('/api/reward/' + (d.banned ? 'unban' : 'ban'), { address: a }).then(function () { done(d.banned ? 'ban lifted' : 'banned', true); }); };
      $('amLb').onclick = function () { if (!d.lbBanned && !confirm('Remove from the season leaderboard? Their entries are wiped for this season.')) return; post('/api/reward/lbban', { address: a, unban: d.lbBanned ? 1 : 0 }).then(function () { done(d.lbBanned ? 'reinstated' : 'removed from the board', true); }); };
      $('amUnlock').onclick = function () { post('/api/reward/unlock', { address: a }).then(function () { done('device unlocked', true); }); };
      $('amRemove').onclick = function () { if (!confirm('Remove ' + (d.username || a) + '? Deletes the faucet account, its device lock and leaderboard entry.')) return; post('/api/reward/remove', { address: a }).then(function () { closeModal(); toast('account removed'); paint(); }); };
    }
    paint(); every(20000, paint);
  } };

  // ---------- SETTINGS / REWARD CONFIG ----------
  // rwd:cfg via /api/reward/config (full body on save, like the old tab, so nothing is reset by omission), plus the smaller stores that lived on
  // the old Settings tab: /api/announce (site banner), /api/ai/admin (Ask-AI daily limit), /api/calendar/admin (custom events),
  // /api/admin/premium (grants), /api/admin/opscfg (brief/alerts/big-trade ping), /api/admin/xppromos (XP promos).
  // Season pass codes (2026-09-06): generate, list, revoke. A code unlocks the pro track for one season; multi-use
  // codes are for drops (chat, Telegram, events) and each account can redeem a given code once.
  VIEWS['settings/codes'] = { render: function (el) {
    function paint() {
      J('/api/admin/passcodes').then(function (d) {
        var codes = d.codes || [], now = Date.now();
        // what a code gives (2026-09-06): the pro pass, cents on the rewards balance, Premium days or Ticks
        var KINDS = { pass: 'Season pass (pro track)', cents: 'Rewards balance (cents)', premium: 'Premium (days)', ticks: 'Ticks' };
        var gives = function (r) { return r.kind === 'cents' ? '<b class="gold">' + USD((r.amt || 0) / 100) + '</b> balance' : r.kind === 'premium' ? '<b>' + N(r.amt) + ' d</b> Premium' : r.kind === 'ticks' ? '<b>' + N(r.amt) + '</b> Ticks' : 'pro pass'; };
        var form = '<div class="bar" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:flex-end">'
          + '<label class="fld">Gives<select class="in" id="pcK">' + Object.keys(KINDS).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '">' + KINDS[k] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>'
          + '<label class="fld" id="pcAw" hidden>Amount<input class="in" id="pcA" type="number" min="1" value="10" style="width:90px"><small id="pcAh">cents, max 500</small></label>'
          + '<label class="fld">Codes<input class="in" id="pcN" type="number" min="1" max="50" value="5" style="width:70px"></label>'
          + '<label class="fld">Uses each<input class="in" id="pcU" type="number" min="1" max="1000" value="1" style="width:80px"></label>'
          + '<label class="fld">Expires in days<input class="in" id="pcD" type="number" min="0" max="365" value="30" style="width:90px"><small>0 = never</small></label>'
          + '<label class="fld" style="flex:1;min-width:160px">Note<input class="in" id="pcT" type="text" maxlength="60" placeholder="e.g. Telegram drop 6 Sep"></label>'
          + '<button class="btn" id="pcGen">Generate</button><span class="meta" id="pcOut" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;word-break:break-all"></span></div>';
        var rows = codes.map(function (c) { var dead = !!+c.revoked || (c.exp && c.exp < now) || (+c.used >= +c.uses); return { code: c.code, kind: c.kind || 'pass', amt: +c.amt || 0, left: (+c.uses - +c.used), uses: +c.uses, used: +c.used, exp: c.exp, created: c.created, note: c.note || '', batch: c.batch || '', state: +c.revoked ? 'revoked' : (c.exp && c.exp < now) ? 'expired' : (+c.used >= +c.uses) ? 'used up' : 'live', dead: dead }; });
        // One line per GENERATION, not per code (owner 2026-09-06): a batch opens to its codes and copies as a block. Codes made before
        // the batch column are grouped by what they share (kind, amount, uses, expiry, note, creation minute).
        var groups = {}, order = [];
        rows.forEach(function (r) { var k = r.batch || ['legacy', r.kind, r.amt, r.uses, r.exp || 0, r.note, Math.floor((r.created || 0) / 60000)].join('|'); if (!groups[k]) { groups[k] = { key: k, kind: r.kind, amt: r.amt, uses: r.uses, exp: r.exp, note: r.note, created: r.created, batch: r.batch, codes: [] }; order.push(k); } groups[k].codes.push(r); });
        var copyOf = function (g) { return g.codes.filter(function (r) { return !r.dead; }).map(function (r) { return r.code; }); };
        var openSet = {}; try { openSet = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ops_codes_open') || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
        var list = order.length ? order.map(function (k) { var g = groups[k], live = g.codes.filter(function (r) { return r.state === 'live'; }).length, used = g.codes.reduce(function (a, r) { return a + r.used; }, 0), usesAll = g.codes.reduce(function (a, r) { return a + r.uses; }, 0), copyable = copyOf(g).length;
          var head = '<b>' + g.codes.length + ' code' + (g.codes.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</b> <span class="mono">' + gives(g) + '</span> <span>' + (g.uses === 1 ? '1 use each' : N(g.uses) + ' uses each') + '</span> <span class="' + (live ? 'up' : 'dim') + '">' + live + ' live</span> <span>' + N(used) + ' / ' + N(usesAll) + ' redeemed</span> <span>' + (g.exp ? 'until ' + dt(g.exp).slice(0, 10) : 'no expiry') + '</span> <span title="' + esc(dtFull(g.created)) + '">' + ago(g.created) + '</span>' + (g.note ? ' <span class="dim">' + esc(g.note) + '</span>' : '');
          var body = '<div class="bar" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin:6px 0 8px">' + (copyable ? '<button class="btn sm" data-copyall="' + esc(k) + '">Copy all ' + copyable + ' live</button>' : '') + (live && g.batch ? '<button class="btn sm" data-revokeall="' + esc(g.batch) + '" data-n="' + live + '">Revoke all ' + live + '</button>' : '') + '</div>'
            + tbl([{ h: 'code', mono: true, f: function (r) { return '<b>' + esc(r.code) + '</b>' + (r.dead ? '' : ' <button class="btn sm" data-copy="' + esc(r.code) + '">copy</button>'); } }, { h: 'left', r: true, f: function (r) { return r.left + ' / ' + r.uses; } }, { h: 'state', f: function (r) { return '<span class="' + (r.state === 'live' ? 'up' : 'dim') + '">' + r.state + '</span>'; } }, { h: '', f: function (r) { return r.state === 'live' ? '<button class="btn sm" data-revoke="' + esc(r.code) + '">Revoke</button>' : ''; } }], g.codes)
            + '<textarea class="in" data-codes="' + esc(k) + '" readonly rows="1" style="display:none">' + esc(copyOf(g).join('\n')) + '</textarea>';
          return '<details class="cl' + (live ? ' hot' : '') + '" data-batch="' + esc(k) + '"' + (openSet[k] ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>' + head + '</summary><div class="cl-b">' + body + '</div></details>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">no codes yet</div>';
