        var src = (d.bySrc || []).map(function (x) { return esc(x.src || '?') + ' ' + N(x.n); }).join(' · ');
        el.innerHTML = card('Generate', form, 'a code gives the pro pass, cents on the rewards balance, Premium days or Ticks · redeemed in the code box on /season/ or /rewards/ (any kind, one box) · every generation, revoke and redemption is posted to Telegram')
          + card('Codes', list, 'one line per generation · open it for the codes · Copy all copies every still-live code in the batch, one per line · a member can redeem a given code once')
          + card('Pro passes', '<div class="line">' + N(d.holders || 0) + ' pro pass' + (d.holders === 1 ? '' : 'es') + ' this season' + (src ? ' · all time by source: ' + src : '') + '</div>', 'from upass');
        var ks = $('pcK'), aw = $('pcAw'), ah = $('pcAh'), ai = $('pcA'); if (ks) ks.onchange = function () { var k = ks.value; aw.hidden = k === 'pass'; ah.textContent = k === 'cents' ? 'cents, max 500 ($5)' : k === 'premium' ? 'days, max 365' : 'Ticks, max 5000'; ai.max = k === 'cents' ? 500 : k === 'premium' ? 365 : 5000; ai.value = k === 'cents' ? 10 : k === 'premium' ? 30 : 100; };
        var copyText = function (t, okMsg) { var done = function () { toast(okMsg || 'copied'); }, fail = function () { toast('copy failed - select the codes and copy by hand'); }; try { if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(done, fail); else fail(); } catch (e) { fail(); } };
        var gb = $('pcGen'); if (gb) gb.onclick = function () { var k = ks ? ks.value : 'pass', a = +$('pcA').value || 0; if (k !== 'pass' && !(a > 0)) { toast('amount first'); return; } if (!confirm('Generate ' + (+$('pcN').value || 1) + ' code(s), each giving ' + (k === 'pass' ?         el.querySelectorAll('[data-copy]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function (e) { e.preventDefault(); copyText(b.getAttribute('data-copy')); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-copyall]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function (e) { e.preventDefault(); var ta = el.querySelector('textarea[data-codes="' + b.getAttribute('data-copyall').replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]'); var t = ta ? ta.value : ''; if (!t) { toast('nothing live to copy'); return; } copyText(t, 'copied ' + t.split('\n').length + ' codes'); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('[data-revoke]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var c = b.getAttribute('data-revoke'); if (!confirm('Revoke ' + c + '?         el.querySelectorAll('[data-revokeall]').forEach(function (b) { b.onclick = function () { var bt = b.getAttribute('data-revokeall'), n = b.getAttribute('data-n'); if (!confirm('Revoke all ' + n + ' live code(s) in this batch? They stop working immediately.')) return; b.disabled = true; post('/api/admin/passcodes', { op: 'revokebatch', batch: bt }).then(function (r) { toast('revoked ' + (r.revoked || 0)); paint(); }); }; });
        el.querySelectorAll('details.cl[data-batch]').forEach(function (dt) { dt.addEventListener('toggle', function () { try { var o = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ops_codes_open') || '{}'); if (dt.open) o[dt.getAttribute('data-batch')] = 1; else delete o[dt.getAttribute('data-batch')]; localStorage.setItem('ops_codes_open', JSON.stringify(o)); } catch (e) {} }); });
        stamp();
      }).catch(function () { el.innerHTML = failHtml('could not load'); });
    }
    paint();
  } };

    function xnow() { return Date.now() + xoff; }
    function fmtDur(ms) { if (ms <= 0) return 'ended'; var h = Math.floor(ms / 3600000), m = Math.floor(ms % 3600000 / 60000); return (h > 0 ? h + 'h ' : '') + m + 'm'; }
    function pstatus(p) { var now = xnow(); if (!p.enabled) return '<span class="badge">OFF</span>'; if (!p.endMs || p.endMs <= now) return '<span class="badge amber">not running</span>'; if (now >= p.startMs) return '<span class="badge ok">LIVE · ' + fmtDur(p.endMs - now) + ' left</span>'; return '<span class="badge lime">starts in ' + fmtDur(p.startMs - now) + '</span>'; }
    function promos() { var box = $('xpList'); if (!box) return; box.innerHTML = PR.length ?     { t: 'Copy pending payment list', s: 'wallet, amount, user, UID check', run: function () { J('/api/admin/withdrawals').then(function (d) { var p = (d.wds || []).filter(function (w) { return w.status === 'pending'; }); if (!p.length) { toast('no pending payouts'); return; } var txt = p.map(function (w) { return w.wallet + '\t' + (+w.usd || 0).toFixed(2) + '\t@' + (w.who || '') + '\t' + (w.uidCheck || ''); }).join('\n'); try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt); toast('copied ' + p.length + ' lines'); } catch (e) { prompt('copy:', txt); } }); } },
    { t: 'Post to the trader chat', s: 'as MarginPad', href: '#inbox/chat' },
    { t: 'Keyboard shortcuts', s: '?', run: function () { $('help').hidden = false; } }
  ];
  function palStatic(q) { var out = [];
    var acts = function () { ACTIONS.forEach(function (a) { if (!q || a.t.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0) out.push({ k: 'action', t: a.t, s: a.s, href: a.href || '', run: a.run }); }); };
    if (!q) { (store('recent') || []).slice(1, 5).forEach(function (k) { out.push({ k: 'recent', t: titleOf(k), s: '', href: '#' + k }); }); acts(); } // empty box = where you were + what you can do; typing = views first
    NAV.forEach(function (s) { s.views.forEach(function (v) { var t = s.title + ' / ' + v.title; if (!q || t.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0 || s.id.indexOf(q) >= 0 || v.id.indexOf(q) >= 0) out.push({ k: 'go', t: t, s: '', href: '#' + s.id + '/' + v.id }); }); });
    if (q) acts();
    [['Pocket view', '/api/stats/pocket'], ['Site', '/'], ['Bug inbox', '/api/bug'], ['Private inbox', '/api/admin/pmail']].forEach(function (x) { if (!q || x[0].toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0) out.push({ k: 'open', t: x[0], s: x[1], href: x[1], blank: true }); }); return out; }
  function palRender(q) { q = (q || '').trim().toLowerCase(); palItems = palStatic(q).slice(0, q ? 8 : 14);
    if (q.indexOf('@') > 0 && q.length >= 5) { J('/api/reward/support/convs').then(function (d) { if ((palIn.value || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== q) return; var hits = ((d && d.conversations) || []).filter(function (c) { return String(c.email || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0; }).slice(0, 4); if (!hits.length) return; palItems = palItems.concat(hits.map(function (c) { return { k: 'ticket', t: c.email, s: (c.closed ? 'closed · ' : 'open · ') + c.messages.length + ' msg · ' + ago(c.lastTs) + ' ago', href: '#inbox/support/' + encodeURIComponent(c.email) }; })); palDraw(); }).catch(function () {}); }
    if (q.length >= 2) { clearTimeout(palT); palT = setTimeout(function () { J('/api/admin/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q)).then(function (d) { if ((palIn.value || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== q) return; var more = [];
      (d.users || []).slice(0, 8).forEach(function (u) { more.push({ k: 'user', t: '@' + (u.username || u.email || ''), s: (u.email || '') + (u.cc ? ' · ' + u.cc : ''), href: '/api/admin/user?u=' + encodeURIComponent(u.username || u.email || ''), blank: true }); });
      (d.accounts || []).slice(0, 5).forEach(function (a) { more.push({ k: 'faucet', t: a.username || a.address || '', s: 'balance ' + USD(a.balanceUsd || a.balance || 0), href: '#money/faucet' }); });
      (d.posts || []).slice(0, 5).forEach(function (p) { more.push({ k: 'post', t: p.title || '', s: p.author || '', href: p.url || '#growth/community', blank: !!p.url }); });
      palItems = palItems.concat(more); palIdx = 0; palDraw(); }).catch(function () {}); }, 200); }
    palIdx = 0; palDraw(); }
  function palDraw() { palRes.innerHTML = palItems.length ? palItems.map(function (it, i) { return '<div class="pr ' + (i === palIdx ? 'on' : '') + '" data-i="' + i + '"><span class="k">' + esc(it.k) + '</span><span class="t">' + esc(it.t) + '</span><span class="s">' + esc(it.s || '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="pr"><span class="t" style="color:var(--faint)">nothing matches</span></div>';
    palRes.querySelectorAll('.pr[data-i]').forEach(function (r) { r.onclick = function () { palGo(+r.getAttribute('data-i')); }; }); }
  function palGo(i) { var it = palItems[i]; if (!it) return; palClose(); if (it.run) it.run(); else if (it.blank) window.open(it.href, '_blank'); else location.hash = it.href.replace(/^#/, ''); }
  palIn.addEventListener('input', function () { palRender(palIn.value); });
  palIn.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { palIdx = Math.min(palItems.length - 1, palIdx + 1); palDraw(); e.preventDefault(); } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { palIdx = Math.max(0, palIdx - 1); palDraw(); e.preventDefault(); } else if (e.key === 'Enter') { palGo(palIdx); } else if (e.key === 'Escape') palClose(); });
  pal.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === pal) palClose(); });
  $('palBtn').addEventListener('click', palOpen);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K')) { e.preventDefault(); if (pal.hidden) palOpen(); else palClose(); } else if (e.key === 'Escape' && !pal.hidden) palClose(); });

  // ---------- boot ----------
  show(); loadTop(); loadMkt(); setInterval(function () { if (!document.hidden) loadTop(); }, 45000); setInterval(function () { if (!document.hidden) loadMkt(); }, 60000);
  J('/api/reward/lb').then(function (d) { if (d && d.weekEnd) { seasonEnd = +d.weekEnd; seasonStart = +d.weekStart; tickClock(); } }).catch(function () {});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (!document.hidden) { loadTop(); } });
  window.__ops2 = { NAV: NAV, VIEWS: VIEWS, route: route, show: show, seen: function (ts) { visitPrev = ts; visitStart = Date.now(); show(); } }; // seen() = E2E hook: pretend the last visit was at ts
})();
